### [1] [Smells and Refactorings for Software Environmental Sustainability: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03838)
*Ornela Danushi,Flavio Sampirisi,Jacopo Soldani,Stefano Forti,Antonio Brogi*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 系统综述归纳20类'绿色坏味道'及其对应重构，可作为代码性能/能耗优化与自动重构的规则知识库参考。

TL;DR: 系统文献综述梳理绿色异味与重构，识别20种影响软件环境可持续性的异味并映射缓解重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICT环境影响持续增长，需应对高能耗软件；将结构性低效视为绿色异味，通过重构减轻生态影响。

Method: 系统文献综述，分析并分类截至2026年3月发表的66项原始研究。

Result: 识别20种影响软件环境可持续性的异味，每种关联一种或多种可减轻负面环境影响的重构技术。

Conclusion: 绿色异味研究受到关注；所编目异味与重构可为实践者提供实用价值，并为可持续软件工程研究提供起点。

中文摘要：背景：信息通信技术（ICT）的环境影响持续增长。提升其环境可持续性需要处理高能耗软件系统，可能通过减少其足迹来实现。目标：为了更好地界定该问题及其持续性，软件可以被视为无论其功能可能多么先进，都携带着结构性低效。将这些低效识别为“绿色异味”为重构代码以减轻其生态影响提供了基础。我们旨在识别影响环境可持续软件的众所周知绿色异味，并将其映射到旨在减少其中影响的相应重构。方法：在本文中，我们对讨论绿色异味及其可能重构的文章进行了系统文献综述。我们分析并分类了截至2026年3月发表的66项原始研究。结果：我们识别出20种影响软件环境可持续性的异味。每种异味都与一种或多种已知可减轻其对软件环境可持续性负面影响的重构技术相关联。结论：文献见证了解决影响软件系统的绿色异味的兴趣。所编目的绿色异味及相应重构可为实践者在其日常开发工作流中提供实用价值，同时也为研究人员在可持续软件工程中确立新研究方向提供起点。

Original abstract: Context: The environmental impact of ICT is constantly growing. Enhancing its environmental sustainability requires dealing with energy-hungry software systems, by possibly reducing their footprint. Objective: To better frame the problem and its persistent nature, software can be viewed as carrying structural inefficiencies regardless of how advanced its functionality may be. Identifying these inefficiencies as "green smells" provides a basis for refactoring code to mitigate its ecological impact. We aim at identifying well-known green smells impacting environmental sustainable software and map them to corresponding refactoring aimed at reducing the impact therein. Method: In this article, we conduct a systematic literature review on the articles that discuss green smells and their possible refactorings. We analyse and classify 66 primary studies published up to March 2026. Results: We identify 20 smells impacting environmental sustainability of software. Each smell is associated with one or more refactoring techniques known to mitigate their negative effects on the environmental sustainability of software. Conclusions: The literature witnesses the interest in resolving the green smells affecting software systems. The catalogued green smells and the corresponding refactorings can provide pragmatic value for practitioners in their daily development workflows, while also offering researchers a starting point to establish new research directions in sustainable software engineering.

</details>


### [2] [How Do Coding Agents Optimize Software and Report Performance Validation? A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Open-Source Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03969)
*Huiyun Peng,Ricardo Calvo,Kelechi G. Kalu,James C. Davis*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 46 pages, 11 figures. Extends our MSR 2026 Mining Challenge paper. Huiyun Peng and Ricardo Calvo contributed equally. Replication package: https://github.com/PurdueDualityLab/EMSE-perf-pr-study

Selection reason: 大规模对比agent与人类提交的性能优化PR，直接揭示agent在优化策略与benchmark验证证据上的缺陷，与SemOpt高度契合。

TL;DR: 该研究比较 1,130 个 AI 代理与 1,130 个人工编写的性能 PR，发现代理 PR 合并率较低、基准测试使用较少，二者优化策略趋同，但验证证据普遍不完整，尤其缺少定量性能指标和权衡分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主 AI 编码代理已向开源项目提交性能优化 PR，但不清楚这些变更是否符合成熟优化实践并充分证明其宣称价值，因此需要系统比较代理与人类 PR 的采纳结果、优化策略和验证行为。

Method: 扩展 MSR 2026 Mining Challenge 对 407 个性能 PR 的试点研究，分析截至 2026 年 6 月收集的 1,130 个代理编写和 1,130 个人工编写的性能 PR，比较两组采纳结果与补丁特征，并考察优化实践和验证行为。

Result: 代理 PR 合并率低于人类 PR（54.4% 对 73.3%），但优化策略相似、验证报告比例相当；代理更依赖静态推理，基准测试使用较少（46.1% 对 57.2%），期末趋同；代理对代码异味重构与资源变更报告验证的频率相同，人类则更少；两组中约半数验证 PR 无定量性能指标，权衡很少量化。

Conclusion: 代理优化实践日益接近人类实践，但支持性证据仍不完整。应建立评估基础设施和审查标准，衡量预期效果与相关成本，而不是仅以存在验证作为充分证据。

中文摘要：软件性能优化需要识别瓶颈和改进机会，并实证评估跨性能目标的效果和权衡。自主 AI 编码代理向开源项目提交面向性能的拉取请求（PR），但这些变更是否反映了已确立的优化实践并充分证实其声称的价值，仍不清楚。作为对我们 MSR 2026 Mining Challenge 针对 407 个性能 PR 的试点研究的扩展，我们分析了截至 2026 年 6 月收集的 1,130 个代理编写和 1,130 个人工编写的性能 PR 样本。我们比较两组之间的采纳结果和补丁特征，并考察其优化实践和验证行为。结果表明，代理 PR 的合并频率低于人工编写的 PR（54.4% 对 73.3%），但两组使用相似的优化策略，并以相当的比例报告验证。代理 PR 更多依赖静态推理，更少依赖基准测试（46.1% 对 57.2%），尽管基准测试的使用在研究期末趋于一致。代理 PR 对代码异味重构报告验证的频率与对面向资源的变更一样高，而人工编写的 PR 则较少如此。在两组中，约一半经过验证的 PR 没有报告任何定量性能指标，相关权衡也很少被量化。代理优化实践已越来越接近人类实践，但支持性证据仍不完整。确定代理提出的优化是否得到严格、定量和多维证据的支持，仍是一项重要挑战。这些发现促使我们建立评估基础设施和审查标准，以衡量预期效果和相关成本，而不是仅将验证的存在视为充分。

Original abstract: Software performance optimization requires identifying bottlenecks and improvement opportunities, and empirically evaluating the effects and trade-offs across performance objectives. Autonomous AI coding agents submit performance-oriented pull requests (PRs) to open-source projects, yet it remains unclear whether these changes reflect established optimization practices and adequately substantiate their claimed value. Extending our MSR 2026 Mining Challenge pilot study of 407 performance PRs, we analyze a sample of 1,130 agentic and 1,130 human-authored performance PRs collected through June 2026. We compare adoption outcomes and patch characteristics between the two groups, and examine their optimization practices and validation behaviors. The results show that agentic PRs are merged less often than human-authored PRs (54.4% vs. 73.3%), but the two groups use similar optimization strategies and report validation at comparable rates. Agentic PRs rely more on static reasoning and less on benchmarks (46.1% vs. 57.2%), although benchmark use converges by the end of the study period. Agentic PRs also report validation for code-smell refactorings as often as for resource-targeting changes, whereas human-authored PRs do so less often. Across both groups, about half of validated PRs report no quantitative performance metric, and relevant trade-offs are rarely quantified. Agentic optimization practice has become increasingly similar to human practice, but the supporting evidence remains incomplete. Establishing whether agent-proposed optimizations are supported by rigorous, quantitative, and multidimensional evidence remains an important challenge. These findings motivate evaluation infrastructure and review criteria that measure intended effects and relevant costs rather than treating the presence of validation alone as sufficient.

</details>


### [3] [World Requirement Model: Learning Requirement-Change Consequences from Typed Artifact Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04445)
*Yuanpeng He,Lijian Li,Dongming Jin,Huanyao Zhang,Fangjing Li,Linyu Li,Chung-ju Huang,Tianxiang Zhan,Qingsong Wen,Wenpin Jiao*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 以类型化制品图世界模型预测需求变更后果，可迁移到代码agent的变更影响分析与仓库图推理。

TL;DR: 论文提出WRM，将需求变更的工程上下文建模为带类型工件图，通过关系感知注意力、类型化传播和多任务读出预测影响、冲突、违规与缺陷风险。在合成需求世界基准上，WRM在影响MAP等指标上优于哈希文本MLP，并给出候选覆盖边界及评估条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求变更会牵涉利益相关者、约束、组件和测试，需要预测变更在共享工件上的后果；现有方法缺乏对工程上下文、关系结构及多类后果的统一建模与评估条件。

Method: 构建带类型工件图，以共享工件标识符为预测位置；使用关系感知注意力与类型化传播编码节点，学习世界与决策表示；共享读出器输出影响、冲突、违规、缺陷风险分数，并辅以后继邻接与潜在预测目标；在合成案例和扩展语料上与哈希文本MLP等比较。

Result: 在六领域282个合成案例的28个评分案例中，WRM影响MAP为0.724，对比MLP的0.623，相对提升16.2%，配对差异0.101，条件95%区间[0.044,0.159]；最低四分位平均AP提升32.4%，等域MAP提升13.3%；扩展语料四次47案例比较MAP增益15.6–29.1%，五个报告指标均值均更高。结果受限于评分案例选检查点及主干不匹配。

Conclusion: WRM提供了以工件为地址的世界模型表述、上下文后果评分的比较证据及需求-世界预测的评估条件；分析给出候选覆盖边界，并指出当前线性影响头无法跨决策重排固定世界的工件。

中文摘要：需求变更可能影响相互关联的利益相关者、约束、组件和测试。我们提出世界需求模型（World Requirement Model，WRM），它将这一工程上下文编码为带类型的工件图，并在共享工件标识符处预测后果。关系感知注意力和类型化传播对节点进行上下文化；世界表示和决策表示支持学习到的动力学。共享读出器对影响、冲突、违规和缺陷风险进行评分；辅助目标监督后继邻接和潜在预测。在来自六个领域的282个合成案例中的28个评分案例上，WRM获得的影响平均精度均值（MAP）为0.724，而哈希文本多层感知机（MLP）为0.623，相对增益为16.2%，配对差异为0.101（条件95%区间[0.044,0.159]）。最低四分位平均AP提升32.4%，等域MAP提升13.3%。在扩展语料上的四次47案例比较显示MAP增益为15.6–29.1%，并且在所有五个报告的指标上均值更高。因此，所记录的优势扩展到评分汇总和标注/训练设置。检查点是在评分案例上选择的，且主干网络不匹配，因此这些结果刻画的是所选系统。我们的分析建立了候选覆盖边界，并表明当前的线性影响头无法跨决策对固定世界的工件进行重排序。WRM贡献了一种以工件为地址的世界模型表述、面向上下文后果评分的比较证据，以及评估需求-世界预测的明确条件。

Original abstract: Requirement changes can affect connected stakeholders, constraints, components, and tests. We present World Requirement Model (WRM), which encodes this engineering context as a typed artifact graph and predicts consequences at shared artifact identifiers. Relation-aware attention and typed propagation contextualize nodes; world and decision representations support learned dynamics. Shared readouts score impact, conflict, violation, and defect risk; auxiliary objectives supervise successor adjacency and latent prediction. On 28 scored cases from 282 synthetic cases in six domains, WRM obtains impact mean average precision (MAP) of 0.724 versus 0.623 for a hashed-text multilayer perceptron (MLP), a 16.2\% relative gain and paired difference of 0.101 (conditional 95\% interval [0.044,0.159]). Lowest-quarter mean AP improves by 32.4\%, and equal-domain MAP by 13.3\%. Four 47-case comparisons on an expanded corpus show MAP gains of 15.6--29.1\% and higher means on all five reported metrics. The recorded advantage thus extends across score summaries and annotation/training settings. Checkpoints were selected on scored cases, and backbones are unmatched, so these results characterize selected systems. Our analysis establishes candidate-coverage bounds and shows that the current linear impact head cannot rerank a fixed world's artifacts across decisions. WRM contributes an artifact-addressed world-model formulation, comparative evidence for contextual consequence scoring, and explicit conditions for evaluating requirement-world prediction.

</details>


### [4] [DreamTest: World-Model Surrogates for Search-Based Testing of Deep Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04494)
*Qinghua Xu,Guancheng Wang,Boxi Yu,Liting Lin,Lionel Briand*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 用世界模型代理替代昂贵执行做搜索式测试，可迁移到降低agent测试/执行预算的代理验证思路。

TL;DR: DreamTest：一种世界模型代理，通过想象推演预测DRL智能体测试中的失败，提升失败预测与测试生成效率，发现更多样化的故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部署前测试DRL智能体以发现故障，但每次执行成本高；现有代理方法将系统视为黑箱直接预测结果，忽略了测试过程信息。

Method: 提出DreamTest，基于循环状态空间模型的世界模型代理，从智能体训练日志学习行为与环境动态；给定配置，通过想象推演产生失败分数指导搜索，避免执行所有候选。

Result: 在Parking、Humanoid、DonkeyCar上，AUPRC分别超过最强基线97%、12%、39%，分布外测试集增益达145%、29%、44%；相同预算下，最佳DreamTest+搜索组合平均多发现29%、22%、79%的新故障；在k=2-40的聚类中，覆盖最多行为聚类。

Conclusion: DreamTest能有效预测失败、生成多样化故障，是一种高效的DRL智能体测试代理。

中文摘要：在信息物理系统中测试深度强化学习（DRL）智能体的目标是在部署前发现各类故障，但每次执行都可能代价高昂。代理辅助测试通过学习预测哪些测试配置可能失败来降低这一成本。先前的代理方法将系统视为黑箱，直接预测通过或失败的结果；我们则对测试如何展开进行建模，并根据想象的情节估计失败。
我们提出了DreamTest，一种用于测试DRL智能体的世界模型代理。DreamTest采用循环状态空间模型，从智能体的训练日志中学习智能体行为和环境动态。给定候选配置，想象推演产生一个失败分数，指导搜索，而无需在模拟器或真实系统中执行每个候选。
我们在Parking、Humanoid和DonkeyCar上评估了DreamTest在失败预测、测试生成和故障多样性方面的表现。平均精度召回曲线下面积（AUPRC）分别超过最强基线97%、12%和39%，在五个分布外测试集上的增益分别达到145%、29%和44%。在相同的模拟器验证预算下，最佳的“DreamTest + 搜索”组合平均发现的新故障分别多出29%、22%和79%。在k = 2-40的聚类中，对于几乎所有k，使用DreamTest生成的故障覆盖了最多的行为聚类，表明DreamTest能够持续发现行为多样的故障。

Original abstract: Testing deep reinforcement learning (DRL) agents in cyber-physical systems aims to uncover diverse failures before deployment, but each execution can be expensive. Surrogate-assisted testing reduces this cost by learning to predict which test configurations are likely to fail. Prior surrogates treat the system as a black box and predict pass or fail outcomes directly; we instead model how a test unfolds and estimate failure from an imagined episode.
  We introduce DreamTest, a world-model surrogate for testing DRL agents. DreamTest adapts a recurrent state-space model to learn agent behaviour and environment dynamics from the agent's training log. Given a candidate configuration, imagined rollouts produce a failure score that guides search without executing every candidate in a simulator or real system.
  We evaluate DreamTest for failure prediction, test generation, and failure diversity on Parking, Humanoid, and DonkeyCar. Mean area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) exceeds the strongest baseline by 97%, 12%, and 39%, respectively, and gains on five out-of-distribution test sets reach 145%, 29%, and 44%. Under the same simulator-validation budget, the best "DreamTest + search" combinations find 29%, 22%, and 79% more novel failures on average. Across clusterings with k = 2-40, failures generated with DreamTest cover the most behavioural clusters for almost all k, indicating that DreamTest consistently discovers behaviourally diverse failures.

</details>


### [5] [Practical Runtime Enforcement of First-Order Temporal Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04611)
*François Hublet,David Basin,Srđan Krstić*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 将时序要求编译为高效运行时执行器，并用于LLM agent安全策略，属于可迁移的运行时验证/约束强制机制。

TL;DR: 提出 EnfFlash：面向 MFOTL 可执行片段的高效时序需求运行时执行器，将需求编译为命令式程序，显著降低执行延迟，并支持 GDPR 等复杂合规策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有支持复杂时序需求的执行机制过慢，生产环境常退化为非时序访问控制与临时插桩，难以随需求复杂度扩展；现实系统可能需同时满足数百条含未来义务的合规要求。

Method: 识别 MFOTL 的一个低运行时复杂度可执行片段，支持义务等实用需求；设计执行算法；实现 EnfFlash，将需求编译为命令式程序并高效解释；支持独立运行与作为 Web 应用执行后端。

Result: 在标准基准上，EnfFlash 延迟较 EnfGuard 最多低 44 倍；在 LLM 代理安全策略上最多低三个数量级；可在社交网络上执行 400 行 GDPR MFOTL 公式，每次页面浏览增加不到 15 ms。

Conclusion: EnfFlash 能高效执行复杂时序合规需求，适合交互式和实时应用，并可集成到 Web 应用中。

中文摘要：运行时执行器观察系统行为并对其施加控制，以确保系统始终遵守其需求。许多自然需求不仅对系统当前行为制定限制，还对其未来行为施加义务；例如，代理式安全可能要求在一次运行期间收集的个人数据在某个截止时间前被擦除。在不断增长的合规环境中，现实世界系统可能受到数百条此类需求的约束。然而，支持复杂策略的现有执行机制对于生产软件来说往往过慢；作为替代，开发者可能诉诸非时序的访问控制机制和临时性的插桩，但这些机制随着需求复杂性的增长而扩展性很差。为了解决这个问题，我们引入一种高效算法和工具，用于执行复杂时序需求，并展示其性能。具体来说，我们识别出度量一阶时序逻辑（MFOTL）的一个片段，该片段可以在低运行时复杂度下被执行，同时支持一大类实际相关的需求，包括义务。然后，我们为该片段设计了一种执行算法，并将其实现在 EnfFlash 中，这是一种新颖工具，在标准基准上执行需求的延迟比先前最先进的执行器 EnfGuard 最多低 44 倍，在针对 LLM 代理的安全策略上最多低三个数量级。这一性能是通过将需求编译为命令式程序并高效解释来实现的。我们在现有基准上独立评估 EnfFlash，并将其作为执行后端集成到 Web 应用中。我们证明，它可以在社交网络上执行一个规定 GDPR 的、长达 400 行的 MFOTL 公式，同时每次页面浏览仅增加不到 15 毫秒，足以满足大多数交互式和实时应用。

Original abstract: Runtime enforcers observe a system's behavior and exert control over it to ensure that the system always adheres to its requirements. Many natural requirements not only formulate restrictions on the system's present behavior, but also impose obligations on its future behavior; for instance, agentic security may require personal data collected during a run to be erased by some deadline. In an ever-growing compliance landscape, real-world systems may be subject to hundreds of such requirements. However, existing enforcement mechanisms supporting complex policies are often too slow for production software; instead, developers may resort to non-temporal access control mechanisms and ad-hoc instrumentation, but these scale poorly with requirement complexity.
  To address this problem, we introduce an efficient algorithm and tool for enforcing complex temporal requirements and demonstrate its performance. Specifically, we identify a fragment of Metric First-Order Temporal Logic (MFOTL) that can be enforced with low runtime complexity while supporting a rich family of practically relevant requirements, including obligations. We then design an enforcement algorithm for this fragment and implement it in EnfFlash, a novel tool that enforces requirements with latency up to 44x lower than EnfGuard, the previous state-of-the-art enforcer, on standard benchmarks, and up to three orders of magnitude lower on security policies for LLM agents. This performance is achieved by compiling requirements into imperative programs that we efficiently interpret. We evaluate EnfFlash both standalone, on existing benchmarks, and integrated in web applications as an enforcement backend. We demonstrate that it can enforce a substantial 400-line MFOTL formula specifying the GDPR on a social network while adding under 15 ms to each page view, which is sufficient for most interactive and real-time applications.

</details>


### [6] [When Talk Isn't Code: Comparing LLM Agents That Simulate and Develop Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04639)
*Xiang Li,Aichun Huang,Mingming Zhang,Zheli Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 21 pages, 6 figures

Selection reason: 同一攻击套件对比LLM的模拟模式与编码模式，揭示知识探针不能预测生成代码安全性，对agent评测与验证设计有迁移价值。

TL;DR: 该论文首次在同一攻击套件与判定器下比较 LLM 的模拟模式、开发模式与知识探针，发现 S-mode 可作初筛但不能替代 D-mode 测试，且安全规范需显式写出，即使模型能复述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作分别评估 LLM 模拟协议实现与编写协议实现，且知识探针或对话可能认可生成代码并未实现的安全检查；缺少将三者与同一模型生成代码进行对比的研究。

Method: 设计并实现统一测试框架，用同一攻击套件和判定器评判 S-mode、D-mode 与知识探针；在四种协议实现上评估 15 个 LLM，并分析安全规范增删的影响。

Result: 完整规范下，重组、HTTP、防火墙中位攻击成功率≤4%；DNS 上 S-mode 13%、D-mode 15%。加入缺失 DNS 要求使 D-mode 攻击从 100% 降至 34% 且仅影响该检查；删除已陈述规则会波及其他检查。编写 DNS 解析器的 14 个模型中 13 个能说出该检查，但 42 个程序均缺少。S-mode 标记 82% 真实 DNS 漏洞，96% 安全案例未标记，但存在双向误报/漏报。

Conclusion: S-mode 可作为初筛，但不能替代 D-mode 测试；安全要求应显式书写，即使模型能背诵。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLMs）既被用于模拟网络协议实现（如同蜜罐所做的那样），也被用于编写这些实现。先前工作分别评估这两种用途：一种基于模型的回答或对话，另一种基于其生成的代码。我们观察到，知识探针（询问模型一个实现需要哪些安全检查）和对话可能会认可生成程序所缺少的安全检查，但尚无研究将它们与同一模型编写的代码进行比较。为填补这一空白，我们提出首个此类比较，在模拟模式（S-mode）与开发模式（D-mode）之间进行，其中 S-mode 指模型在对话中扮演该实现，D-mode 指模型编写程序并让该程序受到攻击，并以知识探针作为基线。我们设计并实现了一个测试框架，用同一攻击套件和同一判定器评判这三者，并在四种协议实现上评估了 15 个 LLM。我们发现，在完整安全规范下，对于重组、HTTP 和防火墙实现，中位模型在两种模式中的攻击成功率至多为 4%，但在 DNS 上，S-mode 为 13%，D-mode 为 15%。在 D-mode 中，将缺失的 DNS 要求加入规范可将该攻击从 100% 降至 34%，并且只改变该项检查；而删除一条已陈述规则也会在其他检查上削弱程序。事先询问模型并不能预测程序具有哪些检查，因为在编写 DNS 解析器的 14 个模型中，有 13 个会说出该检查，但全部 42 个程序都缺少它。S-mode 是有用的初筛器，能标记出 82% 的真实 DNS 漏洞，并让 96% 的安全案例未被标记。它会双向出错：既过度报告 D-mode 未实现的安全检查，也漏报 D-mode 已实现的检查。S-mode 可以筛查，但不能取代 D-mode 测试；即使模型能背诵安全要求，也应将其写下来。

Original abstract: LLMs are used both to simulate network protocol implementations, as honeypots do, and to write them. Prior work evaluates the two uses separately, from a model's answers or conversations in one case and from its generated code in the other. We observe that a knowledge probe (asking the model which security checks an implementation needs) and a conversation can credit security checks that the generated program lacks, but no study has compared them with the code the same model writes. To fill this gap, we present the first such comparison between simulation mode (S-mode), where the model plays the implementation in a conversation, and development mode (D-mode), where the model writes the program and the program is attacked, with a knowledge probe as a baseline. We design and implement a harness that judges all three with one attack suite and one oracle, and evaluate 15 LLMs on four protocol implementations. We find that with the full security specification, the median model's attack success is at most 4\% on reassembly, HTTP, and firewall implementations in both modes, but 13\% in S-mode and 15\% in D-mode on DNS. In D-mode, adding the missing DNS requirement to the specification cuts that attack from 100\% to 34\% and changes only that check, whereas deleting a stated rule weakens the program on other checks too. Asking the model beforehand does not predict which checks a program has, since 13 of the 14 models that write a DNS resolver name the check, yet all 42 programs lack it. S-mode is a useful first filter, flagging 82\% of the real DNS vulnerabilities and leaving 96\% of the safe cases unflagged. It errs in both directions, over-reporting security checks that D-mode does not implement and under-reporting checks that D-mode does. S-mode can screen but not replace D-mode testing, and security requirements should be written down even when a model can recite them.

</details>


### [7] [Back to the Future: Regressing Readability Features from LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04641)
*Tairan Wang,Earl T. Barr*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 39 pages, including appendices

Selection reason: 用可解释特征回归代码可读性，可在agent生成代码的可读性评估与上下文压缩场景中复用。

TL;DR: BTTF 用线性回归加信息论特征（传统代码度量、嵌入语义组织、因果 LM 可预测性）评估代码可读性，在 6 个数据集共 1,100 个样本上平均 Spearman 较最强基线提升最多 0.101，在 13 种可读性降低变换上 Java/Python 响应率达 93.8%/87.6%，优于 Dorn，接近云端 LLM，且具备特征级可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可读性模型在不同数据集上与人类判断不一致；AI 编码智能体普及后，可读代码既便于人类评审智能体输出，也便于智能体在有限上下文窗口内维护代码；直接使用 LLM 评判往往不透明且消耗大量提示 token，因此需要更准确、鲁棒且可解释的可读性模型。

Method: 提出 BTTF（Back To The Future），将线性回归与信息论特征结合，把语言模型用作测量工具而非黑盒评判者。特征融合传统代码度量、基于嵌入的语义组织与因果 LM 可预测性，以刻画源代码结构、语义连贯性和上下文惊奇度。在五个公开人工评分可读性基准和人工标注的 MBJP 开发集上评估，共 1,100 个样本；并在生产代码的 13 种受控可读性降低变换上测试，与 Dorn、本地模型、云端 LLM 和直接 LLM 基线比较。

Result: BTTF 平均 Spearman 相关系数较最强基线最多提升 0.101；在 13 种变换中 Java 响应率 93.8%、Python 87.6%，分别比 Dorn 高 14% 和 19.2%；在本地模型中最佳，仅落后最强云端 LLM 响应 Java 1.7%、Python 5.1%；直接 LLM 基线每次评估平均需 1,083 个提示 token 且不透明；所有评估模型配置中，每个特征系数均与其理论方向效应一致。

Conclusion: BTTF 同时实现当前最佳基准性能、较强变换鲁棒性和特征粒度可解释性，适合作为 AI 编码智能体工作流中的可读性评估方法。

中文摘要：代码可读性支持软件理解、评审与维护。随着 AI 编码智能体得到更广泛使用，可读代码既有助于人类评审智能体生成的输出，也有助于智能体在有限上下文窗口内维护代码。然而，现有可读性模型在不同数据集上与人类判断并不一致。我们提出 BTTF（Back To The Future），将线性回归与信息论特征配对。BTTF 不把语言模型当作黑盒评判者，而是将其用作测量工具。它结合传统代码度量、基于嵌入的语义组织以及因果语言模型可预测性，以捕捉源代码结构、语义连贯性和上下文惊奇度。我们在五个公开的、经人工评分的可读性基准以及我们手动标注的 MBJP 开发集上评估 BTTF，总计 1,100 个样本。BTTF 将平均 Spearman 相关系数较最强基线提升最多 0.101。在生产代码上的 13 种受控可读性降低变换中，它在 Java 中达到 93.8% 的响应率，在 Python 中达到 87.6%，分别比 Dorn 高出 14% 和 19.2%。它在本地模型中表现最佳，在 Java 中落后最强云端 LLM 响应 1.7%，在 Python 中落后 5.1%。直接 LLM 基线每次评估平均需要 1,083 个提示 token，同时仍然不透明。BTTF 将最先进的基准性能与强大的变换鲁棒性同特征粒度的可解释性结合起来：在所有评估的模型配置中，每个特征系数都与其理论方向效应一致。

Original abstract: Code readability supports software understanding, review, and maintenance. As AI coding agents become more widely used, readable code helps both humans review agent-generated output and agents maintain code within limited context windows. Yet existing readability models do not consistently agree with human judgements across datasets.
  We introduce BTTF (Back To The Future), pairing linear regression with information-theoretic features. Rather than using language models as black-box judges, BTTF uses them as measurement instruments. It combines traditional code measurements, embedding-based semantic organisation, and causal-LM predictability to capture source structure, semantic coherence, and contextual surprise.
  We evaluate BTTF on five publicly available human-rated readability benchmarks and our manually annotated MBJP development set, totalling 1,100 samples. BTTF improves average Spearman correlation over the strongest baseline by up to 0.101. Across 13 controlled readability-reducing transformations on production code, it achieves response rates of 93.8% in Java and 87.6% in Python, outperforming Dorn by 14% and 19.2%. It performs best among local models and trails the strongest cloud-LLM response by 1.7% in Java and 5.1% in Python. The direct-LLM baselines require an average of 1,083 prompt tokens per evaluation while remaining opaque. BTTF combines state-of-the-art benchmark performance and strong transformation robustness with feature-granular interpretability: every feature coefficient matches its theoretical directional effect across all evaluated model configurations.

</details>


### [8] [RETRACE: From Entangled Repair Histories to Reusable Experience for CI Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04658)
*Rabeya Khatun Muna,Muhammad Ahasanuzzaman,Nakhla Rafi,Yisen Xu,Jinqiu Yang,Tse-Hsun Chen*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 从纠缠的提交历史中重建问题级修复经验并检索复用，直接提升CI修复agent的Pass@1，属经验记忆/复用机制。

TL;DR: RETRACE 从包含多个 CI 问题、失败尝试、回滚编辑和无关更改的 PR 历史中重建问题级修复经验，并在 CI-REPAIR-BENCH 上显著提升多种 LLM 智能体的修复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数经验复用方法假设问题与解决方案已对齐，但软件历史中 PR 常混杂多个 CI 问题、失败尝试、回滚编辑和无关更改，难以确定哪些更改解决了哪个问题，因此需要从历史中重建问题级修复经验。

Method: RETRACE 结合端点视图（从通过修订中保留的更改向后推理）和开发视图（沿提交历史向前追踪修复演化），利用 CI 执行证据调和二者，并在从具体修复到可迁移修复模式的三个抽象层级上表示恢复的经验；面对新故障时检索相关问题级经验指导修复。

Result: 在包含 101 个仓库、12 个故障类别、565 个 PR 级修复的 CI-REPAIR-BENCH 上，mini-SWE-agent 的 Pass@1 在 MiniMax-M2.5 下由 19.6% 提升至 31.9%，在 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 下由 23.3% 提升至 32.8%；匹配子集上 Codex 由 15.5% 提升至 27.5%；两种视图结合始终优于单独使用任一视图。

Conclusion: 恢复问题-更改对齐能让历史 CI 修复转化为可复用的修复经验，从而提升 LLM 智能体对新故障的修复能力。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）智能体日益复用先前的经验，但大多数方法假设问题与解决方案已经对齐。软件历史很少提供这种对齐：一个拉取请求（PR）可能包含多个持续集成（CI）问题、失败的尝试、被回滚的编辑以及无关更改，从而模糊了哪些更改解决了每个问题。我们提出 RETRACE，一个从这类历史中重建问题级修复经验的框架。RETRACE 结合了一个端点视图，该视图从通过修订中保留的更改向后推理，以及一个开发视图，该视图通过提交历史向前追踪修复演化。CI 执行证据调和这两种视图，并且恢复的经验以三个抽象层级表示，从具体修复到可迁移的修复模式。对于新故障，RETRACE 检索相关的问题级经验来指导修复。在 CI-REPAIR-BENCH 上，该基准包含来自 101 个代码仓库、涵盖 12 个故障类别的 565 个 PR 级修复，RETRACE 将 mini-SWE-agent 的 Pass@1 在使用 MiniMax-M2.5 时从 19.6% 提升到 31.9%，在使用 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 时从 23.3% 提升到 32.8%。在一个匹配子集上，Codex 从 15.5% 提升到 27.5%。结合两种视图始终优于单独使用任一视图，表明恢复问题-更改对齐使历史 CI 修复能够作为可复用的修复经验。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly reuse prior experience, but most approaches assume that problems and solutions are already aligned. Software histories rarely provide this alignment: a pull request (PR) may contain multiple continuous integration (CI) problems, failed attempts, reverted edits, and unrelated changes, obscuring which changes resolve each problem. We present RETRACE, a framework for reconstructing problem-level repair experience from such histories. RETRACE combines an endpoint view that reasons backward from changes retained in the passing revision with a development view that traces repair evolution forward through commit history. CI execution evidence reconciles the two views, and the recovered experience is represented at three abstraction levels, from concrete fixes to transferable repair patterns. For new failures, RETRACE retrieves relevant problem-level experience to guide repair. On CI-REPAIR-BENCH, comprising 565 PR-level repairs from 101 repositories across 12 failure categories, RETRACE improves mini-SWE-agent Pass@1 from 19.6% to 31.9% with MiniMax-M2.5 and from 23.3% to 32.8% with DeepSeek-V4-Flash. On a matched subset, Codex improves from 15.5% to 27.5%. Combining both views consistently outperforms either alone, showing that recovering problem-change alignment enables historical CI repairs to serve as reusable repair experience.

</details>


### [9] [Towards Automatically Pruning Logging Code with Coding Agents: How Far Are We?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04716)
*He Yang Yuan,Haonan Zhang,Xin Wang,An Ran Chen,Kisub Kim,Zishuo Ding,Zhenhao Li*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 构建LogRem数据集并系统评测coding agent删除冗余日志代码的能力，属于agent代码维护任务与评测。

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析真实项目中的日志移除实践，构建 LogRem 数据集，并评估编码智能体；智能体输出大多有效，但仅少数能精确移除与真实变更相同的日志代码，表明日志移除自动化的关键挑战是准确确定移除范围并保留必要代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日志代码对调试、监控和维护很重要，但过度日志会引入噪声、运行时开销并掩盖诊断信息。已有研究多关注日志生成与修改，日志移除研究不足，因此需要理解开发者如何移除日志，并探索编码智能体能否自动完成这一任务。

Method: 从 Python 和 Java 仓库提取并人工验证日志移除案例，归纳 10 种移除模式和 11 种移除原因；构建 LogRem 数据集，包含 387 个真实案例，覆盖直接移除日志语句、移除日志基础设施和日志替换；评估四个编码智能体在多种模型设置下的输出，并与真实已接受变更比较，同时分析执行成本、提交信息、开发者讨论和分类指导的影响。

Result: 智能体输出有效性通过率为 95.6% 到 100.0%，但只有 11.1% 到 19.6% 能移除与真实变更相同的日志代码并保留无关代码。智能体差异表现为漏移除、多余移除和无关代码编辑，且在不同日志移除类别和轨迹上差异显著。执行成本差异很大，但更高成本并不总能带来更接近的对齐。提交信息和开发者讨论带来最大的对齐提升，分类体系指导持续降低运行时间。

Conclusion: 该研究将日志移除确立为一项独立的软件维护任务，并表明可靠自动化取决于准确确定移除范围，同时保留必要代码。这也是首个从该视角考察日志代码移除的研究。

中文摘要：日志代码支持调试、监控和软件维护，但过多的日志会增加噪声、带来运行时开销并掩盖诊断信息。尽管先前研究已广泛研究日志代码生成和修改，日志移除相对而言仍未得到充分探索。本文研究开发者的日志移除实践，并探索使用编码智能体完成该任务。我们从 Python 和 Java 仓库中提取并人工验证日志移除案例，并归纳出 10 种移除模式和 11 种移除原因，用以刻画在真实软件变更中日志代码是如何以及为何被移除。我们进一步构建了 LogRem，一个包含 387 个真实案例的数据集，涵盖直接移除日志语句、移除日志基础设施以及替换日志。我们评估了四个编码智能体在多种模型设置下的表现，并将其输出与已接受的真实变更进行比较。尽管 95.6% 到 100.0% 的输出通过了有效性检查，但只有 11.1% 到 19.6% 的输出移除了与相应真实变更相同的日志代码，同时保留了无关代码。智能体之间的差异体现在漏移除、多余移除和无关代码编辑上，并且在不同的日志移除类别和轨迹上存在显著差异。执行成本差异很大，但更高的成本并不总能带来更接近的对齐。提交信息和开发者讨论带来最大的对齐提升，而分类体系指导则持续减少运行时间。总体而言，我们的研究将日志移除确立为一项独特的软件维护任务，并表明可靠的自动化取决于准确确定移除范围，同时保留必要代码。据我们所知，这是首个从这一视角考察日志代码移除的研究。

Original abstract: Logging code supports debugging, monitoring, and software maintenance, but excessive logging can add noise, impose runtime overhead, and obscure diagnostic information. While prior research has extensively studied logging code generation and modification, logging removal remains comparatively underexplored. In this paper, we study developer logging removal practices and explore the use of coding agents for this task. We extract and manually validate logging removal cases from Python and Java repositories and derive 10 removal patterns and 11 removal reasons that characterize how and why logging code was removed in real-world software changes. We further construct LogRem, a dataset of 387 real-world cases covering direct logging statement removal, logging infrastructure removal, and logging replacement. We evaluate four coding agents with multiple model settings and compare their outputs with accepted real-world changes. Although 95.6% to 100.0% of outputs pass validity checks, only 11.1% to 19.6% remove the same logging code as the corresponding real-world change while preserving unrelated code. Agents differ through missed removals, extra removals, and unrelated code edits, with substantial variation across logging removal categories and trajectories. Execution cost varies widely, but higher cost does not consistently yield closer alignment. Commit messages and developer discussions provide the largest alignment gains, while taxonomy guidance consistently reduces runtime. Overall, our study establishes logging removal as a distinct software maintenance task and shows that reliable automation depends on accurately determining removal scope while preserving necessary code. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to examine logging code removal from this perspective.

</details>


### [10] [Asymmetric Repository Lineage Modeling and Verifier-Guided Coordination in Concurrent AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04779)
*Arjun Subramanian,George Xu,Nithilan Karthik*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 研究并发AI编码agent的冲突预测与验证器引导协调，提出可执行检查器与调度门控，可迁移到多agent协作可靠性。

TL;DR: 论文研究并发 AI 编码智能体的协调与验证分离，提出属性限定验证契约和 MERGEGYM 三轨基准，覆盖范围预测、重放冲突解决与调度；结果显示廉价 lineage 信号不足以单独替代可执行检查，而冲突预测、补丁重建和决策时门控在特定条件下有效，但精确重放廉价时应验证一切。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发 AI 编码智能体需要协调，廉价信号可用于排序工作，但只有可执行检查器能确立所声称性质；作者 PR diff 与三方合并基之间的度量差异会造成操作上重要的代理不匹配。

Method: 提出 property-scoped verification contract 与 MERGEGYM 三轨基准；在 715 对 lineage 分层集合上评估 lineage-union 规则、12 特征逻辑回归、随机森林；在 T1 评估零样本 LLM 与 LLM 加元数据融合；在 T3 使用决策时门控和冻结标签重放。

Result: 47.3% 冲突发生于文件集不相交时；独立 lineage-union 规则 AUROC 0.877，逻辑回归 0.882 [0.845, 0.917] 且 PR-AUC 0.661，随机森林 0.902 [0.875, 0.928] 且 PR-AUC 0.701；33.3% 重放预算下分别恢复 81.2% 和 82.9% 冲突；补丁重建成功 48/79 且全部干净，其余 31 例无定论；T1 零样本 LLM AUC 0.704，融合 0.740；T3 中位去重叠 91.7%、makespan 膨胀 65.0%；本地 git merge-tree 重放中位 0.02 秒。

Conclusion: lineage 分诊本身不足以节省该检查器；当精确重放廉价时应验证一切；本文经验保证仅限文本可合并性或明确陈述的冻结标签调度目标。

中文摘要：并发 AI 编码智能体带来一个协调问题：廉价信号可以对工作进行优先级排序，但只有可执行检查器才能确立所声称的性质。我们通过一个属性限定的验证契约和 MERGEGYM 来研究这种分离；MERGEGYM 是一个三轨基准，用于开放时范围预测、重放条件下的冲突解决和调度。在一个分层的 715 对 lineage 集合（167 个文本冲突）上，尽管编写的文件集合不相交，仍有 79 个冲突（47.3%）发生。这是三方合并预期会出现的一种在操作上重要的代理不匹配：编写的 PR diff 是相对于 PR 特定基线来度量的，而检查器则将两个 head 与它们的共同合并基进行比较。一个独立的 lineage 并集规则达到留出 AUROC 0.877；一个 12 特征逻辑回归模型达到 0.882 [0.845, 0.917]，PR-AUC 为 0.661；在同一决策时特征上未经调参的随机森林达到 0.902 [0.875, 0.928]，PR-AUC 为 0.701。在 33.3% 的留出重放预算下，逻辑回归和随机森林模型分别恢复 81.2% 和 82.9% 的冲突。补丁重建在 48/79 个零重叠冲突上成功，且所有 48 个都变为干净；其余 31 个案例尚无定论，因此该检查验证了预期的三方合并解释，而不是声称一种新的 Git 机制。在 T1 中，零样本 LLM 达到 AUC 0.704，LLM 加元数据融合达到 0.740。在 T3 中，在冻结标签重放下，一个决策时门控以 65.0% 的 makespan 膨胀去重叠了中位数 91.7% 的已标注范围碰撞。因为本地 git merge-tree 重放在我们的日志中已经廉价（中位数 0.02 秒），我们不声称仅靠 lineage 分诊就能节省该检查器：当精确重放廉价时，验证一切。本文中的所有经验性保证仍限于文本可合并性或明确陈述的冻结标签调度目标。

Original abstract: Concurrent AI coding agents create a coordination problem in which cheap signals may prioritize work, but only an executable checker can establish the property being claimed. We study this separation through a property-scoped verification contract and MERGEGYM, a three-track benchmark for open-time scope forecasting, replay- conditioned conflict resolution, and scheduling. On a stratified 715-pair lineage set (167 textual conflicts), 79 conflicts (47.3%) occur despite disjoint authored file sets. This is an operationally important proxy mismatch expected from three-way merge: authored PR diffs are measured against PR-specific bases, whereas the checker compares both heads to their common merge base. A standalone lineage-union rule reaches held-out AUROC 0.877; a 12-feature logistic model reaches 0.882 [0.845, 0.917] with PR-AUC 0.661, and an untuned random forest on the same decision-time features reaches 0.902 [0.875, 0.928] with PR-AUC 0.701. At a 33.3% held-out replay budget, the logistic and random-forest models recover 81.2% and 82.9% of conflicts, respectively. Patch reconstruction succeeds for 48/79 zero- overlap conflicts and all 48 become clean; the other 31 cases are inconclusive, so this check validates the expected three-way-merge explanation rather than claiming a new Git mechanism. In T1, a zero-shot LLM reaches AUC 0.704 and LLM-plus- metadata fusion 0.740. In T3, a decision-time gate de-overlaps a median 91.7% of labeled scope collisions at 65.0% makespan inflation under frozen-label replay. Because local git merge-tree replay is already cheap in our logs (median 0.02 s), we do not claim that lineage triage saves this checker alone: when exact replay is cheap, verify everything. All empirical guarantees in this paper remain limited to textual mergeability or the explicitly stated frozen-label scheduling target.

</details>


### [11] [Agent Skill Evolution: How Revisions Affect Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04832)
*Jiajie Wang,Yutong Zhao,Tianlin Li,Huashan Chen,Jinfu Chen,Kebin Peng,Sen He*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 实证量化Agent Skill修订对21个模型/4个agent合规性与正确性的影响及成本，直接指导技能/提示工程优化。

TL;DR: 研究分析 3,159 个 Agent Skills 的 2,608 个修订对，发现多数修订改变规则；添加规则提升单次回答合规性与智能体行动率，但真实按需加载 Skill 时增益减半，且修订增加输入 token 但未显著增加回合成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skills 像代码一样被修订，但其修订对 LLM 编码智能体的实际影响未知；需量化规则变更对模型回答、智能体行为、正确性与成本的影响。

Method: 从 3,159 个 Skill 提取 2,608 个首次/末次修订对，分析演化及其与 harness 配置的关系；聚焦可自动检查的规则增删，在单次回答中测试 21 个模型、沙箱中测试 4 个智能体，并在 20 个模型和 4 个智能体上测量成本；另比较真实按需加载 Skill 正文的设定与盲评结果。

Result: 55% 修订改变规则或流程；修订 Skill 的提交更常改动 CLAUDE.md 等 harness 文件。添加规则使 16 个开放权重模型单次回答合规性平均 +0.41；4 个智能体所需行动率平均 +0.23（+0.16 至 +0.36），3 个盲评智能体最终正确性平均 +0.10（+0.06 至 +0.14）。增益主要来自指定新命令或路径的规则。按需加载下，4 个智能体保留约 51% 行动增益，3 个开放模型约 38%。修订增加单次回答 18–19% 输入 token，对智能体回合未检测到成本；加载 Skill 正文使回合 token 平均增加 50%。

Conclusion: Skill 修订主要通过规则/流程变更显著提升合规与行动，但真实按需加载会削弱增益，且正文加载 token 成本高；因此 Skill 设计需权衡规则明确性与加载成本。

中文摘要：Agent Skills 是告诉 LLM 编码智能体项目如何运作的 SKILL.md 文件，它们像代码一样被修订，但一次修订对智能体有何影响尚不清楚。基于 3,159 个 Skill 的 2,608 个首次/末次修订对，我们刻画了 Skill 如何演化，以及它们如何与智能体 harness 的配置共同变化。随后我们聚焦于规则变更，即添加或删除一条可自动检查的规则的修订，例如“run allium check”。我们在单次回答中测量其对 21 个模型的影响，在沙箱中测量其对四个智能体的影响，并在其中 20 个模型和这四个智能体上测量其成本。大多数修订（55%）改变了一条规则或流程，且修订 Skill 的提交比同等规模的其他提交更常改动诸如 CLAUDE.md 之类的 harness 文件。在 16 个开放权重模型上，添加一条规则使单次回答中的合规性平均提高 +0.41。在四个智能体上，智能体采取所需行动的比例平均提高 +0.23（+0.16 至 +0.36），对于由盲审评判者评估的三个智能体，最终正确性平均提高 +0.10（+0.06 至 +0.14）。增益主要来自提及旧 Skill 未提及的命令或路径的规则。真实工具仅在智能体判断需要时才加载 Skill 正文。在该设定下，四个智能体平均保留约一半的行动增益（51%），三个开放模型约保留 38%。一次修订给单次回答增加 18–19% 输入 token，对智能体回合未检测到成本，而加载 Skill 正文会使一个回合的 token 平均增加 50%。

Original abstract: Agent Skills, the SKILL.md files that tell an LLM coding agent how a project works, are revised like code, yet what a revision does to the agent is unknown. From 2,608 first/last revision pairs of 3,159 Skills, we characterize how Skills evolve and how they change together with the configuration of the agent's harness. We then focus on rule changes, revisions that add or remove a rule we can check automatically, such as "run allium check". We measure their effect on 21 models in single answers and on four agents in a sandbox, and their cost on 20 of these models and the four agents. Most revisions (55%) change a rule or procedure, and commits that revise a Skill change harness files such as CLAUDE.md more often than other commits of the same size. Across 16 open-weight models, an added rule raises compliance in a single answer by +0.41 on average. Across the four agents, the rate at which the agent takes the required action rises by +0.23 on average (+0.16 to +0.36), and for the three agents that blind judges assessed, final correctness rises by +0.10 on average (+0.06 to +0.14). The gain comes mainly from rules that name a command or path the old Skill did not mention. Real tools load a Skill's body only when the agent decides it needs it. In that setting the four agents keep about half of the action gain on average (51%), and the three open models about 38%. A revision adds 18-19% input tokens to a single answer and no detectable cost to an agent episode, while loading a Skill's body raises the tokens of an episode by 50% on average.

</details>


### [12] [CARET: Training-Free Test-Time Scaling for Repository-Level Code Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04837)
*Jiajie Wang,Yutong Zhao,Kebin Peng,Sen He,Qing Guo,Tianlin Li*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 训练无关的检索与生成协同测试时扩展，反向上文似然自评选择候选，可迁移到代码生成的自验证与预算控制。

TL;DR: CARET：免训练联合改进RAG的检索与生成；用样本一致性路由检索上下文，用缓存前缀采样与反向上下文似然选择补全，在18种设置中平均提升EM 10.8点，计算量约1.45倍单次生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级代码补全的RAG工作只优化检索阶段，忽略生成阶段；作者认为检索与生成可联合改进，生成可通过测试时扩展提升。

Method: CARET免训练：检索端基于自身样本一致性在检索上下文间路由，样本分歧时级联到备选上下文；生成端在缓存提示前缀上采样候选，避免重复编码长上下文；用反向上下文似然让同一模型根据光标后代码概率对候选打分选择最终补全。

Result: 在CrossCodeEval、RepoEval-Line、RepoEval-API与六种代码模型（1.1B-7B，四家族）的18种组合中，精确匹配全部提升，平均超贪心解码10.8点、超self-consistency@10 5.3点；词元级计算约为单次生成1.45倍，实测墙钟时间1.3-2.9倍并随生成器规模增长。

Conclusion: 联合改进检索与生成比仅改进检索获得更准确代码，且开销接近单次前向。

中文摘要：检索增强生成（RAG）主导了仓库级代码补全：它检索跨文件上下文（R），然后解码一个贪心补全（G）。现有工作主要关注检索，并止步于此。我们认为这两个阶段可以一起改进，尤其是生成阶段可以从测试时扩展中获益。我们提出CARET，一种免训练方法。对于R，CARET利用其自身样本之间的一致性在检索上下文之间进行路由，当样本分散时级联到备选上下文。对于G，它在缓存的提示前缀上采样候选，因此长检索上下文只需编码一次，而不是每个样本编码一次。然后它通过反向上下文似然选择最终补全：正确的补全使光标之后的代码更可能出现，因此同一模型通过向前阅读来自行评分其候选。在CrossCodeEval、RepoEval-Line和RepoEval-API上，使用六种代码模型（1.1B到7B，四个家族），CARET在全部18种组合中提高了精确匹配，平均比贪心解码高10.8个百分点，比self-consistency@10高5.3。词元级计算量保持在一次生成的约1.45倍（实测墙钟时间为1.3-2.9倍，随生成器规模增长而增加）。一起改进检索和生成比单独改进检索产生更准确的代码，且预算仍接近单次前向。

Original abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) dominates repository-level code completion: it retrieves cross-file context (R), then decodes one greedy completion (G). Existing work mainly focuses on retrieval and stops there. We argue both stages can be improved together, with generation in particular gaining from test-time scaling. We present CARET, a training-free method. For R, CARET routes among retrieval contexts using the agreement among its own samples, cascading to an alternative context when the samples scatter. For G, it samples candidates over a cached prompt prefix, so the long retrieved context is encoded once rather than once per sample. It then selects the final completion by reverse-context likelihood: a correct completion makes the code after the cursor more probable, so the same model grades its own candidates by reading ahead. Across CrossCodeEval, RepoEval-Line, and RepoEval-API with six code models (1.1B to 7B, four families), CARET improves exact match in all 18 combinations by 10.8 points on average over greedy decoding and 5.3 over self-consistency@10. Token-level compute stays near 1.45 times one generation (measured wall-clock 1.3-2.9 times, growing with generator size). Improving retrieval and generation together yields more accurate code than improving retrieval alone, at a budget that stays close to a single pass.

</details>


### [13] [Complex Agents, Shallow Tests: Demystifying and Enhancing Test Adequacy of Agent Harness in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04921)
*Yifan Xiong,Jingyi Ge,Zhenpeng Chen,Yiling Lou*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 首个针对agent harness的测试充分性实证与契约感知测试生成工具，检出大量真实harness缺陷，属agent可靠性保障。

TL;DR: 首个实证研究揭示智能体harness测试不足，尤其LDH代码行/分支覆盖不足一半；提出HarnessTester，通过显式智能体-harness契约生成测试，显著提升覆盖率，并发现122个真实缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体harness日益复杂且易含实现缺陷，可靠性风险高；但harness测试是否充分尚不明确，尤其是处理LLM输出和控制智能体行为的关键LDH代码可能被忽视。

Method: 开展首个针对真实世界智能体系统中harness测试充分性的实证研究；提出HarnessTester，引入显式智能体-harness契约支持，构建契约忠实的测试设置，并广泛执行LDH代码。

Result: 分析发现harness严重测试不足，LDH代码现有测试覆盖不足一半行和分支。评估中，HarnessTester相比最先进通用测试生成技术取得75.95%/84.76%更大的行/分支覆盖增益和69.89%更大的变异得分增益；检测到122个真实harness缺陷，其中88个未知、69个已确认。

Conclusion: HarnessTester能有效提升测试充分性并保障真实世界智能体系统可靠性，凸显面向harness的测试生成技术的实用价值。

中文摘要：基于LLM的智能体系统正在成为一种新的软件范式。现代智能体通常由骨干LLM以及一个围绕其的harness组成，该harness作为智能体执行的运行时软件基础设施。随着智能体harness日益复杂，智能体会遭受多种harness实现缺陷，从而引发重大可靠性问题。在这项工作中，我们开展了首个实证研究，系统性地探究真实世界智能体系统中harness的测试充分性。我们的分析表明，智能体harness仍然严重缺乏测试。特别是，依赖LLM的harness（LDH）代码，尽管在处理LLM输出和控制智能体行为方面具有关键作用，却只获得有限的测试关注，现有测试覆盖其不到一半的行和分支。受这些发现启发，我们进一步提出HarnessTester，这是首个面向harness的测试生成技术，它引入显式的智能体-harness契约支持，以构建忠实于契约的测试设置，并广泛地执行LDH代码。我们的评估表明，HarnessTester在实现行/分支覆盖率增益方面分别比最先进的通用测试生成技术高75.95%/84.76%，在变异得分增益方面高69.89%。此外，HarnessTester在广泛使用的智能体系统（例如OpenClaw）中检测到122个真实世界harness缺陷，其中88个是先前未知的缺陷，69个已得到智能体开发者确认。这些结果凸显了HarnessTester在提高测试充分性和保障真实世界智能体系统可靠性方面的实际有效性。

Original abstract: LLM-based agentic systems are emerging as a new software paradigm. Modern agents are typically composed of backbone LLMs and a surrounding harness that serves as the operational software infrastructure for agent execution. As agent harnesses grow increasingly complex, agents suffer from diverse harness implementation bugs, raising substantial reliability concerns. In this work, we conduct the first empirical study to systematically investigate the test adequacy of harness in real-world agentic systems. Our analysis reveals that agent harness remains substantially undertested. In particular, LLM-dependent harness (LDH) code, despite its critical role in processing LLM outputs and governing agent behavior, receives limited testing attention, with less than half of its lines and branches covered by existing tests. Motivated by these findings, we further propose HarnessTester, the first harness-oriented test generation technique that incorporates explicit agent-harness contract support to construct contract-faithful test setups and extensively exercise LDH code. Our evaluation shows that HarnessTester substantially outperforms state-of-the-art general-purpose test generation techniques in achieving 75.95%/84.76% larger line/branch coverage gains and 69.89% larger mutation-score gains. Furthermore, HarnessTester detects 122 real-world harness bugs in widely-used agentic systems (e.g., OpenClaw), among which, 88 bugs are previously-unknown bugs and 69 bugs have been confirmed by agent developers. These results highlight the practical effectiveness of HarnessTester in improving test adequacy and assuring the reliability of real-world agentic systems.

</details>


### [14] [Assembling Insights for Agentic Machine Learning Engineering Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04927)
*Bihui Jin,Yinxi Li,Kaiyuan Wang,Pengyu Nie*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 构建MLE-InsightBench与洞察装配agent，系统研究洞察类型与注入方式对agentic MLE的影响，可迁移到agent记忆/知识注入机制。

TL;DR: 该文提出MLE-InsightBench与MLE-InsightForge，系统研究相关MLE任务洞见对智能体式MLE的影响，发现三类洞见经技能或行动手册注入可显著提升成绩并具互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体式MLE中洞见收集与注入零散，评估未控制洞见来源导致数据泄漏，需系统研究洞见类型与注入方式的影响。

Method: 构建涵盖12个领域、160个Kaggle竞赛的基准，每领域留一目标、148个源竞赛；开发洞见组装智能体从源竞赛、顶级人类方案和技术文章构建12条领域洞见、148条竞赛洞见、16条改进洞见；以按需技能或前置行动手册注入。

Result: 提取洞见使归一化私有测试分数较无洞见基线提升123.9%；三类洞见互补；更优注入模式因任务而异，技能总体更具成本效益；增益跨后端LLM泛化。

Conclusion: 系统化洞见注入可有效提升智能体式MLE，且需按任务选择注入方式；基准与防泄漏设计有助于可靠评估。

中文摘要：智能体式机器学习工程（Agentic MLE）是面向复杂ML任务的新兴AI4SE应用，也是迈向AI系统递归自我改进的一步。近期的智能体式MLE系统显示了利用相关MLE任务洞见的价值：一些系统以专家领域知识为条件进行代码生成，而这些知识隐式地从同类MLE任务中整理而来；一些系统具有求解一个MLE任务、收集记忆以惠及未来任务的循环。然而，洞见收集与注入仍是零散的，且现有评估往往不控制洞见来源，使数据泄漏成为对有效性的威胁。为弥合这些差距，我们系统研究来自相关MLE任务的洞见如何影响智能体式MLE系统，以及其影响如何取决于洞见类型和注入方法。我们提出MLE-InsightBench，一个涵盖12个领域的160个Kaggle竞赛基准。为防止泄漏，每个领域留出一个竞赛作为目标，其余148个仅当它们不可能受到目标任务影响时才作为来源，例如通过后续版本或被复用的解决方案。我们还开发了MLE-InsightForge，一个洞见组装智能体，它探索源竞赛、顶级人类解决方案和技术文章，以构建三类洞见：总结最佳实践的12条领域洞见、描述获胜解决方案的148条竞赛洞见，以及捕捉通用调试与优化策略的16条改进洞见。这些洞见以按需技能（skill）或前置行动手册（playbook）的形式注入。在我们的评估中，所提取洞见将归一化私有测试分数较无洞见基线提升123.9%。受控实验进一步表明，三类洞见互补；更好的注入模式（技能 vs. 行动手册）因任务而异，总体上技能更具成本效益；且这些增益可泛化到不同后端LLM。

Original abstract: Agentic machine learning engineering (MLE) is an emerging AI4SE application for complex ML tasks and a step toward recursive self-improvement of AI systems. Recent agentic MLE systems show the value of leveraging insights from related MLE tasks: some systems condition code generation on expert domain knowledge, which is implicitly curated from peer MLE tasks; some systems have a loop of solving an MLE task, gathering memory to benefit future tasks. However, insight collection and injection remain ad hoc, and existing evaluations often do not control insight sources, making data leakage a threat to validity.
  To close these gaps, we systematically study how insights from related MLE tasks affect agentic MLE systems, and how their impact depends on insight type and injection method. We introduce MLE-InsightBench, a benchmark of 160 Kaggle competitions across 12 domains. To prevent leakage, one competition per domain is held out as the target, while the remaining 148 serve as sources only when they cannot have been influenced by the target task, such as through later editions or reused solutions. We also develop MLE-InsightForge, an insight assembly agent that explores source competitions, top human solutions, and writeups to construct three insight types: 12 domain insights summarizing best practices, 148 competition insights describing winning solutions, and 16 improvement insights capturing generic debugging and optimization strategies. These insights are injected either as on-demand skills or as an upfront playbook. In our evaluation, the extracted insights improve the normalized private-test score by 123.9% over a no-insight baseline. Controlled experiments further show that the three insight types are complementary; the better injection mode (skill vs. playbook) varies by tasks, with skills more cost-effective overall; and the gains generalize across backend LLMs.

</details>


### [15] [TeleGen: Improving LLM-Based Web Application Generation via Runtime Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04981)
*Yujia Luo,Haonan Zhang,Jiasi Shen,Zishuo Ding,Weiyi Shang*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 19 pages, 3 figures. Accepted to Findings of EMNLP 2026

Selection reason: 用运行时遥测简报增强web应用生成的修复反馈，属执行反馈驱动的agent修复闭环，可迁移到工具反馈设计。

TL;DR: TeleGen 通过插桩生成应用、收集并压缩运行时遥测，为 LLM 生成的 Web 应用修复提供可观测性反馈；在 WebGen-Bench 上任务成功率提升 8.5 个百分点，在 Web-Bench 上累积 Pass@2 从 21.7% 提升至 29.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成-执行-修复流水线仅依赖任务结果或错误消息，缺失浏览器操作与最终任务结果之间的运行时行为，导致交互级故障难以诊断。

Method: 提出 TeleGen：对生成的应用进行插桩，在任务执行期间收集运行时遥测，并将原始遥测日志压缩为简洁简报用于修复；在 WebGen-Bench 和 Web-Bench 上评估。

Result: WebGen-Bench 上任务成功率从无遥测修复的 67.7% 提升至 76.2%，提高 8.5 个百分点；Web-Bench 上累积 Pass@2 从 21.7% 提升至 29.8%。消融表明运行时遥测提供有用诊断信号，简报使其更有效且成本更低；对隐藏执行路径故障尤其有帮助，如导航、表单工作流和前后端协调。

Conclusion: 运行时遥测与遥测简报能有效改善 LLM 生成 Web 应用的交互级修复，尤其有助于诊断涉及隐藏执行路径的故障。

中文摘要：大型语言模型能够根据自然语言需求生成可运行的 Web 应用，但许多生成的应用仍然无法完成交互式任务。现有的生成-执行-修复流水线会执行生成的应用，并使用任务结果或错误消息来指导代码修订。然而，这种反馈通常遗漏了浏览器操作与最终任务结果之间的运行时行为，使得交互级故障难以诊断。因此，我们提出了 TeleGen，这是一种面向基于 LLM 的 Web 应用生成的可观测性增强框架。TeleGen 对生成的应用进行插桩，在任务执行期间收集运行时遥测数据，并将原始遥测日志压缩为简洁的简报以供修复使用。我们在 WebGen-Bench 和 Web-Bench 上评估 TeleGen。在 WebGen-Bench 上，TeleGen 将任务成功率从无遥测修复的 67.7% 提升至 76.2%，提高了 8.5 个百分点。在 Web-Bench 上，它将累积 Pass@2 从 21.7% 提升至 29.8%。消融结果表明，运行时遥测提供了有用的诊断信号，而遥测简报使该信号更有效且使用成本更低。进一步分析表明，遥测对于涉及隐藏执行路径的故障尤其有帮助，例如导航、表单工作流以及前后端协调。

Original abstract: Large language models can generate runnable web applications from natural-language requirements, but many generated applications still fail interactive tasks. Existing generate-execute-repair pipelines execute the generated application and use task outcomes or error messages to guide code revision. However, this feedback often misses the runtime behavior between a browser action and the final task outcome, making interaction-level failures difficult to diagnose. Therefore, we propose TeleGen, an observability-enhanced framework for LLM-based web application generation. TeleGen instruments generated applications, collects runtime telemetry during task execution, and compresses raw telemetry logs into concise briefs for repair. We evaluate TeleGen on WebGen-Bench and Web-Bench. On WebGen-Bench, TeleGen improves task success from 67.7% with repair without telemetry to 76.2%, an increase of 8.5 percentage points. On Web-Bench, it improves cumulative Pass@2 from 21.7% to 29.8%. Ablation results show that runtime telemetry provides a useful diagnostic signal, while telemetry briefs make this signal more effective and less costly to use. Further analysis shows that telemetry is especially helpful for failures involving hidden execution paths, such as navigation, form workflows, and frontend-backend coordination.

</details>


### [16] [LLM-Based Test Generation: Information Sources, Generation Strategies, and Quality Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05001)
*Yunhao Liang,Chengguang Gan,Ruixuan Ying,Hanjun Wei,Zhe Cui,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 结构化综述LLM测试生成的信息来源、生成策略与质量证据，为测试agent的oracle独立性与评测设计提供框架。

TL;DR: 该综述连接LLM测试生成过程与测试质量证据，基于95条记录提出四维分类框架，区分可执行性、预言独立性与测试正确性，并给出基准与研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的测试制品目的和信息来源各异，覆盖率提升、参考程序一致与需求违规检测提供不同证据，需统一框架理解生成过程与质量证据。

Method: 结构化叙述性综述；截至2026年9月29日95条精选来源；沿四维度组织：测试目标与制品、训练与生成信息、生成与学习机制、评估证据；综合单元/需求测试、测试预言、API/GUI测试、模糊测试、学习型测试生成器。

Result: 框架解释执行反馈提升可执行性但影响预言独立性，规约来源与训练时参考监督影响比较，下游代码选择收益不等同于测试正确性；据此组织基准。

Conclusion: 提出研究议程：独立预言评估、预算感知生成、仓库规模评估、测试维护。

中文摘要：大型语言模型正越来越多地被用于生成测试场景、可执行测试套件、断言、交互序列和模糊测试基础设施。这些制品服务于不同目的，并依赖关于正确行为的不同信息来源。因此，一个提高实现覆盖率的测试、一个与参考程序一致的测试，以及一个检测到需求违规的测试，提供的是不同种类的证据。我们呈现一项结构化叙述性综述，将生成过程与用于证明测试质量的证据联系起来。基于截至2026年9月29日的95条精选来源记录，我们沿四个维度组织该领域：测试目标与制品、训练与生成期间可用的信息、生成与学习机制，以及评估证据。我们综合了关于单元测试与基于需求的测试、测试预言、API 与 GUI 测试、模糊测试以及学习型测试生成器的研究。由此得到的框架解释了执行反馈如何能够提高可执行性，同时又如何影响预言独立性；为什么规约来源和训练时参考监督对于比较很重要；以及下游代码选择收益如何不同于测试正确性。我们利用这些区分来组织基准，并为独立预言评估、预算感知生成、仓库规模评估和测试维护推导研究议程。

Original abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to generate test scenarios, executable test suites, assertions, interaction sequences, and fuzzing infrastructure. These artifacts serve different purposes and rely on different sources of information about correct behavior. A test that increases implementation coverage, a test that agrees with a reference program, and a test that detects a requirements violation therefore provide distinct kinds of evidence. We present a structured narrative survey that connects the generation process to the evidence used to justify test quality. Drawing on 95 curated source records through 29 September 2026, we organize the field along four dimensions: testing objectives and artifacts, information available during training and generation, generation and learning mechanisms, and evaluation evidence. We synthesize work on unit and requirements-based testing, test oracles, API and GUI testing, fuzzing, and learned test generators. The resulting framework explains how execution feedback can improve executability while also affecting the independence of an oracle, why specification provenance and training-time reference supervision matter for comparisons, and how downstream code-selection gains differ from test correctness. We use these distinctions to organize benchmarks and derive a research agenda for independent oracle assessment, budget-aware generation, repository-scale evaluation, and test maintenance.

</details>


### [17] [Rethinking Tool Design for Agentic RCA: A Controlled Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05009)
*Yu Luo,Rongchen Gao,Zhenhui Zhou,Changchang Liu,Yuliang You,Yongqian Sun,Shenglin Zhang,Qiuai Fu,Shijie Wang,Dan Pei*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 受控实证研究agentic RCA中工具抽象层级与组合对诊断的影响，揭示工具设计可迁移到通用agent工具编排。

TL;DR: 对微服务RCA中LLM智能体工具抽象与组合的受控实证研究：实现24个L1-L3结构化工具与L0参考，基于三系统375个故障案例、8种配置；工具配置对根因定位和故障类型识别影响不同，且收益随模型与目标系统变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体被越来越多地用于微服务RCA，但如何设计和组合其工具仍缺乏经验性指导。

Method: 实现24个结构化工具（L1指标访问、L2证据分析、L3诊断）及基于Python的L0参考设定；使用三个微服务系统的375个故障案例，在Qwen3.7-Plus上评估八种配置，并在四种配置共享子集上比较四个Qwen模型。

Result: Qwen3.7-Plus下L3的top-1定位准确率为82.8%，L0为85.4%，但L3每案例耗时不到一半；增加工具层级可提升故障类型识别却降低定位准确率；轨迹分析发现智能体查阅额外证据后会覆盖正确诊断建议；将L3加入L1+L2改善三个模型诊断，但损害很少调用L3的Qwen3-8B；工具配置排名随微服务系统变化。

Conclusion: 研究为RCA工具的设计与使用提供实证基础，指导依据模型、诊断目标和目标系统进行工具选择与组合。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）智能体在微服务系统根因分析（RCA）中的探索日益增多，但关于如何设计并组合其工具的经验性指导仍然有限。我们开展了一项受控实证研究，跨模型和微服务环境考察工具抽象与组合。我们实现了24个结构化工具，分别用于指标访问（L1）、证据分析（L2）和诊断（L3），并设置了一个基于Python的参考设定（L0）。使用来自三个微服务系统的375个故障案例，我们以Qwen3.7-Plus评估了八种配置，并在四种配置的共享子集上比较了四个Qwen模型。结果表明，工具配置对根因定位和故障类型识别的影响不同。使用Qwen3.7-Plus时，L3达到82.8%的top-1定位准确率，而L0为85.4%，同时每个案例所需时间不到一半。增加工具层级可以提高类型识别，同时降低定位准确率。轨迹分析揭示了智能体在查阅额外证据后覆盖正确诊断建议的案例。模型选择也会改变工具收益：将L3加入L1+L2可改善三个模型的诊断，但会损害Qwen3-8B，后者很少调用L3。工具配置排名在不同微服务系统之间也会变化。这些发现为设计和使用RCA工具提供了实证基础，指导根据模型、诊断目标和目标系统进行工具选择与组合。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly explored for root cause analysis (RCA) in microservice systems, yet empirical guidance on how to design and combine their tools remains limited. We conduct a controlled empirical study of tool abstraction and composition across models and microservice environments. We implement 24 structured tools for metric access (L1), evidence analysis (L2), and diagnosis (L3), alongside a Python-based reference setting (L0). Using 375 failure cases from three microservice systems, we evaluate eight configurations with Qwen3.7-Plus and compare four Qwen models on a shared subset of four configurations. Our results show that tool configurations affect root cause localization and failure type identification differently. With Qwen3.7-Plus, L3 achieves 82.8% top-1 localization accuracy compared with 85.4% for L0, while requiring less than half the time per case. Adding tool levels can improve type identification while reducing localization accuracy. Trajectory analysis reveals cases in which agents override correct diagnostic recommendations after consulting additional evidence. Model choice also changes tool benefits: adding L3 to L1+L2 improves diagnosis for three models but hurts Qwen3-8B, which rarely invokes L3. Tool configuration rankings further change across microservice systems. These findings provide an empirical foundation for designing and using RCA tools, guiding tool selection and composition according to the model, diagnostic objective, and target system.

</details>


### [18] [Characterizing Security Effects of OSS Vulnerabilities in Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05036)
*Yu Ji,Yang Wei,Yutao Hu,Haojun Zhao,Yueming Wu,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 研究 agent 工作流中开源依赖漏洞的运行时传播与影响边界，提出多层级 provenance+差分执行判定方法，对 coding agent 的依赖风险与执行级验证有可迁移价值。

TL;DR: 论文研究已知 OSS 漏洞在智能体工作流中的执行级后果，利用漏洞感知语义、差分执行和多层运行时溯源判断漏洞是否产生可观察安全效应及其影响边界，并在真实框架中发现多个安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件智能体依赖开源组件，其漏洞后果可经工具输出、智能体状态和模型暴露信息跨层传播，仅判断漏洞是否存在不足以说明是否实际产生影响。

Method: 分析已知 OSS 漏洞在智能体工作流中的表现；利用漏洞感知语义信息、易受攻击与修正软件的差分执行、跨多层运行时溯源，判定可观察安全效应并定位其最远表现层。

Result: 评估表明该方法能在多样化漏洞场景中准确区分已实现漏洞效应并确定表现边界；应用于真实世界智能体框架的已记录工作流时，发现其 OSS 依赖中已知漏洞引发的多个安全效应。

Conclusion: 应基于执行级后果而非仅漏洞存在性来推理易受攻击依赖；该工作强调依赖漏洞在智能体系统中的实际传播与影响边界。

中文摘要：软件智能体在执行工具并与外部系统交互时，越来越依赖开源组件。因此，这些依赖中的安全缺陷可能影响的不仅仅是其所在的软件进程：其后果可以通过工具输出、智能体状态以及随后暴露给模型的信息传递。确定此类后果是否在特定执行中实际实现，以及其影响在智能体系统内部何处停止，仍然具有挑战性。我们研究已知 OSS 漏洞在作为智能体工作流的一部分被触发时如何表现。我们的研究揭示了安全相关后果在跨运行时层出现和传播方式中的反复出现的模式。基于这些观察，我们使用漏洞感知的语义信息、易受攻击软件与已修正软件的差分执行，以及跨越多个层的运行时溯源，来确定漏洞是否产生可观察的安全效应，并识别该效应仍然表现的最远层。我们的评估表明，该方法能够在多样化的漏洞场景集合中准确区分已实现的漏洞效应并确定其表现边界。我们还将该分析应用于真实世界智能体框架中已记录的工作流，并发现了多个源自其 OSS 依赖中已知漏洞的安全效应。这些结果凸显了从执行级后果而非仅从漏洞存在性来推理易受攻击依赖的重要性。

Original abstract: Software agents increasingly depend on open-source components when executing tools and interacting with external systems. Security flaws in these dependencies may therefore influence more than the software process in which they occur: their consequences can be carried through tool outputs, agent state, and information subsequently exposed to the model. Determining whether such a consequence is actually realized in a particular execution, and where its influence stops within the agent system, remains challenging.
  We investigate how known OSS vulnerabilities behave when exercised as part of agent workflows. Our study reveals recurring patterns in the way security-relevant consequences emerge and propagate across runtime layers. Building on these observations, we use vulnerability-aware semantic information, differential executions of vulnerable and corrected software, and runtime provenance spanning multiple layers to determine whether a vulnerability produces an observable security effect and to identify the furthest layer at which that effect remains manifested.
  Our evaluation shows that this approach can accurately distinguish realized vulnerability effects and determine their manifestation boundaries across a diverse collection of vulnerability scenarios. We additionally apply the analysis to documented workflows in a real-world agent framework and uncover multiple security effects originating from known vulnerabilities in its OSS dependencies. These results highlight the importance of reasoning about vulnerable dependencies in terms of their execution-level consequences rather than vulnerability presence alone.

</details>


### [19] [Beyond Task Completion: Measuring Interaction Cost in Terminal User Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05047)
*Ruida Hu,Yuanhao Wang,Chao Peng,Yakun Zhang,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Web Page: https://tuinaut.pages.dev/ Source Code: https://github.com/kinesiatricssxilm14/Agent-as-a-User

Selection reason: 提出 Agent-as-a-User 范式，用 LLM agent 在 TUI 中记录交互轨迹并以独立 oracle 验证结果，作为执行级评测机制对 agent 评测设计有参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出Agent-as-a-User范式，让LLM智能体充当用户，以Agent-KLM分解解释与操作成本，并用TUINaut记录轨迹、用独立于行动者的oracle验证结果；在TUINaut-Bench上评估真实与生成TUI，发现总体成功率会掩盖任务差异，成本排序相关且稳定，生成TUI功能正确但仍需可用性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠任务完成无法衡量TUI理解与操作难度；现有人类评估和LLM生成评分或报告缺乏可重复、经验证任务执行支撑的交互努力测量。

Method: 提出Agent-as-a-User，将LLM智能体置于用户角色。Agent-KLM分离智能体侧解释成本与操作成本，并结构关联人类交互。TUINaut记录交互轨迹，并用独立于行动者的oracle验证结果。使用TUINaut-Bench研究15个真实TUI的84个任务，涉及6名人类参与者和5种观察-评估者配置，并评估3个领先技术栈生成的45个TUI。

Result: 人类与智能体的总体成功率相似，但所完成的任务存在差异；解释与操作成本的任务排序相关，操作尤其明显，且该模式跨配置持续。生成TUI可实现正确功能，但仍需可用性改进。

Conclusion: Agent-as-a-User是一种可重复、基于执行的范式，可用于测量基于任务的可用性。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地通过终端用户界面（TUIs）被使用，然而仅凭任务完成情况并不能刻画一个界面在理解和操作上的困难程度。现有的人类评估以及由LLM生成的评分或报告，无法提供基于经验证的任务执行的、可重复的交互努力测量。我们提出Agent-as-a-User，一种将LLM智能体置于用户角色的评估范式。Agent-KLM分离了智能体侧的解释成本与操作成本，并在结构上将它们与人类交互相关联。TUINaut通过记录交互轨迹并使用独立于行动者的oracle验证结果，将该范式可操作化。使用TUINaut-Bench，我们研究了15个真实世界TUI中的84个任务，涉及六名人类参与者和五种观察-评估者配置，并评估了由三个领先技术栈生成的45个TUI。相似的总体成功率掩盖了人类与智能体在完成哪些任务上的差异，而解释成本和操作成本则遵循相关的任务排序，尤其是在操作方面。这一模式在不同配置下持续存在。生成的TUI可以实现正确的功能，但仍然需要可用性改进。这些结果将Agent-as-a-User定位为一种可重复的、基于执行的范式，用于测量基于任务的可用性。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used through terminal user interfaces (TUIs), yet task completion alone does not capture how difficult an interface is to understand and operate. Existing human assessments and LLM-generated ratings or reports do not provide repeatable measurements of interaction effort grounded in verified task execution.
  We propose Agent-as-a-User, an evaluation paradigm that places an LLM agent in the user role. Agent-KLM separates agent-side interpretation and operation costs and relates them structurally to human interaction. TUINaut operationalizes the paradigm by recording interaction trajectories and verifying outcomes with actor-independent oracles.
  Using TUINaut-Bench, we study 84 tasks across 15 real-world TUIs with six human participants and five observation-evaluator configurations, and evaluate 45 TUIs generated by three leading stacks. Similar aggregate success rates mask differences in which tasks humans and agents complete, whereas interpretation and operation costs follow correlated task rankings, especially for operation. This pattern persists across configurations. Generated TUIs can implement correct functionality while still requiring usability improvements. These results position Agent-as-a-User as a repeatable, execution-grounded paradigm for measuring task-based usability.

</details>


### [20] [SpecAgent: Empowering Program Verification with Agentic Synthesis of Formal Program Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05132)
*Lezhi Ma,Han Wang,Shangqing Liu,Jiawan Wang,Lei Bu*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: SpecAgent 用依赖感知规划、RAG、agentic 修复与批判来合成 ACSL 规格，直接面向程序验证与 agent 工作流，可迁移到代码 agent 的验证与修复。

TL;DR: SpecAgent 是一个面向真实 C 程序的智能体框架，通过依赖感知规划、RAG、智能体修复与批评合成高质量 ACSL 规范，在规范合成和程序验证任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动构建形式化规范成本高，现有 LLM 方法多采用仅前向工作流和局部修复，难以处理真实程序中函数与循环间的复杂依赖及由先前规范错误引发的级联验证失败。

Method: 提出 SpecAgent，集成依赖感知规划、检索增强生成、智能体修复和智能体批评四个组件，用于为真实 C 程序合成高质量 ACSL 规范。

Result: 在 14 个真实仓库的 50 个程序上，SpecAgent 配合 DeepSeek-V4 达到 96.82% 精确率和 88.95% 召回率，优于所有基线；在 811 个真实验证目标上成功完成 604 个，也超过现有基线。

Conclusion: SpecAgent 能有效合成高质量规范并促进真实程序验证。

中文摘要：形式化规范对于演绎程序验证至关重要，它为复杂软件的组合式验证提供语义抽象。然而，手动构建规范劳动密集，这推动了自动合成。尽管大语言模型（LLM）近期取得进展，现有方法通常依赖仅前向的工作流和局部修复，限制了其在具有函数和循环之间复杂依赖的真实程序上的有效性。验证失败可能源于先前生成的规范，导致局部细化无法解决的级联失败。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 SpecAgent，一个用于为真实 C 程序合成高质量 ACSL 规范的智能体框架。SpecAgent 集成了四个组件：依赖感知规划以识别规范目标，检索增强生成（RAG）以提供相关规范模式和程序上下文，智能体修复以诊断缺陷并重新审视依赖规范，以及智能体批评以在证明成功之外评估和精炼语义强度。我们在规范合成和程序验证任务上评估 SpecAgent。在来自 14 个真实仓库的 50 个程序上，使用 DeepSeek-V4 的 SpecAgent 在合成正确且强的规范时达到 96.82% 精确率和 88.95% 召回率，优于所有基线。在 811 个真实验证目标上，它成功完成 604 个，也超过现有基线。这些结果证明 SpecAgent 在合成高质量规范和促进真实程序验证方面的有效性。

Original abstract: Formal specifications are essential for deductive program verification, providing semantic abstractions for compositional verification of complex software. However, manually constructing specifications is labor-intensive, motivating automated synthesis. Despite recent advances in large language models (LLMs), existing approaches often rely on forward-only workflows and localized repair, limiting their effectiveness on real-world programs with complex dependencies among functions and loops. Verification failures may stem from previously generated specifications, causing cascading failures that local refinement cannot resolve. To address these challenges, we present SpecAgent, an agentic framework for synthesizing high-quality ACSL specifications for real-world C programs. SpecAgent integrates four components: dependency-aware planning to identify specification targets, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to provide relevant specification patterns and program context, agentic repair to diagnose defects and revisit dependent specifications, and agentic critique to assess and refine semantic strength beyond proof success. We evaluate SpecAgent on specification synthesis and program verification tasks. On 50 programs from 14 real-world repositories, SpecAgent with DeepSeek-V4 achieves 96.82% precision and 88.95% recall in synthesizing correct and strong specifications, outperforming all baselines. On 811 real-world verification targets, it successfully discharges 604, also surpassing existing baselines. These results demonstrate SpecAgent's effectiveness in synthesizing high-quality specifications and facilitating real-world program verification.

</details>


### [21] [StateWise: Diagnosing and Repairing Persistent Operational State Before Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05241)
*Yongyuan Peng,Zhou Feng,Tongying Wu,Jiahao Chen,Yuan Su,Chunyi Zhou,Tianyu Du,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 21 pages, 8 figures

Selection reason: StateWise 在 agent 行动前诊断并修复持久化状态（反事实重规划+证据落地+独立校验），直接针对 coding agent 记忆/状态可靠性与长程执行，价值高。

TL;DR: StateWise 通过在执行前诊断并修复失效的持久操作状态，防止无效记录复用，在编码智能体任务中将总体正确率从 38.7% 提升至 93.3% 且无不安全动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体复用持久操作记录来支持跨任务工作，但环境或需求变化会使记录失效；现有审查、溯源和澄清机制可能无法修正底层持久状态，导致任务失败和不安全修改。

Method: 提出 StateWise，在执行前诊断并修复持久操作状态：用记录级反事实重规划识别决策关键记录，通过可靠性检查、机器可观察事实的只读验证和开发者意图的针对性澄清确定其当前有效性；用类型化证据接地将证据绑定到记录和作用域，支持带修复谱系的持久校正；修复后重规划，并在执行前进行独立状态-动作检查。

Result: 在 150 个可执行编码智能体案例及跨模型评估中，持久状态损坏时 StateWise 总体正确率为 93.3%，基线为 38.7%，且无不安全动作；组件消融、多任务实验和迁移评估显示了有效恢复、持久校正以及跨代码库和工具接口的可迁移性。

Conclusion: StateWise 能在动作执行前修复失效的持久状态，提高编码智能体在状态损坏下的正确性与安全性，并具备跨环境、跨仓库和跨工具接口的迁移能力。

中文摘要：LLM 智能体结合推理、工具使用和持久记忆，通过将存储的操作记录作为后续行动的前提，来支持跨任务的工作。然而，环境或需求变化可能使这些记录失效，而现有的行动审查、溯源追踪和澄清机制可能无法校正底层的持久状态。我们对编码智能体轨迹的审计识别出候选失效链，其中无效记录被复用，导致任务失败和不安全修改。我们提出 StateWise，一个用于在执行动作之前诊断和修复持久操作状态的框架。StateWise 使用记录级反事实重规划来识别对决策至关重要的记录，然后通过可靠性检查、对机器可观察事实的只读验证以及对开发者所拥有意图的针对性澄清，来确定这些记录的当前有效性。类型化证据接地将证据绑定到特定记录和作用域，从而支持带有修复谱系的持久校正。随后，智能体从修复后的状态重新规划，并在执行前进行独立的状态-动作检查。我们在 150 个可执行的编码智能体案例上评估 StateWise，这些案例涵盖多样的运行时环境、工作区配置和代码库设置，并辅以跨模型评估。在持久状态被破坏的情况下，StateWise 达到 93.3% 的总体正确率，而基线智能体为 38.7%，且没有不安全动作。组件消融、多任务实验和迁移评估进一步证明了有效的恢复、持久校正以及跨代码库和工具接口的可迁移性。

Original abstract: LLM agents combine reasoning, tool use, and persistent memory to support work across tasks by reusing stored operational records as premises for later actions. However, environmental or requirement changes can invalidate these records, while existing action review, provenance tracking, and clarification mechanisms may leave the underlying persistent state uncorrected. Our audit of coding-agent trajectories identifies candidate failure chains in which invalid records are reused, leading to task failures and unsafe modifications. We propose StateWise, a framework for diagnosing and repairing persistent operational state before action execution. StateWise uses record-level counterfactual replanning to identify decision-critical records, then establishes their current validity through reliability checks, read-only verification of machine-observable facts, and targeted clarification of developer-owned intent. Typed evidence grounding binds evidence to specific records and scopes, enabling persistent corrections with repair lineage. The agent then replans from the repaired state, followed by an independent state-action check before execution. We evaluate StateWise on 150 executable coding-agent cases across diverse runtime environments, workspace configurations, and repository settings, complemented by cross-model evaluations. Under corrupted persistent state, StateWise achieves 93.3% overall correctness, compared with 38.7% for the baseline agent, with no unsafe actions. Component ablations, multi-task experiments, and transfer evaluations further demonstrate effective recovery, persistent corrections, and transferability across repositories and tool interfaces.

</details>


### [22] [Mind the Gaps: From Failure Attribution to Closed-Form Repair of Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05277)
*Jian Gu,Hongyu Zhang,Chunyang Chen,Aldeida Aleti*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: ASTRA 针对代码语言模型 API 演化失效做闭式修补，指出定位与定制两个 gap，对代码生成模型的失效归因与自动修复有可迁移方法。

TL;DR: 论文检验代码模型修复中“归因神经元即可修补”和“通用更新适用所有失败”的假设，提出 ASTRA，用对比语义定位神经元并以闭式线性系统定制补丁，在 Python/Rust API 演化任务上全面领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码模型修复流程依赖两个未经验证的假设：失败归因选出的神经元就是应被修补的神经元；通用更新能适配每一种失败。作者在 Python 和 Rust 的可执行 API 演化任务上检验这两个假设，发现定位缺口与定制缺口：归因目标和可承载补丁的神经元重叠很低，不同承载神经元所需的补丁近乎正交。

Method: 提出 ASTRA。定位阶段采用对比语义归因，按神经元对目标 token 与已产生 token 之间 logit 对比的贡献打分，以选择承载神经元。修补阶段通过闭式求解一个小型线性系统来定制补丁，联合纠正一个样本的所有失败 token，无需优化器和反向传播。

Result: 归因目标与可承载补丁神经元的前若干集合 Jaccard 重叠仅 0.15 至 0.20，不同承载神经元的补丁近乎正交。对比语义在 6 种设置中的 3 种显著优于基于梯度的归因，其余相当。ASTRA 平均 Pass@1 为 66.7%，优于 AlphaEdit、STAR 和低秩适应最佳基线的 48.4%；单样本修复耗时 2.7 秒；在全部 6 种设置和每种 API 变更类型上均最佳，且在测试提示换用未见措辞时优势仍保持。副作用在大型模型上较小，在小型模型上较大。

Conclusion: 修复代码语言模型时应区分失败定位与补丁承载，并针对具体失败定制更新。ASTRA 通过对比语义定位和闭式定制补丁，在多种设置与 API 变更类型上取得最佳修复效果，但需关注其对无关代码的副作用，尤其在小模型上。

中文摘要：代码语言模型必须像其周边软件一样被维护：当库演进时，模型仍会写出其在训练期间所见到的接口。修复模型本身可让一次纠正惠及所有下游用途。现有修复方法将失败归因于神经元，选择排名最高的神经元，并施加通用更新。该流程假设被归因的神经元就是需要修补的神经元，且通用更新适用于每种失败，而这两个假设均未被检验。我们在 Python 和 Rust 的可执行 API 演化任务上、使用三个代码模型检验了二者，并识别出两个缺口。定位缺口将失败归因所针对的神经元与能够承载补丁的神经元区分开：它们的前若干集合的 Jaccard 重叠仅为 0.15 至 0.20。定制缺口将通用更新与针对该失败构建的补丁区分开：不同承载神经元所需的补丁近乎正交。为解决这两个缺口，我们提出 ASTRA。它通过对比语义来定位神经元，这种归因根据神经元对目标 token 与已产生 token 之间 logit 对比的贡献进行打分。随后，它通过以闭式解求解一个小型线性系统来定制补丁，从而联合纠正一个样本中所有失败 token，且既不需要优化器也不需要反向传播。在 6 种设置中的 3 种里，对比语义选出的承载神经元显著优于基于梯度的归因，在其他设置中则与之相当。平均而言，ASTRA 达到 66.7% 的 Pass@1，而 AlphaEdit、STAR 和低秩适应中最佳方法为 48.4%，且修复一个样本耗时 2.7 秒。它在所有 6 种设置以及每种 API 变更类型中都是最佳方法，且该优势在测试提示以未见过的措辞表述时依然存在。其对无关代码的副作用在大型模型上较小，在小型模型上较大。

Original abstract: Code language models must be maintained like the software around them: when a library evolves, a model keeps writing the interface that it saw during training. Repairing the model itself lets one correction reach all downstream uses. Existing repair methods attribute a failure to neurons, select the highest-ranked ones, and apply a generic update. This pipeline assumes that the attributed neurons are the ones to patch and that a generic update fits every failure, and neither assumption has been examined. We examine both on executable API evolution tasks in Python and Rust with three code models and identify two gaps. The targeting gap separates the neurons that failure attribution targets from the neurons that can carry a patch: their top sets have a Jaccard overlap of only 0.15 to 0.20. The tailoring gap separates a generic update from a patch built for the failure: the patches that different carrier neurons need are nearly orthogonal. To address both gaps, we propose ASTRA. It targets neurons by contrastive semantics, an attribution that scores a neuron by its contribution to the logit contrast between the target token and the produced token. It then tailors the patch by solving one small linear system in closed form, which corrects all failing tokens of a sample jointly and needs neither an optimizer nor a backward pass. Contrastive semantics selects significantly better carrier neurons than gradient-based attributions in 3 of 6 settings and comparable ones in the others. On average, ASTRA reaches 66.7 percent Pass@1, against 48.4 percent for the best of AlphaEdit, STAR and low-rank adaptation, and repairs a sample in 2.7 seconds. It is the best method in all 6 settings and for every type of API change, and this advantage persists under an unseen phrasing of the test prompt. Its side effects on unrelated code are small on the large models and larger on the small one.

</details>


### [23] [Understanding the Hierarchical Structure and Functional Landscape of the Model Context Protocol Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05319)
*Tingxuan Tang,Zilong Chen,Yue,Xiao*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 构建跨市场 MCP 工具层级功能分类并验证可提升模型任务完成率，直接服务于 agent 的工具检索与选择。

TL;DR: 该论文构建了 MCPacific，一个覆盖 17 个市场、124,267 个唯一 MCP 服务器和 1,328,233 条工具规范的工具级跨市场功能图谱，并基于 58,915 项能力的分层分类体系揭示 MCP 工具生态的领域分布、替代方案差异及分类呈现对任务完成率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP 服务器数量庞大但仅按市场特定的粗粒度类别组织，导致智能体和用户难以按操作查找工具、发现功能替代方案并比较其差异，因此需要工具级、跨市场的功能组织与映射。

Method: 收集 17 个市场的 368,754 个 MCP 服务器列表，去重得到 124,267 个唯一服务器；静态提取七种语言的 1,328,233 条工具规范；通过迭代 LLM 驱动设计-测试-精炼构建 58,915 项能力的分层功能分类体系；使用校准嵌入路由将完整语料映射到该分类体系。

Result: 85% 的 MCP 工具服务于开发者工具以外领域；98.5% 的工具至少有一个功能替代方案，但近四分之一能力仅由一个工具支持；功能可比工具在安全警报、代码复杂度和项目维护上存在差异，41% 的可比工具对复杂度差异超过 2.5 倍；以分类体系而非扁平列表呈现候选工具，可提升四个评估模型的任务完成率，在拥挤候选集中 Pass@0.75 最高提升 12 个百分点。

Conclusion: MCPacific 提供了目前最大规模的工具级、跨市场 MCP 功能图谱，表明 MCP 已扩展到广泛领域且替代方案分布不均；按功能分类组织候选工具有助于提升智能体任务完成率，并为工具发现、比较和选择提供结构化基础。

中文摘要：AI 智能体日益依赖通过模型上下文协议（Model Context Protocol, MCP）暴露的工具来完成用户任务。市场中共列出了数十万个 MCP 服务器，但它们仅按粗粒度、特定于市场的服务器类别进行组织。这使得智能体和用户难以针对给定操作识别工具、找到功能替代方案，并评估这些替代方案之间的差异。我们提出 MCPacific，这是 MCP 生态系统中最大的工具级、跨市场图谱。MCPacific 收集了来自 17 个市场的 368,754 个 MCP 服务器列表，对应 124,267 个唯一服务器，从这些服务器中静态提取了七种语言的 1,328,233 条工具规范，并将它们组织成一个包含 58,915 项能力的分层功能分类体系。我们通过迭代的 LLM 驱动设计-测试-精炼过程构建该分类体系，并使用校准的嵌入路由将完整语料映射到其中。我们的研究揭示，MCP 远远超出了开发者工具范畴，85% 的工具服务于其他领域。功能替代方案广泛存在但分布不均：98.5% 的工具至少有一个替代方案，但近四分之一的能力仅由一个工具支持。功能可比的工具在安全警报、代码复杂度和项目维护方面也存在差异，在 41% 的可比工具对中，复杂度差异超过 2.5 倍。最后，通过该分类体系而非扁平列表来呈现候选工具，可提高所有四个被评估模型的任务完成率，在拥挤的候选集中，Pass@0.75 的提升最高达 12 个百分点。

Original abstract: AI agents increasingly rely on tools exposed through the Model Context Protocol (MCP) to complete user tasks. Hundreds of thousands of MCP servers are listed across marketplaces, yet they are organized only by coarse, marketplace-specific server categories. This makes it difficult for agents and users to identify tools for a given operation, find functional alternatives, and assess how those alternatives differ. We present MCPacific, the largest tool-level, cross-marketplace map of the MCP ecosystem. MCPacific collects 368,754 MCP server listings corresponding to 124,267 unique servers across 17 marketplaces, statically extracts 1,328,233 tool specifications from these servers in seven languages, and organizes them into a hierarchical functional taxonomy of 58,915 capabilities. We construct the taxonomy through an iterative LLM-driven design-test-refine process and map the full corpus to it using calibrated embedding routing. Our study reveals that MCP extends well beyond developer tooling, with 85% of tools serving other domains. Functional alternatives are widespread but unevenly distributed: 98.5% of tools have at least one alternative, yet nearly a quarter of capabilities are supported by only one tool. Functionally comparable tools also differ in security alerts, code complexity, and project maintenance, with complexity differing by more than 2.5x in 41% of comparable tool pairs. Finally, presenting candidate tools through the taxonomy rather than a flat list improves task completion rate across all four evaluated models, with gains of up to 12 percentage points in Pass@0.75 for crowded candidate sets.

</details>


### [24] [Adaptive Code Revision Attacks on AI Pull Request Reviewers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05399)
*Jingzhi Gong,Jie M. Zhang,Gunel Jahangirova,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: AFCRA 利用 AI PR 审查反馈自适应改写代码以保留漏洞，揭示自动审查的验证盲点，对 agent 代码审查与安全验证机制有迁移意义。

TL;DR: 该文研究 AI 自动化 PR 审查反馈是否可被攻击者用于引导代码修订以保留漏洞，提出 AFCRA 与 AFCRA-Bench，实验显示其攻击成功率显著高于文本/评论攻击，并给出安全启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 代理越来越多参与 PR 审查并给出修复反馈；对攻击者而言，反馈可能泄露获批所需修改。现有 PR 攻击主要靠文本/评论说服且不改代码，尚未明确能否利用反馈修复表象问题而保留漏洞。

Method: 开展实证研究，提出 AFCRA 自适应反馈引导代码修订攻击；构建 AFCRA-Bench，含 159 个已披露漏洞及可执行漏洞利用；与 Sonnet 5、GPT-5.5 审查者进行五轮交互，并与文本/评论攻击对比。

Result: AFCRA 成功率分别为最强文本/评论攻击的 2.5 倍与 12.5 倍；案例显示审查者接受所报问题修复却忽略遗留漏洞。

Conclusion: 反馈引导的代码修订构成自动化 PR 审查威胁；为研究者、AI 提供商、PR 审查者与作者提出保障 AI 辅助开发安全的可操作启示。

中文摘要：拉取请求（pull-request）审查在新代码到达用户之前保护软件，有助于防止可能使用户暴露于攻击的漏洞。AI 代理越来越多地执行这些审查，并解释哪些问题需要修复。然而，对于提交易受攻击代码的攻击者来说，这种反馈也揭示了哪些更改可能获得批准。现有的 PR 攻击通过有说服力的文本和评论来寻求此类批准，同时保持可执行代码不变。这使得尚不清楚攻击者是否可以利用该反馈来修复所报告的问题，同时在修订后的代码中保留漏洞。因此，我们使用 AFCRA（自适应反馈引导的代码修订攻击，Adaptive Feedback-guided Code Revision Attack）对此威胁进行实证研究。为了区分成功攻击与真正的修复，我们基于 159 个已披露漏洞构建了 AFCRA-Bench，并带有可执行漏洞利用，以验证代码中的漏洞。在与 Sonnet 5 和 GPT-5.5 审查者的五轮交互中，AFCRA 的成功率分别达到所评估最强的基于文本或评论的攻击的 2.5 倍和 12.5 倍。这些成功案例研究表明，审查者会接受对所报告问题的修复，同时忽略仍存在的漏洞。这些发现确立了反馈引导的代码修订是对自动化 PR 审查的一种威胁。为了应对这一威胁，我们为研究人员、AI 提供商、PR 审查者和 PR 作者得出了关于保障 AI 辅助开发安全的可操作启示。

Original abstract: Pull-request review protects software before new code reaches users, helping prevent vulnerabilities that could expose users to attacks. AI agents increasingly perform these reviews and explain which problems need fixing. However, for an attacker submitting vulnerable code, this feedback also reveals what changes may secure approval. Existing PR attacks seek such approval through persuasive text and comments while keeping executable code fixed. This leaves unclear whether an attacker can use the feedback to repair the reported problem while preserving a vulnerability in the revised code. We therefore conduct an empirical study of this threat using AFCRA (Adaptive Feedback-guided Code Revision Attack). To distinguish successful attacks from genuine repairs, we construct AFCRA-Bench from 159 disclosed vulnerabilities, with executable exploits to verify vulnerabilities in code.
  Across five-round interactions with Sonnet 5 and GPT-5.5 reviewers, AFCRA reaches success rates 2.5x and 12.5x those of the strongest evaluated text- or comment-based attack. Case studies of these successes show how reviewers accept repairs of reported problems while overlooking surviving vulnerabilities. These findings establish feedback-guided code revision as a threat to automated PR review. To address this threat, we derive actionable implications for researchers, AI providers, PR reviewers, and PR authors on securing AI-assisted development.

</details>


### [25] [UndoBench: Separating Task Competence from Recovery Capability in Tool-Using AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05622)
*Dolly Sah,Tanmay Sah,Harshul Jain,Tanya Sah*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 18 pages, 6 figures. Code and benchmark available at https://github.com/tradertanmay/undobench

Selection reason: UndoBench 以配对反事实试验分离工具使用 agent 的任务能力与故障恢复能力，并提供 wire 级效果与环境状态 oracle，是 agent 恢复评测的重要基准。

TL;DR: UndoBench 通过反事实配对试验将任务能力与故障恢复能力解耦，发现名义完成率高但恢复成功率低，且恢复漏洞具有阶段依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估名义任务完成，混淆了规划能力与运行故障恢复能力，导致自主智能体的恢复漏洞被掩盖。

Method: 提出 UndoBench，覆盖8个企业领域、36个基础工作流和36个故障场景；采用相同种子下的反事实配对试验，配合线路级效应历史和环境状态预言机；在12个留出TEST工作流上，跨2个开放权重模型、2个框架、3种恢复范式进行5,760次执行/2,880次配对试验，并扩展到商业API模型。

Result: 名义能力达83.54%，条件恢复成功率（CRSR）仅46.72%；朴素重试在53.33%试验中产生重复外部效应。商业API模型复现能力-恢复分离。恢复呈阶段依赖：变更前方法相似且无重复效应；部分变更期间朴素重试、每次调用幂等性和零权限日志记录失效；提交后确认前，验证和服务器端幂等性显著提升安全性。

Conclusion: 仅评估名义完成会掩盖自主智能体中关键的、阶段依赖的恢复漏洞；需在基准中显式评估故障恢复。

中文摘要：使用工具的AI智能体正越来越多地部署于企业软件系统中，但广泛使用的基准主要评估名义任务完成情况，将基线规划能力与运行故障恢复混为一谈。我们提出 UndoBench，一个涵盖8个企业领域、36个基础工作流和36个故障场景的基准，通过相同种子下的反事实配对试验，以及线路级效应历史和环境状态预言机，将任务能力与恢复能力解耦。在冻结的丢失确认研究中，针对12个留出的TEST工作流，跨两个开放权重模型、两个框架和三种恢复范式（5,760次执行/2,880次配对试验），名义能力达到83.54%，而条件恢复成功率（CRSR）降至46.72%，朴素重试在53.33%的试验中产生重复外部效应。对商业API模型的扩展复现了这种能力-恢复分离。跨互补执行边界的评估表明，恢复具有阶段依赖性：在变更之前，各方法表现相似，且在有能力完成的试验中没有重复效应；在部分变更期间，朴素重试、每次调用幂等性和零权限日志记录在所评估的组合工作流上崩溃；在提交之后但确认之前，验证和服务器端幂等性显著提高了安全性。这些发现表明，仅评估名义完成情况会掩盖自主智能体中关键的、阶段依赖的恢复漏洞。

Original abstract: Tool-using AI agents are increasingly deployed across enterprise software systems, yet widely used benchmarks primarily evaluate nominal task completion, conflating baseline planning competence with operational fault recovery. We introduce UndoBench, a benchmark spanning 36 base workflows and 36 fault scenarios across 8 enterprise domains, decoupling task competence from recovery capability via counterfactual paired trials under identical seeds alongside wire-level effect-history and environment-state oracles. On 12 held-out TEST workflows across two open-weight models, two frameworks, and three recovery paradigms (5,760 executions / 2,880 paired trials) in the frozen lost-acknowledgment study, nominal competence reached 83.54% while conditional recovery success rate (CRSR) fell to 46.72%, with naive retry producing duplicate external effects in 53.33% of trials. Extensions to commercial API models reproduced this competence-recovery separation. Evaluations across complementary execution boundaries show that recovery is phase-dependent: before mutation, methods perform similarly without duplicate effects among capable trials; during partial mutation, naive retry, per-call idempotency, and zero-privilege journaling collapse on the evaluated composite workflows; after commit but before acknowledgment, verification and server-side idempotency substantially improve safety. These findings demonstrate that evaluating nominal completion alone masks critical, phase-dependent recovery vulnerabilities in autonomous agents.

</details>


### [26] [PreMaQ: Predicting Maintainability-Related Quality of LLM-Generated Code Before Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05858)
*Taiki Wakamatsu,Yuta Ishimoto,Masanari Kondo,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 10 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: PreMaQ 在生成前从 LLM 内部表示预测生成代码的可维护性（代码坏味/可维护性指数），可用于代码生成质量预筛，具参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出PreMaQ，在生成前利用LLM内部表示预测生成代码的代码异味分数和可维护性指数，并在4个LLM与4个Python基准的16个组合上验证预测相关性及模型选择增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在软件开发中采用LLM生成代码时，除功能正确性外还需关注可维护性相关质量；若能在生成前估计此类质量，可避免生成、审查和丢弃低质量代码的开销，但此前尚不清楚可维护性相关质量是否像功能正确性一样可提前预测。

Method: 提出生成前可维护性相关质量预测（PreMaQ），从LLM生成前的内部表示预测生成代码的代码异味分数（CSS）和可维护性指数（MI）；评估涵盖四个开放权重LLM和四个Python代码生成基准，共2,695个任务，并用于模型选择，同时与提示嵌入结合。

Result: 在所有16个模型-基准组合中，预测CSS和MI均与观测值相关，平均Spearman秩相关系数分别为0.57和0.65；用于模型选择时，PreMaQ在多个模型均能生成功能正确代码的任务上达到理想可维护性选择器相对随机选择所获提升的59.5%，结合提示嵌入后提高到60.7%，表明两类信号互补。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，PreMaQ能够用于预测并改善LLM生成代码的可维护性相关质量。
