中文摘要：递归自我改进（RSI）依赖评估反馈来评估进展并指导进一步研究，然而反复运行复杂基准测试成本高昂且会拖慢迭代。人类专家通过选择基准测试子集或设计紧凑测试套件来降低这一成本。我们提出，AI智能体能否自动化这一设计过程，并引入EvalResearchBench（ERB），一个用于自主评估研究的基准。给定目标材料、开发参考、候选API以及固定的时间和API预算，一个名为researcher的智能体选择或合成任务、实现评分器，并在试点测试中修订它们，之后冻结一个用于编码、协作和推理的可执行评估器。我们研究了9个researcher和13个候选模型，并在分数一致性和成对一致性上将每个冻结评估器与14个目标基准进行比较。最佳评估器对约75%的候选对排序与目标基准相同，低于由目标基准之间的分歧所设定的91%上限。没有哪个researcher在所有指标上都领先，且在开发目标上表现最佳的评估器在隐藏于researcher的密封目标上并非最佳。一个由人类设计的公共任务样本仍然是一个强基线，而记录执行成本最低的评估器获得了最高的成对一致性。智能体通过试点反馈修复任务和评分器，但它们的评估器仍可能截断答案、耗尽评估预算，或让少数问题主导某个领域分数。

Original abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) relies on evaluation feedback to assess progress and guide further research, yet repeatedly running complex benchmarks is costly and slows iteration. Human experts reduce this cost by selecting benchmark subsets or designing compact suites. We ask whether AI agents can automate this design process and introduce EvalResearchBench (ERB), a benchmark for autonomous evaluation research. Given target materials, development references, candidate APIs, and fixed time and API budgets, an agent called the researcher selects or synthesizes tasks, implements graders, and revises them in pilot tests before freezing an executable evaluator for coding, co-work, and reasoning. We study 9 researchers and 13 candidate models and compare each frozen evaluator with 14 target benchmarks on score concordance and pairwise agreement. The best evaluators order about 75\% of candidate pairs as the targets do, below the 91\% ceiling set by disagreements among the targets. No researcher leads on every metric, and the best evaluator on development targets is not the best on sealed targets hidden from the researcher. A human-designed sample of public tasks remains a strong baseline, and the evaluator with the lowest recorded execution cost attains the highest pairwise agreement. Agents repair tasks and graders through pilot feedback, yet their evaluators can still truncate answers, exhaust the evaluation budget, or let a few questions dominate a domain score.

</details>


### [54] [MemLeak: Cross-User Semantic Leakage in Multi-Tenant AI Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04195)
*Priyanka Mudgal,Kai Zhao,Guilin Zhang,Andy Olsen,Ezekiel Miller,Xu Chu,Aletta Johanna Blanken*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Neurips-PALM 2026

Selection reason: 揭示多租户 agent 长期记忆向量库的跨用户语义泄漏并提出所有权门控缓解，涉及 agent 记忆的可靠性与安全控制。

TL;DR: 企业多租户个人AI智能体共享向量记忆会导致严重的跨用户记忆泄漏，非对抗泄漏率达70%--100%，端到端响应污染可达5/5；硬性检索后所有权门控是唯一一致有效且低延迟的缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业多租户智能体共享长期记忆向量库与嵌入空间，普通余弦检索即可造成跨用户记忆泄漏，需形式化该风险并评估其严重性与缓解方案。

Method: 将问题形式化为跨用户可接纳性失效，在TF-IDF与MiniLM-L6-v2检索下开展六个实验及后续消融，测试非对抗与对抗构造记忆、端到端响应污染，并比较三种架构缓解措施（含硬性检索后所有权门控）及延迟开销。

Result: 非对抗偶然泄漏在池化同团队检索下达70%--100%；对抗构造记忆的top-k置入率达90%--100%，稠密检索下得分提升+0.416至+0.511；端到端污染在生产检索路径为5.00/5、Claude Sonnet 4.5下为4.67/5，污染回答常被评为同样或更有帮助；三种缓解中仅硬性检索后所有权门控恢复干净基线1.00/5，每次查询延迟约1.4 ms。

Conclusion: 共享嵌入空间本身可导致跨用户可接纳性失效，硬性检索后所有权门控是有效且可部署的缓解方案，其他架构缓解措施不足。

中文摘要：企业多租户部署中的个人AI智能体共享一个用于长期记忆的公共向量存储。共享嵌入空间为跨用户记忆泄漏创造了暴露面：用户的查询可以通过普通余弦相似度检索，在无需任何漏洞利用的情况下，检索到属于另一用户的语义相邻记忆。我们将此形式化为跨用户可接纳性失效，并在稀疏（TF-IDF）与生产级忠实（MiniLM-L6-v2）检索下，通过六个实验及后续消融进行评估。非对抗性的、偶然的泄漏在池化{同团队}检索下达到70%--100%；对抗性构造的记忆实现90%--100%的top-$k$置入率，超过较弱的基于关键词的攻击者基线，在生产级忠实稠密检索（配置B）下得分提升$+0.416$至$+0.511$；端到端响应污染在生产检索路径下达到5.00/5，在Claude Sonnet~4.5下达到4.67/5，且受污染响应通常被评分与干净响应同样有帮助或更有帮助，这一差距经人工判断验证。在三种架构缓解措施中，只有硬性检索后所有权门控能一致地将干净基线恢复至（1.00/5），在{两个生成模型上，测得每次查询约1.4~ms的延迟开销。

Original abstract: Personal AI agents in enterprise multi-tenant deployments share a common vector store for long-term memory. Shared embedding spaces create a surface for cross-user memory leakage: a user's query can retrieve semantically adjacent memories belonging to another user through ordinary cosine-similarity retrieval, without any exploit. We formalize this as cross-user admissibility failure and evaluate it across six experiments, plus follow-up ablations, under both sparse (TF-IDF) and production-faithful (MiniLM-L6-v2) retrieval. Non-adversarial, incidental leakage reaches 70--100\% under pooled {same-team} retrieval; adversarially crafted memories achieve 90--100\% top-$k$ placement, exceeding weaker keyword-based attacker baselines, with score lifts of $+0.416$ to $+0.511$ under production-faithful dense retrieval (Config B); and end-to-end response contamination reaches 5.00/5 under a production retrieval path and 4.67/5 with Claude Sonnet~4.5, with contaminated responses often scoring as helpful or more helpful than clean ones, a gap validated against human judgment. Among three architectural mitigations, only hard post-retrieval ownership gating consistently restores the clean baseline (1.00/5) across {two generation models, at a measured latency overhead of roughly 1.4~ms per query.

</details>


### [55] [Asynchronous Is Nearly Free for Evolution Strategies on Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04196)
*William Hoy,Jingxuan Fan,Nurcin Celik,Xu Pan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 在终端式 agentic coding 任务上验证有界陈旧异步进化策略后训练，其同步/异步 rollout 权衡与策略滞后容忍度对 agent 训练有直接参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出有界陈旧度异步进化策略（ES），在 Endless Terminals 多轮终端编码任务上验证 Async-1 与同步 ES 相当，并量化策略陈旧度带来的有限性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 长时程智能体后训练的 rollout 生成长、完成时间方差大，同步更新造成快 worker 等待慢 worker；异步 RL 虽提升吞吐但带来策略滞后与离策略问题；ES 无需反向传播但 rollout 需求大且多为同步。

Method: 在 ES 后训练中引入有界陈旧度异步机制；以 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 在 Endless Terminals 基准上评测；比较自然 Async-1、同步 ES、以及延迟 10% 更新队列 4/8 个策略更新的受控调度；并与 GRPO 对比。

Result: 三个评估随机种子下，Async-1 留出成功率 25.9%，同步 ES 25.4%；延迟 4/8 个更新分别降低 1.6/3.1 个百分点，且无显式离策略校正。GRPO 总体更优，达 29.0% 留出成功率。

Conclusion: ES 能容忍中等策略陈旧度且退化有限，为异步 ES 后训练改进提供依据；作者称首次在多轮终端风格智能体编码任务上证明同步与异步 ES 的有效性。

中文摘要：基于 LLM 的长时程智能体后训练常常受限于 rollout 生成：轨迹跨越多个交互轮次，完成时间差异很大，而同步更新屏障会让更快的 worker 等待落后者。异步强化学习已在 LLM 后训练中被采用，它通过在有轨迹到达时即消费它们来缓解这种低效，但会引入策略滞后和离策略优化。进化策略（ES）为 LLM 后训练提供了一种无需反向传播的替代方案，但它依赖于更多数量的 rollout，且现有实践在很大程度上仍是同步的。在这篇短文论文中，我们引入了有界陈旧度异步 ES，并在 Endless Terminals 基准上使用 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 进行了演示。在三个评估随机种子中，自然 Async-1 与同步 ES 相匹配，取得 25.9% 对 25.4% 的留出成功率。受控调度将每个更新队列中 10% 的样本分别延迟四个或八个策略更新，仅使成功率分别降低 1.6 和 3.1 个百分点，且没有显式的离策略校正。GRPO 总体表现更好，达到 29.0% 的留出成功率，但重要的是，我们的结果表明 ES 能容忍中等程度的策略陈旧度且性能退化有限，这为未来通过异步算法改进基于 ES 的后训练开辟了可能性。据我们所知，我们是首个在多轮终端风格智能体编码任务上证明同步和异步 ES 有效性的工作。

Original abstract: LLM-based long-horizon agentic post-training is often bottlenecked by rollout generation: trajectories span many interaction turns, completion times vary substantially, and synchronous update barriers leave faster workers waiting for stragglers. Asynchronous reinforcement learning which has been adopted in LLM post-training addresses this inefficiency by consuming trajectories as they arrive, but introduces policy lag and off-policy optimization. Evolution strategies (ES) offer a backpropagation-free alternative for LLM post-training, yet it relies on a larger number of rollouts and existing practices have remained largely synchronous. In this short-form paper, we introduce bounded-staleness asynchronous ES and demonstrate it on Endless Terminals benchmark using Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. Across three evaluation seeds, natural Async-1 matches synchronous ES, achieving 25.9\% versus 25.4\% held-out success. Controlled schedules that delay 10\% of each update cohort by four or eight policy updates reduce success by only 1.6 and 3.1 percentage points, respectively, without explicit off-policy correction. GRPO performs better overall, reaching 29.0\% held-out success, but importantly our results show that ES tolerates moderate policy staleness with limited degradation, opening possibilities for future improvement of ES-based post-training with asynchronous algorithms. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to demonstrate the effectiveness of sync and async ES on a multi-turn terminal style agentic coding task.

</details>


### [56] [Spec2Game: Can LLMs Generate Complete Playable Games from Detailed Specifications?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04253)
*Yixue Cai,Yuzhe Zhao,Hanxiang Chao,Qingsen Ma,Ziheng Xiong,Jinhu Qi,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 37 pages, 11 figures

Selection reason: Spec2Game 要求从详细规格生成完整可运行 Pygame 项目并评估规格实现度，是可迁移的代码生成/规格遵循 benchmark，结论对 coding agent 有启发。

TL;DR: 论文提出 Spec2Game 基准，用 15 个游戏族、150 个任务实例和 14 个 LLM 的 3330 个项目，评估模型从自然语言规格生成完整 Pygame 交互程序的能力；结果发现高可执行性不等于忠实实现规格，规则、机制、目标与终止逻辑建模仍是主要难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成可执行程序并不等于正确实现自然语言行为需求，因此需要系统评估大语言模型将详细规格转化为完整交互式程序的能力。

Method: 构建 Spec2Game 基准，要求模型根据详细自然语言游戏规格生成完整 Pygame 项目；基准含 15 个游戏族、150 个任务实例，每族含 1 个规范任务和 9 个受控规则变体，覆盖 3 个实现复杂度层级；利用源代码、运行时和视觉证据，从可执行性、规格实现、代码质量和面向用户质量四个维度评估 14 个 LLM 生成的 3330 个项目。

Result: 高可执行性并不代表忠实的规格实现；组件级分析显示，模型在游戏元素建模上的表现明显优于规则与机制建模以及目标与终止条件建模。

Conclusion: 忠实实现游戏规则和终止逻辑仍是重大挑战，评估 LLM 编程能力不能仅以程序可执行性为标准。

中文摘要：生成可执行程序并不一定意味着其正确实现了自然语言所规定的行为需求。为了评估大语言模型将详细规格实现为完整交互式程序的能力，我们提出了 Spec2Game，一个要求模型根据详细的自然语言游戏规格生成完整 Pygame 项目的基准。Spec2Game 包含 15 个游戏族和 150 个任务实例，每个游戏族包含一个规范任务和九个受控规则变体，跨越三个实现复杂度级别。我们使用源代码、运行时和视觉证据，从四个维度评估生成的项目——可执行性、规格实现、代码质量和面向用户的质量。在 14 个 LLM 和 3,330 个生成项目上，我们发现高可执行性并不意味着忠实的规格实现。组件级分析进一步表明，模型在游戏元素建模上的表现显著优于规则与机制建模或目标与终止条件建模，这表明忠实实现游戏规则和终止逻辑仍然是一项重大挑战。

Original abstract: Generating an executable program does not necessarily mean that it correctly implements the behavioral requirements specified in natural language. To evaluate large language models' ability to realize detailed specifications as complete interactive programs, we introduce Spec2Game, a benchmark that requires models to generate complete Pygame projects from detailed natural-language game specifications. Spec2Game comprises 15 game families and 150 task instances, with one canonical task and nine controlled rule variants per family, spanning three levels of implementation complexity. Using source-code, runtime, and visual evidence, we evaluate generated projects along four dimensions---Executability, Specification Realization, Code Quality, and User-Facing Quality. Across 14 LLMs and 3,330 generated projects, we find that high executability does not imply faithful specification realization. Component-level analysis further shows that models perform substantially better on Game Element Modeling than on Rule and Mechanism Modeling or Goal and Termination Modeling, indicating that faithfully implementing game rules and termination logic remains a major challenge.

</details>


### [57] [CADForge: Agentic Single-View CAD Reconstruction with Explicit Geometry Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04262)
*Keyang Lu,Zhifei Yang,Tianao Dong,Mingzhe Xing,Zhen Xiao,Yikai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: CADForge 以 agent 逐步生成 CadQuery 代码，含显式几何推理、review agent 反馈迭代、失败驱动工具沉淀与参数化记忆，是典型的工具化+验证+记忆 coding agent 架构。

TL;DR: CADForge是一种智能体框架，可从单张图像逐步生成可编辑的CadQuery参数化CAD模型；它通过组件分解、显式几何约束推理、审查反馈、失败引导工具构建和参数化CAD记忆，在重建保真度和感知质量上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单视图图像重建可编辑参数化CAD模型对现代制造具有重要价值，但受限于不完整的几何观测和复杂的零件间关系，该任务仍很困难。

Method: 提出CADForge智能体框架，将物体分解为CAD意义组件并逐组件显式几何推理：先识别CAD相关约束，再用数学代码转化为精确建模参数；推断参数驱动组件级可执行CadQuery程序合成，审查智能体评估几何并反馈以迭代细化；还引入失败引导工具箱构建机制和紧凑参数化CAD记忆。

Result: 在多种单部件和多部件物体上的实验表明，CADForge在重建保真度和感知质量方面始终优于现有基线。

Conclusion: CADForge为准确的单视图CAD重建提供了一种有效方法。

中文摘要：从单视图图像重建可编辑的参数化CAD模型对现代制造业具有重要实用价值，但由于几何观测不完整以及零件间关系复杂，该任务仍具挑战性。为解决这一问题，我们提出CADForge，一种逐步将单张图像转换为CadQuery程序的智能体框架。CADForge将物体分解为具有CAD意义的组件，并对每个组件进行显式几何推理；该过程首先识别与CAD相关的约束，然后通过数学代码将其转化为精确的建模参数。推断出的参数随后驱动可执行CadQuery程序的组件级合成，并由审查智能体评估生成的几何体并提供有针对性的反馈以进行迭代细化。为进一步提高鲁棒性和效率，CADForge引入失败引导的工具箱构建机制，将积累的经验提炼为工具，并维护一个紧凑的参数化CAD记忆，以按需检索建模上下文。在多种单部件和多部件物体上的实验表明，CADForge在重建保真度和感知质量方面始终优于现有基线，证明了准确单视图CAD重建的一种有效方法。

Original abstract: Reconstructing editable parametric CAD models from a single-view image is of great practical value for modern manufacturing, yet remains challenging due to incomplete geometric observations and complex inter-part relationships. To address it, we propose CADForge, an agentic framework that progressively converts a single image into CadQuery programs. CADForge decomposes an object into CAD-meaningful components and performs explicit geometric reasoning for each component, a process that first identifies CAD-relevant constraints and then translates them into precise modeling parameters through mathematical code. The inferred parameters then drive component-wise synthesis of executable CadQuery programs, with a review agent evaluating the resulting geometry and providing targeted feedback for iterative refinement. To further improve robustness and efficiency, CADForge incorporates a failure-guided toolkit construction mechanism to distill accumulated experience into tools, and maintains a compact parametric CAD memory for retrieving modeling context on demand. Experiments on diverse single- and multi-part objects show that CADForge consistently outperforms existing baselines in reconstruction fidelity and perceptual quality, demonstrating an effective approach to accurate single-view CAD reconstruction.

</details>


### [58] [VIGIL: Verifier-Informed Gated Improvement Loop for Spreadsheet Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04287)
*Kang Li,Lu He,Sandarsita Guntupalli*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: VIGIL 提出带门控的持续 harness 学习与验证器信息修复，强调有界可审计的 harness 更新与非回归风险，与 agent harness 优化和可靠迭代直接相关。

TL;DR: 该文提出 VIGIL，在固定基础模型、提示、检索器和候选池的前提下，通过问题内 SQL 候选验证修复与跨轮次门控全回放，仅更新契约校准器和列选择器，实现电子表格问答的持续 harness 适配；结果显示有界改进，但也暴露有限回放门控的泛化局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体需要从延迟反馈中持续改进，但不应让每次纠正都改写系统行为；因此本文研究语料库级电子表格问答中的持续 harness 学习，并强调更新应受限且可审计。

Method: 在静态检索与执行骨干 FiCo（Find-then-Compute）之上提出 VIGIL（Verifier-Informed Gated Improvement Loop）。问题内对多样化提示生成的 SQL 候选进行验证与修复；跨轮次仅用延迟标签更新契约校准器及查询/结果列选择器，基础模型、提示、检索器和记录的候选池均保持固定。采用无门控、双门控等全回放变体，并在更严格划分中测试仅准确率门控、仅校准器适配及 MiMoTable/外部任务案例。

Result: 提供金标准工作簿和期望类型时，无门控与双门控全回放变体在 79 个文档上达到 82.4% 和 82.0% 前向准确率，静态基线为 75.8%；双门控回溯增益 2.7 对 2.3 个百分点，前向增益 6.3 个百分点，95% t 区间为 5.6–6.9。严格划分排除 16 个文档和 380 个问题后，仅准确率门控将十个最终 harness 的平均留出准确率从 80.3% 升至 86.7%；仅校准器适配增益 5.9 个百分点，组合增益 6.4 个百分点。26 个门控批准更新中有 3 个降低留出准确率。

Conclusion: 结果支持有界、可审计的 harness 适配，同时表明回放缓冲区不退化并不保证留出不退化；有限回放门控可能无法泛化到其提升缓冲区之外。

中文摘要：企业智能体应当从延迟反馈中改进，而不允许每一次纠正都重写系统行为。我们研究语料库级电子表格问答中的持续 harness 学习。基于 FiCo（先查找后计算）——一个静态检索与执行骨干——我们提出 VIGIL（验证器信息引导的门控改进循环）。在一个问题内，VIGIL 验证并修复以多样化提示生成的结构化查询语言（SQL）候选。跨轮次，延迟标签仅更新契约校准器与查询/结果列选择器。在持续学习协议中，基础模型、提示、检索器和记录的候选池保持固定。在提供金标准工作簿和期望类型的情况下，无门控与双门控的全回放变体在 79 个文档上分别达到 82.4% 和 82.0% 的前向准确率，而静态基线为 75.8%。双门控具有更大的回溯增益（2.7 对 2.3 个百分点），其 6.3 个百分点的前向增益的 95% t 区间为 5.6–6.9。在一个单独且更严格的划分中，将 16 个文档和 380 个问题排除在拟合与在线提升之外，仅准确率门控将十个最终 harness 的平均留出准确率从 80.3% 提高到 86.7%。仅校准器适配获得 5.9 个百分点的增益，接近组合的 6.4 个百分点增益。然而，26 个经门控批准的更新中有 3 个相对于其现有版本降低了留出准确率，因此回放缓冲区不退化并不意味着留出不退化。MiMoTable 和外部任务案例研究测试任务内验证，并复用相同的“提升或保留”准则。总体而言，结果支持有界、可审计的 harness 适配，同时揭示了有限回放门控在何处无法泛化到其提升缓冲区之外。

Original abstract: Enterprise agents should improve from delayed feedback without allowing every correction to rewrite system behavior. We study continual harness learning for corpus-level spreadsheet question answering. Building on FiCo (Find-then-Compute), a static retrieval-and-execution backbone, we introduce VIGIL (Verifier-Informed Gated Improvement Loop). Within a question, VIGIL verifies and repairs diversely prompted Structured Query Language (SQL) candidates. Across episodes, delayed labels update only a contract calibrator and query/result-column selector. The base model, prompts, retriever, and recorded candidate pool remain fixed in the continual-learning protocol. With the gold workbook and expected type supplied, the ungated and dual-gated full-replay variants reach 82.4% and 82.0% forward accuracy over 79 documents, from a 75.8% static baseline. The dual gate has the larger retrospective gain (2.7 versus 2.3 points), and its 6.3-point forward gain has a 95% t-interval of 5.6-6.9. In a separate stricter split that excludes 16 documents and 380 questions from fitting and online promotion, the accuracy-only gate raises mean held-out accuracy across ten final harnesses from 80.3% to 86.7%. Calibrator-only adaptation gains 5.9 points, close to the combined 6.4-point gain. Yet three of 26 gate-approved updates reduce held-out accuracy relative to their incumbents, so replay-buffer non-regression does not imply held-out non-regression. MiMoTable and external-task case studies test within-task verification and reuse the same promote-or-retain discipline. Overall, the results support bounded, auditable harness adaptation while revealing where finite replay gates fail to generalize beyond their promotion buffers.

</details>


### [59] [LMBuild: Evaluating LLM Agents for Generating Buildable and Functional Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04292)
*Jiateng Liu,Rushi Wang,Cheng Qian,Xuejun Zhang,Sun Li,Jiayu Liu,Yifan Shen,Xu Cao,Jiarui Yao,Bingxuan Li,Ruhi Sarikaya,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 64 pages

Selection reason: LMBuild 为工具交互式 3D 结构生成 agent 基准，揭示功能可用性瓶颈与检索/自创组件差异，对 agent 工具使用与可执行性评测有参考价值。

TL;DR: LMBuild是一个评估LLM智能体生成可建造且功能性3D结构的基准，包含交互环境、CAD+Wikipedia增强数据集和四维评估框架；对30个系统的测试表明，功能可供性与物理可操作性仍是主要难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多关注几何质量，忽视物理可实现性；而生成优雅几何与生成可建造、可执行预期功能的物体本质不同。

Method: 提出LMBuild基准，将生成对象表示为含部件分解、连接、材料和序列的装配结构，并提供统一框架：交互式构建环境、基于CAD数据集和Wikipedia增强的精选基准、覆盖结构稳健性/功能可供性/设计质量/物理实现的评估框架。

Result: 评估30个系统发现：前沿闭源模型在健全性和对齐上不再是主要瓶颈，但功能可供性和物理可操作性仍具挑战；强模型更善于创建新组件，弱模型更依赖检索；提供功能规格可显著提升部件完整性、运动学和物理可操作性。

Conclusion: 生成真实世界结构需要更深入推理功能可供性、力学以及设计和创建新组件；LMBuild为衡量进展并推动相关智能体研究提供基础。

中文摘要：基于LLM的智能体正越来越有能力生成复杂3D结构，并有可能重塑物体在物理世界中的设计与实现方式。然而，生成优雅的几何形状与生成可被建造并能执行其预期功能的物体存在本质区别。现有评估主要关注几何质量，而忽视了物理可实现性。我们提出LMBuild，一个用于评估LLM智能体生成可建造且功能性结构的基准。LMBuild将生成对象表示为装配结构，包括部件分解、连接、材料和序列。为支持可复现评估，我们提供统一框架，包括：（1）交互式环境，智能体可借助工具检索、创建和放置组件以构建物体；（2）精心整理的基准，其重新利用已有CAD数据集并用来自维基百科的知识进行增强；（3）评估框架，涵盖结构稳健性、功能可供性、设计质量和物理实现。对30个系统的评估揭示若干有趣发现：（a）对于前沿闭源模型，健全性和对齐不再是主要瓶颈，而功能可供性和物理可操作性仍然更具挑战性；（b）更强的模型更有效地创建新组件，而较弱的模型倾向于依赖检索；（c）提供功能规格可显著提高部件完整性、运动学和物理可操作性。这些结果表明，生成真实世界结构需要更深入地推理功能可供性、力学，以及设计和创建新颖组件。我们期望LMBuild为衡量进展并激励面向能生成可建造且功能性结构的智能体的研究提供基础。

Original abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly capable of generating complex 3D structures, with the potential to reshape how objects are designed and realized in the physical world. Yet, producing elegant geometry is fundamentally different from producing objects that can be built and perform their intended functions. Existing evaluations largely focus on geometric quality while overlooking physical realizability. We introduce LMBuild, a benchmark for evaluating LLM agents on generating buildable and functional structures. LMBuild represents generated objects as assembled structures comprising part decompositions, joints, materials, and sequences. To support reproducible evaluation, we provide a unified framework consisting of: (1) an interactive environment in which agents can use tools to retrieve, create, and place components to construct objects; (2) a curated benchmark that repurposes established CAD datasets and augments them with knowledge from Wikipedia; and (3) a evaluation framework covering structural soundness, functional affordance, design quality, and physical realization. Evaluations across 30 systems reveal several intriguing findings: (a) Soundness and alignment are no longer the primary bottlenecks for frontier closed-source models, while functional affordance and physical operability remain substantially more challenging; (b) stronger models more effectively create new components, whereas weaker models tend to rely on retrieval; and (c) providing functional specifications substantially improves part completeness, kinematics, and physical operability. These results show that generating real-world structures requires deeper reasoning about functional affordances, mechanics, and designing and creating novel components. We expect LMBuild to provide a foundation for measuring progress and incentivizing research toward agents that generate buildable and functional structures.

</details>


### [60] [Functionally Equivalent or Not? Graph-Grounded Differential Surrogate Execution for Code Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04371)
*Amit Kachroo,Like Hui,Haitao Mao,Yuhao Zhang,Nguyen Vo*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 14 pages, 5 figures, 4 tables, accepted by NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on AI for Verifiable Coding

Selection reason: FEAgent 以程序图证据+差分代理执行做代码等价性判定，含证据账本与不确定性裁决机制，可直接迁移到代码 agent 的补丁/生成结果验证与审计。

TL;DR: FEAgent通过程序图证据与差分代理执行，选择性地判定程序等价性，并在证据不足时给出UNCLEAR；在EquiBench和SWE-bench Verified上发现基准标签与测试通过补丁中的大量行为分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序功能等价判定是代码现代化、补丁验证、重构与代码生成评估的核心，但测试、文本相似性和无约束LLM判断均不完整，且依赖不可执行环境时直接执行不可行。

Method: FEAgent先对齐公共接口与行为相关图锚点，并对调用流、控制流、数据流、类型、导入和效应关系进行有界查询；分支感知生成器提出区分性输入，两个盲化LLM代理分别预测源与目标可观测值，所有主张与预测分歧记入证据账本；确定性协调器输出EQUIVALENT、INEQUIVALENT或UNCLEAR。

Result: 在EquiBench的1,200对中，216对（18.0%）被直接执行确认与已发布标签分歧；在SWE-bench Verified的331个通过测试的智能体补丁中，94个（28.4%）与参考补丁分歧。

Conclusion: FEAgent可作为测试与形式验证之间的审计层，提供可审查证据并显式表达不确定性，但不声称证明等价性。

中文摘要：确定两个程序是否功能等价，对于代码现代化、补丁验证、重构和代码生成评估至关重要。然而，常用信号并不完整：测试仅覆盖有限输入，文本相似性混淆了实现与行为，未受约束的LLM判断难以审计。当程序依赖过时的、有许可限制的、不可用的或不安全的环境时，直接执行通常不可能。我们提出FEAgent，一种选择性等价评估智能体，它将带类型的程序图证据与差分代理执行相结合。FEAgent首先对齐公共接口和与行为相关的图锚点，然后针对调用流、控制流、数据流、类型、导入和效应关系发出有界查询。接下来，一个分支感知的生成器智能体提出具有区分性的输入，两个盲化的LLM代理独立预测源和目标可观测值。每个主张和预测到的分歧都记录在证据账本中。随后，一个确定性协调器返回EQUIVALENT、INEQUIVALENT或UNCLEAR，而不是在路径未覆盖或证据冲突时强行给出判定。我们在函数级等价性和仓库级缺陷补丁上评估FEAgent，其中现有oracle是基准标签或通过的测试套件。与oracle的每个分歧都由直接执行进行裁定，从而揭示基准标签中的错误以及仅凭单元测试评分所遗漏的行为分歧。在EquiBench上，执行确认FEAgent在1,200个评估对中的216个（18.0%）与已发布标签存在分歧；在SWE-bench Verified上，331个通过测试的智能体补丁中有94个（28.4%）与参考补丁不同。因此，FEAgent充当测试与形式验证之间的审计层，使其证据可审查、不确定性显式，而不声称等价性证明。

Original abstract: Determining whether two programs are functionally equivalent is central to code modernization, patch validation, refactoring, and code-generation evaluation. Yet the usual signals are incomplete: tests cover only finite inputs, textual similarity confuses implementation with behavior, and unconstrained LLM judgments are difficult to audit. Direct execution is often impossible when a program depends on an obsolete, licensed, unavailable, or unsafe environment. We introduce FEAgent, a selective equivalence assessor agent that combines typed program-graph evidence with differential surrogate execution. FEAgent first aligns public interfaces and behaviorally relevant graph anchors, then issues bounded queries over call-flow, control-flow, data-flow, type, import, and effect relations. Next, a branch-aware generator agent proposes discriminating inputs, and two blinded LLM surrogates independently predict source and target observables. Every claim and predicted divergence is recorded in an evidence ledger. A deterministic reconciler then returns EQUIVALENT, INEQUIVALENT, or UNCLEAR rather than forcing a verdict when paths are uncovered or evidence conflicts. We evaluate FEAgent on function-level equivalence and repository-level bug patches, where the existing oracle is a benchmark label or a passing test suite. Every disagreement with that oracle is adjudicated by direct execution, revealing errors in benchmark labels and behavioral divergences missed by unit-test-only scoring. On EquiBench, execution confirms FEAgent's disagreements with published labels on 216 of 1,200 evaluated pairs (18.0%); on SWE-bench Verified, 94 of 331 test-passing agent patches (28.4%) diverge from the reference patch. FEAgent thus serves as an audit layer between testing and formal verification, keeping its evidence reviewable and its uncertainty explicit without claiming a proof of equivalence.

</details>


### [61] [Do Tool Calls Execute as Intended? Measuring and Repairing Intent-Execution Correspondence in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04375)
*Boyang Yang,Zhenhao Li,Ziyao Yang,Kanghui Jia,Xin Yin,Mingmou Liu,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出 intent-execution correspondence 与 IntAct，系统测量并修复工具调用在 harness 各跳被篡改的问题，构建 IEC-Bench，对 coding agent 工具链可靠性与重试/验证机制高度可迁移。

TL;DR: LLM智能体工具调用可能被中间环节静默改变，造成错误执行与重试成本；论文定义IEC，提出逐环节观测协议与IntAct，并构建IEC-Bench。实验显示生产环境中Claude Code Bash改变12.0%相关调用，10个框架均会改变调用；IntAct可恢复79.2%的改变调用失败，强调逐环节设计与测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准和失败分析只读取调用及其结果，忽略中间环节接收到的内容，无法发现调用被静默改变；基于轨迹的判断还会将多数失败错误归因于LLM，而非执行路径。

Method: 定义意图-执行一致性（IEC）；协议在不执行调用的情况下观察各环节接收内容，并用接收方自己的解析器定位首个改变调用的环节；IntAct以该环节无法改变的形式交付调用，或拒绝调用；基于真实使用中观察到的改变构建IEC-Bench，包含4个广泛使用执行框架下依赖调用组成的调用链。

Result: 在47,828次生产shell调用中，Claude Code的Bash工具改变12.0%携带代码、转义序列或长文本的调用；80.7%反斜杠被改变的调用在无错误报告下执行错误动作；10个被测执行框架均会改变调用；轨迹判断将95.1%生产失败归因于LLM，但路径造成超过一半；IEC-Bench上路径使每通过任务token成本增至2.4倍（最高12.3倍）；55.1%失败在首个环节修复后才出现；IntAct恢复79.2%的改变调用失败。

Conclusion: 执行框架应按环节设计和测试，确保正确调用按预期执行，或被拒绝，从而避免中间环节静默改变调用带来的错误与成本。

中文摘要：基于大语言模型（LLM）构建的智能体通过工具调用来构建和运行软件。一次调用会经过多个环节才能到达其程序，而任一环节都可能在未被告知的情况下改变该调用。当被改变的调用失败时，智能体会重试一次正确的调用，这会给用户带来时间和金钱成本。基准测试和失败分析看不到这种改变，因为它们读取的是调用及其结果，而不是某个环节接收到的内容。我们将意图-执行一致性（IEC）定义为：在工具契约下，已执行的动作与所发出调用所表示的动作相匹配这一性质。我们的协议在不执行调用的情况下观察每个环节接收到的内容，并通过接收方自己的解析器指出第一个改变该调用的环节。随后，IntAct 以该环节无法改变的形式交付调用，或拒绝该调用。我们根据真实使用中观察到的改变构建了 IEC-Bench，其中包含在 4 个广泛使用的执行框架（harness）的执行路径下由依赖调用组成的调用链。在生产会话中的 47,828 次 shell 调用里，Claude Code 的 Bash 工具改变了 12.0% 的携带代码、转义序列或长文本的调用。对于反斜杠被改变的调用，80.7% 的情况下错误动作会在没有任何报告错误的情况下运行。所有 10 个被测执行框架都会改变调用。基于轨迹的判断将 95.1% 的生产失败归因于 LLM，尽管其中超过一半是由路径造成的。在 IEC-Bench 上，该路径使每个通过任务的 token 成本增至 2.4 倍（最高 12.3 倍）。改变调用的环节也会隐藏其后的改变，因此某条路径上 55.1% 的失败只有在该路径的第一个环节被修复后才会出现。IntAct 已部署在一个商业产品中，可恢复 79.2% 的发生调用改变的失败。因此，执行框架应按环节设计和测试，以确保正确的调用按预期执行，或被拒绝。

Original abstract: Agents built on large language models (LLMs) build and run software through tool calls. A call reaches its program through several hops, and any hop can change the call without notice. When the changed call fails, the agent retries a correct call, which costs users time and money. Benchmarks and failure analyses do not see the change, because they read the call and its result but not what a hop received. We define intent-execution correspondence (IEC) as the property that the executed action matches the action the emitted call denotes under the tool contract. Our protocol observes what each hop received without executing the call, and names the first hop that changed it by the receiver's own parser. IntAct then delivers the call in a form that this hop cannot alter, or refuses the call. We build IEC-Bench from the changes observed in real-world use, with chains of dependent calls under the execution paths of 4 widely-used harnesses.
  In 47,828 shell calls within production sessions, Claude Code's Bash tool changes 12.0% of the calls that carry code, escape sequences, or long text. For 80.7% of the calls whose backslashes are changed, the wrong action runs without any reported error. All 10 measured harnesses change a call. Trajectory-based judgment attributes 95.1% of the production failures to the LLM, although the path caused more than half of them. On IEC-Bench, the path raises the token cost per passed task 2.4 times (up to 12.3 times). A hop that changes a call also hides the changes after it, so 55.1% of the failures on one path appear only after its first hop is repaired. IntAct, deployed in a commercial product, recovers 79.2% of the failures with a changed call. Harnesses should therefore be designed and tested hop-by-hop to ensure a correct call executes as intended or is refused.

</details>


### [62] [What Does a Harness Buy? Tokens, Mostly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04433)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 22 pages, 7 figures. Code and data: https://github.com/YangzeLiu/what-does-a-harness-buy

Selection reason: 定量剖析 harness 对 SWE-bench 成绩与 token 成本的真实影响并给出重跑校准方法，对 agent 评测机制与成本优化有直接参考价值。

TL;DR: 在固定模型时，生产级编码外壳对 SWE-bench Verified 分数的影响大多被运行噪声淹没：换外壳与重跑同外壳都翻转约 13% 任务，唯一显著效应是 OpenCode 最多落后 9 个百分点；外壳可靠决定的是成本，每任务成本最多差 3 倍，且基准任务量常不足以分辨所宣称的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化编码智能体外壳本身对基准成绩的贡献：当模型不变时，系统提示、工具集和上下文管理带来的分数变化、任务级翻转与成本差异到底有多大，以及常用基准规模能否分辨厂商宣称的通过率提升。

Method: 在 SWE-bench Verified 上，将五个模型分别接入三个生产级外壳 Claude Code、mini-SWE-agent 和 OpenCode，并重复相同配置以校准运行噪声；在 447 个任务全集和 45 个任务困难子集上比较分数、任务翻转、输出截断影响和每任务成本；进一步分析系统提示与工具模式前言、步数、每步增长、每次调用工具输出以及缓存输入价格对成本的影响。

Result: 447 任务、两个模型下，Claude Code 与 mini-SWE-agent 在 5 个百分点内等价。45 任务困难子集、五个模型下，换外壳与重跑同外壳均翻转 13% 任务，且胜出任务不一致。唯一超过噪声的外壳效应是损失：OpenCode 在大任务池最多落后 9 个百分点，某模型上该差距一半来自输出上限截断。相同模型、任务和价目表下，每任务成本跨外壳最多差 3 倍；差距由首次调用时每步发送的系统提示与工具模式前言设定并按步数缩放；每步增长和每次调用工具输出差异小得多；缓存输入价格缩放账单但不改变排序。重跑数据显示，在观察到的差异率下，45 任务只有一半概率捕捉 13 个百分点差距，80% 概率捕捉不到差距；447 任务可分辨 5 个百分点，仍比许多外壳改动宣称的增益更粗。

Conclusion: 外壳对分数的稳定影响远小于常被宣传的水平，任务级结果受运行噪声主导；其最可靠、可复现的影响体现在成本结构上。外壳比较需要足够多任务才能分辨小幅增益，成本比较则应在固定模型、任务和价目表下考察系统提示与工具模式前言。

中文摘要：编码智能体是一个被外壳（harness）包裹的语言模型：系统提示、工具集以及上下文管理共同将聊天模型转变为能够在代码仓库中工作的东西。生产级外壳每天发布新版本，厂商宣传外壳改动带来的通过率提升，排行榜随意混合不同的外壳。但很少被测量的是：当模型固定不变时，外壳本身能在多大程度上推动分数变化。我们让五个模型在三个生产级外壳——Claude Code、mini-SWE-agent 和 OpenCode——上运行 SWE-bench Verified，并重复运行相同配置，以校准当除运行本身外没有任何变化时分数会波动多少。在 447 个任务和我们在此运行的两个模型上，Claude Code 和 mini-SWE-agent——最重与最轻的外壳——在五个百分点以内等价。在 45 个任务的困难子集和五个模型上，更换外壳所翻转的任务数量与重跑同一外壳一样多，两种情况均为 13%，而且一个外壳在某次运行中胜出的任务，并不是它在下一次运行中胜出的任务。唯一超过噪声的外壳效应是一种损失而非收益：OpenCode 在大任务池上最多落后 9 个百分点，而在一个模型上，这一差距的一半存在于其输出上限被截断的运行中。外壳真正决定的是账单。在相同模型、相同任务和同一价目表下，不同外壳的每任务成本最多相差 3 倍。这一差距在第一次调用时由每个外壳每一步都发送的系统提示和工具模式（tool schemas）前言所设定，并按步数缩放；每步增长和每次调用的工具输出差异要小得多。提供商对缓存输入的价格会缩放账单，但不会改变其排序。重复运行数据还给出了外壳比较所需的分辨率：在我们观察到的差异率下，45 个任务只有一半的概率能捕捉到 13 个百分点的差距，而有 80% 的概率捕捉不到差距；447 个任务能分辨 5 个百分点，但仍比许多外壳改动所宣称的增益更粗。

Original abstract: A coding agent is a language model wrapped in a harness: the system prompt, the tool set, and the context management that turn a chat model into something that can work inside a repository. Production harnesses ship releases daily, vendors advertise pass-rate gains from harness changes, and leaderboards mix harnesses freely. What is rarely measured is how much the harness itself moves the score when the model is held fixed. We run five models through three production harnesses, Claude Code, mini-SWE-agent, and OpenCode, on SWE-bench Verified, and rerun the same configurations to calibrate how much a score moves when nothing changes but the run. On 447 tasks and the two models we ran there, Claude Code and mini-SWE-agent, the heaviest and the lightest harness, are equivalent within five points. On a 45-task hard subset and five models, swapping the harness flips as many tasks as rerunning the same harness, 13% in both cases, and the tasks a harness wins in one run are not the tasks it wins in the next. The one harness effect that clears the noise is a loss, not a gain: OpenCode trails by up to 9 points on the large pool, and on one model half of that gap sits in runs its output cap cut short. What the harness does decide is the bill. With the same model, the same tasks, and one price list, cost per task differs by up to 3x across harnesses. The gap is set at the first call, by the preamble of system prompt and tool schemas each harness sends with every step, and scaled by the number of steps; per-step growth and per-call tool output differ far less. The provider's price for cached input scales the bill and does not reorder it. The rerun data also give the resolution a harness comparison needs: at the discordance we observe, 45 tasks catch a 13-point gap only half the time and no gap with 80% power, and 447 tasks resolve 5 points, still coarser than the gains many harness changes claim.

</details>


### [63] [ShadowMiner v1 - An Experience Report on Implementing and Measuring a Problem-and-Hypothesis Discovery Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04339)
*Jinhyuk Choi*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多阶段研究问题与假设发现流水线，含知识图谱缺口定位与生成结果验证，其验证式工作流对agent设计有参考价值。

TL;DR: ShadowMiner v1 通过九阶段流水线，将 AI 论文构建为知识图谱并识别研究盲点，以此提示 LLM 生成假设，再对假设进行覆盖、质量和事实来源验证；本报告不提出新方法，而是总结实现与应用先前工作的经验并评估各组件贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动从 AI 论文中发现研究问题并生成假设，并复用先前工作想法，测量各想法是否真正有贡献。

Method: ShadowMiner v1 采用九阶段流水线，将文档结构化为知识图谱，查找图中的结构性盲点（图间隙），将图间隙加入 LLM 生成提示；生成的每个假设均经过验证：检查是否已被现有研究覆盖、评分质量、核对事实是否准确来自来源。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验数值；说明系统实现并应用了先前工作的想法，并测量了每个想法是否实际有贡献。每个假设都会经过覆盖、质量和事实来源验证。

Conclusion: 本报告不提出新的生成或评估技术，而是分享实现和应用先前工作想法的经验，并评估各组件贡献。

中文摘要：ShadowMiner v1 是一个能够从 AI 论文中自动发现研究问题并生成假设的系统。它是一个九阶段流水线。它将文档组织成知识图谱，并在其中寻找图间隙——研究中的结构性盲点。这些图间隙被纳入大语言模型生成提示中。随后，每个生成的假设都会通过以下方式进行验证：检查它是否已被现有研究覆盖、对其质量进行评分，并检查它所依赖的事实是否准确地来自其来源。本报告不提出新的生成或评估技术。它描述了我们在实现和应用先前工作想法方面的经验，并衡量每个想法是否真正有贡献。

Original abstract: ShadowMiner v1 is a system that automatically discovers research problems and generates hypotheses from AI papers. It is a nine-stage pipeline. It structures documents into a knowledge graph and finds graph gaps in it - structural blind spots in research. These graph gaps are included in the LLM generation prompt. Each generated hypothesis is then verified by checking whether it is already covered by existing research, scoring its quality, and checking that the facts it relies on are accurately drawn from its sources. This report does not propose a new generation or evaluation technique. It describes our experience of implementing and applying ideas from prior work, and measuring whether each one actually contributed.

</details>


### [64] [AgentPersonaBench: Benchmarking Persona-Driven User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04379)
*Jintao Huang,Yifan Wang,Hongyu Shen,Yi Daniel Lu,Shirley Huang,Minsik Oh,Yewen Wang,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Zhen Xu,Yilan Fan,Zichen Yuan,Ahsan Bilal,Zibu Wei,Sankalp Jajee,Henry Gagnier,Saksham Kapoor,Jicheng Wang,Qianfeng Wen,Yixuan He,Steven Dillmann,Jiashu He,Yucheng Lu,Linqiang Guo,Danyang Zhang,Shi Bo,Raunak Mondal,Haixiang Tang,Weihang Xiao,Allen Nie,Jing Tang,Yueying Li,Yifan Simon Liu,Jianheng Hou,Dianzhuo Wang,Qianyu Zhu,Zhixu Silvia Tao,Zhejian Peng,Zihan Wang,Ishan Gupta,Jinxuan Fan,Wanting Jiang,Shushu Liang,Chenxi Qiu,Yijun Wang,Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向人格驱动用户模拟的agent行为保真度基准，涉及多交互界面下的agent评测方法，可迁移到agent评估。

TL;DR: 提出 AgentPersonaBench (APB)，在调查、聊天、网页和应用四类交互界面中以可观察行为评估人格条件化是否忠实引导智能体行为；评测 20 个前沿模型分支后发现高保真用户模拟已可实现，但跨模态、多属性和模型家族间存在明显行为边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估对话风格或自我报告，而非人格条件化是否真实、忠实地引导下游智能体行为，因此需要面向行为保真度的评测。

Method: APB 一次评估一个特质，将目标特质嵌入完整合成画像中，不明确命名特质或披露测试，并在调查、聊天、网页和桌面应用四个现实性递增的交互界面上，依据可观察行为严格验证真实遵循情况；基准含 2,460 个任务、867 个特质，经自动审计和专家评审验证。

Result: 对 20 个前沿模型分支的评测显示，领先模型在无提示条件下最高达到 84.7% 的完全通过遵循率；但仅 37.9–64.3% 能通过全部四个交互界面，多属性要求会降低保留率，竞争模型家族之间行为差异显著。

Conclusion: 高保真用户模拟已经可以实现，但 APB 揭示了清晰的行为边界，表明人格条件化在跨模态、多属性及不同模型家族中仍存在显著局限。

中文摘要：我们介绍了 AgentPersonaBench (APB)，一个用于评估人格条件化是否忠实引导下游智能体行为的基准。尽管语言模型越来越多地被部署用于人格驱动的用户模拟，现有基准主要评估对话风格或自我报告，而非真实的行为保真度。APB 一次评估一个特质的潜在人格遵循情况，将每个目标特质嵌入一个完整的合成画像中，而不明确命名该特质或披露测试。真实遵循情况严格通过四个交互界面上可观察的行为来验证，这些界面的现实性递增：调查、聊天、网页（交互式网页环境）和应用（桌面软件环境）。APB 包含 2,460 个任务，涵盖 867 个特质，并通过自动审计和专家评审进行验证。我们对 20 个前沿模型分支的评估表明，高保真用户模拟已经可以实现：领先模型在无提示条件下达到高达 84.7% 的完全通过遵循率。同时，APB 识别出清晰的行为边界：遵循率在不同交互模态间下降（仅 37.9–64.3% 通过全部四个界面），多属性要求会降低保留率，并且相互竞争的模型家族表现出显著的行为差异。

Original abstract: We introduce AgentPersonaBench (APB), a benchmark evaluating whether persona conditioning faithfully steers downstream agent behavior. While language models are increasingly deployed for persona-driven user simulation, existing benchmarks primarily evaluate conversational styling or self-reports rather than authentic behavioral fidelity. APB evaluates latent persona adherence one trait at a time, embedding each target trait within a complete synthetic profile without explicitly naming the trait or disclosing the test. Ground-truth adherence is verified strictly from observable actions across four interaction surfaces of increasing realism: survey, chat, web (interactive web environments), and app (desktop software environments). APB comprises 2,460 tasks spanning 867 traits, verified through automated audits and expert review. Our evaluation of 20 frontier model arms demonstrates that high-fidelity user simulation is already attainable: leading models achieve up to 84.7% full-pass adherence under unprompted conditions. At the same time, APB identifies clear behavioral boundaries: adherence drops across interaction modalities (only 37.9-64.3% pass all four surfaces), multi-attribute demands degrade retention, and competing model families exhibit pronounced behavioral divergence.

</details>


### [65] [TrustMed-RL: Long-Horizon Reinforcement Learning for Evidence-Grounded Clinical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04387)
*Wenxin Zhan,Yizheng Jiao,Haifeng Song,Shuai Xu,Chencheng Pan,Jiayi Feng,Anjie Xie*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 长程RL训练带工具与证据获取的临床诊断策略，其长程行动空间与奖励设计对agent长程训练有借鉴意义。

TL;DR: TrustMed-RL 是面向长时程、以证据为基础诊断的 8B 视觉--语言模型，基于 PubMed 罕见病病例与超过 24,000 张人工标注图像面板，通过状态依赖动作和临床适配 GiGPO 训练，在诊断准确率、证据获取和轨迹可信度上优于多个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学语言模型可能在不完整检查和无支持推理下给出正确诊断，因此需要支持长时程、以证据为基础的诊断方法。

Method: 构建 TrustMed-RL：基于 PubMed 罕见病病例和超过 24,000 张人工标注图像面板；通过状态依赖动作整合问诊、体格检查、检验、专科会诊和文献检索；采用 8B 视觉--语言策略，并使用临床适配的 GiGPO 与覆盖度调整的诊断奖励进行训练。

Result: 在 2,500 个评估病例上达到 37.1% 诊断准确率，优于所有评估的开放权重基线，并较监督微调提升 12.4 个百分点；当成功还要求获取至少 50% 支持性检验证据时达到 32.5%，超过 GPT-4o 6.8 个百分点；在 MTMedDialog 上超过所有评估基线，在 AgentClinic 上超过多个更大的 27--32B 模型；医生评审 200 条诊断上被接受的测试集轨迹时，83.0% 获得 5 分制中 4--5 分的证据基础评分。

Conclusion: 医生的评估表明，这些诊断轨迹可信，并与人类诊断推理一致，支持 TrustMed-RL 用于长时程、以证据为基础的诊断。

中文摘要：医学语言模型即使在不完整检查和缺乏支持推理的情况下，也能产生正确诊断。为支持长时程、以证据为基础的诊断，我们提出了 TrustMed-RL。它基于 PubMed 罕见病病例和超过 24,000 张人工标注的图像面板构建，通过状态依赖动作整合问诊、体格检查、检验、专科会诊和文献检索。我们的 8B 视觉--语言策略使用临床适配的 GiGPO 和覆盖度调整的诊断奖励进行训练，在 2,500 个评估病例上达到 37.1% 的诊断准确率，优于所有评估的开放权重基线，并较监督微调提升 12.4 个百分点。当成功还要求获取至少 50% 的支持性检验证据时，TrustMed-RL 达到 32.5%，超过 GPT-4o 6.8 个百分点。此外，它在 MTMedDialog 上超过所有评估基线，并在 AgentClinic 上超过多个更大的 27--32B 模型。在医生对 200 条诊断上被接受的测试集轨迹的评审中，83.0% 获得 5 分制中 4--5 分的证据基础评分。医生的评估表明，这些诊断轨迹是可信的，并与人类诊断推理一致。

Original abstract: Medical language models can produce correct diagnoses despite incomplete investigations and unsupported reasoning. To support long-horizon, evidence-grounded diagnosis, we introduce \textbf{TrustMed-RL}. Built from PubMed rare-disease cases and over 24,000 manually annotated image panels, it integrates interviews, examinations, testing, specialist consultation, and literature search through state-dependent actions. Our 8B vision--language policy, trained with clinically adapted GiGPO and coverage-adjusted diagnostic rewards, achieves 37.1\% diagnostic accuracy on 2,500 evaluation cases, outperforming all evaluated open-weight baselines and improving over supervised fine-tuning by 12.4 percentage points.. When success additionally requires acquiring at least 50\% of supporting test evidence, TrustMed-RL achieves 32.5\%, exceeding GPT-4o by 6.8 percentage points. Furthermore, it surpasses all evaluated baselines on MTMedDialog and multiple larger 27--32B models on AgentClinic. In physician review of 200 diagnostically accepted test-set trajectories, 83.0\% receive evidential-grounding scores of 4--5 out of 5. Physicians' assessments suggest that these diagnostic trajectories are trustworthy and aligned with human diagnostic reasoning.

</details>


### [66] [Asking Earns Nothing: Scoring the Decision to Act in BFCL Multi-Turn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04429)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 19 pages, 2 figures. Code and data: https://github.com/YangzeLiu/asking-earns-nothing

Selection reason: 在BFCL多轮工具调用中评分agent该问还是该做的决策，直接针对工具调用agent的评测与决策行为，价值较高。

TL;DR: BFCL多轮中应询问轮次未被评分，导致询问无收益、猜测无代价；作者构建223对应询问/应行动配对并直接评分决策，发现官方分数更多受推动行动影响而非决策质量；发布配对、轮次级评分器和31个坏项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在信息不足时应询问，但BFCL多轮虽有两个类别围绕应询问轮次，其评分器却忽略该轮次，使询问无收益、猜测无代价，因此需要检验官方分数是否真正衡量该决策。

Method: 将基础项移除某轮一条信息构造应询问项，使同一请求在同一轮次索引出现完整/不完整两次；按对评分模型在该轮是否尝试改变世界的调用，直接读取存储轨迹，无LLM裁判；总是行动/总是询问均得50；比较七个模型，并做提示干预。

Result: 223对中，gpt-5.4完整轮次尝试调用83.4%、不完整轮次克制78.0%，决策准确率80.7%为七模型最高，但官方分数排第六，第一名在此居中；提示其不要询问使其两侧都倾向行动，决策准确率无显著变化，官方分数在应询问类别和基础孪生项上升13.5–23.5分；提示gemma-4-31B-it先询问使决策提高4.5分但官方分数无提升。

Conclusion: 官方分数随推动行动而变化，而非随应否询问的决策质量变化；基准评分遗漏了关键决策，需要改用轮次级决策评分。作者发布223对样本、无需API密钥的轮次级评分器及31个经人工验证的坏项。

中文摘要：一个缺少所需信息的智能体应当询问而不是行动，智能体排行榜的任务定义也如此规定。BFCL多轮将其四个类别中的两个建立在一个模型本应询问的轮次上，而其评分器从不查看该轮次：此处的黄金轨迹为空，检查器跳过它，脚本化用户无法回答，因此询问没有任何收益，猜测在该轮次没有代价，而询问两次会导致该项失败。该基准也包含了针对该决策的对照实验。一个应询问项是一个基础项，其中从某一轮次移除了一条信息，因此同一请求在同一轮次索引处出现两次，一次完整、一次不完整：在第一次上，模型应进行改变世界的调用；在第二次上，它应询问。我们为每一对评分一个决策，即模型在该轮次是否尝试了改变世界的调用，直接从存储的轨迹中读取，不使用LLM裁判；总是行动和总是询问都得分50。在提出该决策的223对样本上，gpt-5.4在83.4%的完整轮次上尝试调用，并在78.0%的不完整轮次上保持不行动，这是七个模型中最佳的决策准确率，为80.7%；在同一批项目上，官方分数将其排第六，并把一个在此处居于中游的模型排在第一。添加一行告诉gpt-5.4不要询问，会把它推向在配对的两侧都行动，因此其决策准确率没有可检测的变化，而其在两个应询问类别以及基础孪生项上的官方分数上升了13.5至23.5分；相反的一行，告诉gemma-4-31B-it先询问，使其决策提高了4.5分，并且没有获得官方分数提升。分数随朝向行动的推动而变化，而不是随决策而变化。我们发布了这些配对、一个可在任何BFCL输出目录上运行且无需API密钥的轮次级评分器，以及31个经人工验证的坏项。

Original abstract: An agent that lacks the information it needs should ask rather than act, and the task definitions of agent leaderboards say so. BFCL multi-turn builds two of its four categories around a turn on which the model is supposed to ask, and its scorer never looks at that turn: the gold trajectory there is empty, the checker skips it, and the scripted user cannot answer, so asking earns nothing, guessing costs nothing on that turn, and asking twice loses the item. The benchmark also contains the control experiment for that decision. A should-ask item is a base item with one piece of information removed from one turn, so the same request appears twice at the same turn index, once complete and once not: on the first the model should make the call that changes the world, on the second it should ask. We score one decision per pair, whether the model attempted a world-changing call on that turn, read off the stored trajectories with no LLM judge; acting always and asking always both score 50. On the 223 pairs that pose this decision, gpt-5.4 attempts the call on 83.4% of the complete turns and holds back on 78.0% of the incomplete ones, the best decision accuracy of seven models at 80.7%; on the same items the official score ranks it sixth and puts first a model that lands in the middle here. One added line telling gpt-5.4 not to ask pushes it toward acting on both sides of the pair, so its decision accuracy shows no detectable change, while its official score rises by 13.5 to 23.5 points on the two should-ask categories and on the base twins; the opposite line, telling gemma-4-31B-it to ask first, improves its decision by 4.5 points and gains no official score. The score moves with the push toward action, not with the decision. We release the pairs, a turn-level scorer that runs on any BFCL output directory without an API key, and 31 manually verified bad items.

</details>


### [67] [CRAFT: An Agentic Spreadsheet Form Filling System with Template Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04437)
*Leyao Gu,Yingjie Xiong,Zirui Tang,Jiangtao Zhou,Yeye He,Chunwei Liu,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 模板感知的表格填写agent，含反思验证、错误定位与约束式局部修复，其反思-验证-修复闭环可迁移到代码agent。

TL;DR: CRAFT 是一个模板感知的智能体框架，通过将反思式验证与受约束的局部修复结合，提升电子表格表单填写准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格表单填写需整合外部证据、精确定位单元格并保持不规则模板结构，早期编辑错误会覆盖标签或错位字段，影响后续决策。

Method: 提出 CRAFT，将反思式验证连接至受约束局部修复；通过 RASG 提议可写单元格，并用标签-槽位提示与受保护区域约束编辑；必要时恢复模板并重新定位可写槽位。

Result: 构建 FormFillBench，含 327 个表单，覆盖 Instruction-Only 与 Multi-File 赛道；相比最强基线，配对准确率分别提升 8.51 和 23.38 个百分点；组件移除实验和第二个主干模型验证了关键模块与相对优势。

Conclusion: CRAFT 有效提升表单填写性能，组件有效，且在不同主干模型上保持优势；代码与基准已开源。

中文摘要：电子表格表单填写要求智能体整合外部证据、将值定位到精确单元格，并保持不规则模板结构。早期编辑中的错误可能会覆盖标签或使字段错位，从而破坏后续决策。我们提出 CRAFT，一个模板感知的智能体框架，将反思式验证与受约束的局部修复相连接。CRAFT 不把反思视为重新生成工作簿的自由形式请求，而是将检测到的错误定位到电子表格区域，在必要时恢复受损的模板状态，并在后续编辑前重新定位可能的可写槽位。矩形感知槽位定位器（RASG）提出可写单元格，而标签-槽位提示和受保护区域约束后续编辑。我们提出 FormFillBench，包含 327 个表单，横跨仅指令（Instruction-Only）和多文件（Multi-File）两个赛道。与最强基线相比，CRAFT 在这些赛道上分别将配对准确率提高了 8.51 和 23.38 个百分点。组件移除实验支持流水线中的结构化裁定和槽位重新定位，并且该框架在第二个主干模型上评估的方法中仍保持相对优势。代码和基准 FormFillBench 可在 https://github.com/Glllllly/CRAFT 获取。

Original abstract: Spreadsheet form filling requires agents to consolidate external evidence, ground values to precise cells, and preserve irregular template structure. Errors in early edits can overwrite labels or misalign fields, undermining later decisions. We propose CRAFT, a template-aware agent framework that connects reflective validation to constrained local repair. Instead of treating reflection as a free-form request to regenerate the workbook, CRAFT grounds detected errors to spreadsheet regions, restores corrupted template state when necessary, and re-grounds plausible writable slots before subsequent edits. A Rectangle-Aware Slot Grounder (RASG) proposes writable cells, while label-slot hints and protected regions constrain subsequent edits. We introduce FormFillBench, with 327 forms across Instruction-Only and Multi-File tracks. Compared with the strongest baselines, CRAFT improves pair accuracy by 8.51 and 23.38 percentage points on these tracks, respectively. Component-removal experiments support structural adjudication and slot re-grounding within the pipeline, and the framework retains its relative advantage among the methods evaluated with a second backbone. The code and benchmark FormFillBench are available at https://github.com/Glllllly/CRAFT.

</details>


### [68] [Trinity: Self-Evolving Vision-Language Models with a Self-Verifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04469)
*Youngwan Lee,Yong-Ju Lee,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: NeurIPS'26 Workshop on Agentic AI for Biological Discovery (AgenticLS)

Selection reason: 单模型充当提问者/求解者/自验证者的自演化机制，自验证器与自博弈课程对agent自我改进有迁移价值。

TL;DR: Trinity 让同一个 VLM 分别担任 Questioner、Solver 和基于策略 EMA 的自验证器，仅用无标注图像自演进训练，在数学视觉推理及含生物内容的科学基准上提升 Qwen3-VL-8B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进 VLM 依赖 Questioner-Solver 并用采样答案一致性奖励，但一致性不能保证问题源于图像、参考答案正确，也可能出现自信的多数一致错误，因此需要无需标签和外部评判器的可靠验证。

Method: Trinity 使用单个 VLM 扮演三种角色：Questioner 生成问题，Solver 作答，Verifier 为策略自身的 EMA 自验证器。Verifier 先筛查生成问题是否具备图像根据且答案正确，通过后才作为监督；再依据图像给 Solver 推理打分；并在参考答案与强 Solver 共识冲突时裁决，若共识正确则修正参考答案并惩罚 Questioner。训练仅使用图像。

Result: 在 Qwen3-VL-8B 上，Trinity 提升数学视觉推理与含生物学内容的科学基准表现，例如 SciVQR 生物学划分 +8.6 分、MathVerse +12.8 分；奖励动态符合健康自我对弈课程的特征。

Conclusion: 自演进多模态智能体可仅从未标注科学图像中增强科学推理能力，并由模型自身充当验证器。

中文摘要：自演进的视觉语言模型（VLM）是一种自我改进形式，其中模型从未标注图像生成自己的训练数据；它是一条有前景的路径，通向能够以无监督方式扩展其推理能力的智能体，而无需依赖不断增大的标注预算。现有方法将提出问题的问题生成器（Questioner）与回答问题的求解器（Solver）配对，但主要通过采样答案之间的一致性来奖励这两个角色。一致性是真理的弱代理：它无法判断问题是否以图像为根据、所提出的参考答案是否正确，或者一个自信的多数是否以相同方式出错。我们提出 Trinity，其中一个 VLM 扮演三个角色：Questioner、Solver 和 Verifier，而 Verifier 是自验证器：策略本身的指数移动平均（EMA），既不需要标签也不需要外部评判器。Verifier 在每一个生成的问题成为监督之前，筛查其图像根据性和答案正确性，根据图像对 Solver 的推理进行评分，并裁决参考答案与强 Solver 共识之间的争议，当共识正确时纠正参考答案并惩罚 Questioner。仅在图像上训练，Trinity 在数学视觉推理和包含生物学内容的科学基准上提升了 Qwen3-VL-8B，例如在 SciVQR 的生物学划分上 +8.6 分，在 MathVerse 上 +12.8 分，并且其奖励动态表现得像一个健康的自我对弈课程应有的样子。这些结果表明，一个自演进的多模态智能体可以仅从未标注的科学图像中增强其科学推理能力，并由模型自身充当验证器。

Original abstract: Self-evolving vision-language models (VLMs), a form of self-improvement in which a model generates its own training data from unlabeled images, are a promising route toward agents that expand their reasoning capability in an unsupervised manner, without relying on ever-larger annotation budgets. Existing methods pair a Questioner that proposes problems with a Solver that answers them, but reward both roles mainly by agreement among sampled answers. Agreement is a weak proxy for truth: it cannot tell whether a question is grounded in the image, whether the proposed reference answer is right, or whether a confident majority is wrong in the same way. We present Trinity, in which one VLM plays three roles, Questioner, Solver, and Verifier, and the Verifier is a self-verifier: an exponential moving average (EMA) of the policy itself, requiring neither labels nor an external judge. The Verifier screens every generated question for image grounding and answer correctness before it becomes supervision, scores Solver reasoning against the image, and adjudicates disputes between the reference answer and a strong Solver consensus, correcting the reference and penalizing the Questioner when the consensus is right. Trained on images alone, Trinity improves Qwen3-VL-8B on mathematical visual reasoning and on science benchmarks with biology content, for example, +8.6 points on the biology split of SciVQR and +12.8 on MathVerse, and its reward dynamics behave as a healthy self-play curriculum should. These results suggest that a self-evolving multimodal agent can strengthen its scientific reasoning from unlabeled scientific images alone, with the model itself serving as the verifier.

</details>


### [69] [InferOpt: Constrained Multi-Objective Search for LLM Inference Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04473)
*Qi Chen,Yingying Cheng,Zhaoyi Sun,Li Zhou,Fan Zhang,Jie Sun*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将 LLM 推理配置调优重构成受约束多目标黑盒搜索，其代理集预筛、预算自适应收紧与全量再验证的流程，可迁移为 agent 自动优化中的低成本筛选与可信收益验证范式，但本身不含 LLM/coding agent。

TL;DR: 提出 InferOpt，将 LLM 推理配置建模为受约束多目标黑盒优化，统一搜索 KV 与 MoE 的逐层参数，在压缩缓存/专家时保持性能并优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理时需设置大量逐层参数，现有机制特定启发式只给单点且难以扩展到逐层搜索空间，因此需要可复用、可扩展的自动配置方法。

Method: 将推理配置视为受约束多目标黑盒优化，构建 InferOpt：仅需变量边界、确定性资源成本与评估钩子；在冻结采样代理集搜索，调用模型前拒绝超预算候选，自适应收紧预算，并在全规模数据集上重新验证 Pareto 代表点。统一处理 28 维连续 KV（Qwen2.5-7B）与 26 维离散 MoE（DeepSeek-V2-Lite）。

Result: KV：预填充后剪枝减少 16K 缓存 64.4%，TPOT 降低 22.9--48.5%；逐层预算比匹配均匀预算在 Full KV 参考点上高 7.3% 和 14.0%。MoE：top-k 调度移除 43.0% 路由 token--专家对，距默认值 0.59 个百分点以内，优于匹配预算基线。相比 Random Search、NSGA-II、MOTPE，InferOpt 在两个空间领先：KV 代理超体积最佳、保留量最低；MoE 在最低专家数下最接近未压缩参考。

Conclusion: InferOpt 是可复用的 LLM 推理配置搜索框架，可有效处理逐层连续/离散空间，在压缩 KV 缓存和 MoE 专家计算的同时保持性能，并优于现有搜索基线。

中文摘要：服务一个 LLM 意味着设置数十个推理时旋钮，从每层 KV 保留量到每层专家数量。实践中使用机制特定的启发式来设置它们，这些启发式只返回单一工作点，并且无法扩展到逐层搜索空间。我们将推理配置重新表述为受约束的多目标黑盒优化，并构建 InferOpt，一个可复用的搜索框架，它只需要变量边界、确定性的资源成本和评估钩子。InferOpt 在冻结的采样代理集上搜索，在任何模型调用之前拒绝超预算候选，自适应收紧预算，并在全规模数据集上重新验证 Pareto 代表点。一个流水线覆盖 28 维连续 KV 空间（Qwen2.5-7B）和 26 维离散 MoE 空间（DeepSeek-V2-Lite）。在 KV 上，预填充后剪枝将 16K 缓存减少 64.4%，并将 TPOT 降低 22.9--48.5%，而 InferOpt 搜索到的逐层预算在 Full KV 参考点上分别以 7.3% 和 14.0% 的优势优于匹配的均匀预算。在 MoE 上，InferOpt 搜索到的 top-k 调度移除了 43.0% 的路由 token--专家对，同时与默认值差距保持在 0.59 个百分点以内，比匹配预算的基线更接近。与 Random Search、NSGA-II 和 MOTPE 相比，InferOpt 在两个空间上都领先，在 KV 上取得最佳代理超体积和最低保留量，在 MoE 上以最低专家数最接近未压缩参考。

Original abstract: Serving an LLM means setting dozens of inference-time knobs, from per-layer KV retention to per-layer expert counts. Practice sets them with mechanism-specific heuristics that return a single operating point and do not scale to layer-wise search spaces. We recast inference configuration as constrained multi-objective black-box optimization and build InferOpt, a reusable search framework that requires only variable bounds, a deterministic resource cost, and an evaluation hook. InferOpt searches on a frozen sampled proxy set, rejects over-budget candidates before any model call, tightens the budget adaptively, and re-validates Pareto representatives on full-scale dataset. One pipeline covers a 28-dimensional continuous KV space (Qwen2.5-7B) and a 26-dimensional discrete MoE space (DeepSeek-V2-Lite). On KV, post-prefill pruning cuts the 16K cache by 64.4% and TPOT by 22.9--48.5%, and the searched layer-wise budget by InferOpt beats a matched uniform budget by 7.3% and 14.0% of the Full KV reference points. On MoE, a searched top-k schedule by InferOpt removes 43.0% of routed token--expert pairs while staying within 0.59 points of the default, closer than the matched-budget baselines. Against Random Search, NSGA-II, and MOTPE, InferOpt leads on both spaces, taking the best proxy hypervolume and the lowest retention on KV and staying closest to the uncompressed reference at the lowest experts on MoE.

</details>


### [70] [EvoCast: Reliable Autonomous Research Agents for Iterative Forecasting Architecture Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04517)
*Kaipeng Xu,Xianli Yan,Yan Wang,Xiang Liu,Shan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 26 pages, 11 figures, including references and appendices. Code: https://github.com/18e0-x/EvoCast

Selection reason: EvoCast 用 LLM agent 自主完成预测架构的代码实现与迭代演化，并以确定性程序权威约束源码编辑边界、规范评测与晋升决策，和自动代码优化 agent 的验证式控制高度同构。

TL;DR: EvoCast 是一个全自主研究智能体系统，通过认知-权限分离与证据驱动迭代实现预测架构自动演化，并在真实预测任务中优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度时序预测模型众多，但任务适配仍依赖大量专家工作；AutoML 受限于预定义搜索空间，通用 LLM 研究智能体又难以可靠控制实验协议与模型晋升。

Method: EvoCast 先通过机制消融建立并诊断任务特定基线，再综合数据集特征、诊断结果、历史轮次和失败记录生成有证据的研究方向；其认知-权限分离设计让 LLM 智能体负责开放式假设与代码实现，确定性程序权限控制源码编辑边界、规范评估和晋升决策，并累积实验证据指导后续迭代。

Result: EvoCast 以更高实现成功率和更低智能体侧 token/时间成本完成复杂架构修改；在三个真实世界预测案例中，所得任务特定架构优于所选基线、强预测模型和智能体基线。

Conclusion: EvoCast 表明了全自主、受控且证据驱动的架构演化在时序预测任务适配中的有效性，代码已开源。

中文摘要：深度时间序列预测模型已迅速多样化，但将其适配到特定任务仍需在模型选择、机制诊断、架构设计、实现和评估方面投入大量专家工作。现有 AutoML 方法受限于预定义的搜索空间，而通用 LLM 研究智能体缺乏对实验协议和模型晋升的可靠控制。我们提出 EvoCast，一个用于迭代式预测架构演化的全自主研究智能体系统。EvoCast 首先通过执行机制消融来建立并诊断任务特定的基线，然后根据数据集特征、诊断结果、先前轮次和失败记录生成有证据支撑的研究方向。其核心设计——认知-权限分离——将开放式假设生成和代码实现分配给 LLM 智能体，而确定性程序权限控制源代码编辑边界、规范评估和晋升决策。实验结果被累积为证据，以指导后续轮次。结果表明，EvoCast 以更高的实现成功率和更低的智能体侧 token/时间成本完成复杂架构修改，并在三个真实世界预测案例中开发出优于所选基线、强预测模型和智能体基线的任务特定架构。代码见 https://github.com/18e0-x/EvoCast。

Original abstract: Deep time-series forecasting models have rapidly diversified, yet adapting them to a specific task still requires extensive expert effort in model selection, mechanism diagnosis, architecture design, implementation, and evaluation. Existing AutoML methods are constrained by predefined search spaces, while general-purpose LLM research agents lack reliable control over experimental protocols and model promotion. We introduce EvoCast, a fully autonomous research-agent system for iterative forecasting architecture evolution. EvoCast first establishes and diagnoses a task-specific baseline through executed mechanism ablations, then generates evidence-grounded research directions from dataset characteristics, diagnostic results, prior rounds, and failure records. Its central design, cognition-authority separation, assigns open-ended hypothesis generation and code implementation to LLM agents, while deterministic program authorities control source-edit boundaries, canonical evaluation, and promotion decisions. Experimental outcomes are accumulated as evidence to guide subsequent rounds. Results show that EvoCast completes complex architecture modifications with higher implementation success and lower agent-side token/time cost, and develops task-specific architectures that outperform selected baselines, strong forecasting models, and agent baselines in three real-world forecasting cases. The code is available at https://github.com/18e0-x/EvoCast.

</details>


### [71] [Decide, Ask, or Defer: Clinical LLMs under Incomplete Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04542)
*Mingzhan Yang,Weili Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Preprint. Accepted to the Main Track of the NeurIPS 2026 GenAI4Health Workshop

Selection reason: 提出 DECIDE/ASK/DEFER 动作框架与盲化协议，把信息获取与延迟决策分离评估，其“证据不足时主动求取信息”的机制对 agent 可靠性控制有参考价值，虽在临床场景验证。

TL;DR: 提出DECIDE/ASK/DEFER表述与盲法协议，将信息获取与延迟决策分开；在DDXPlus的200个匹配证据状态上评估Qwen、Gemini和GPT，发现模型动作选择差异显著，三动作策略存在安全—自主性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床大语言模型不仅要决定诊断，还要判断证据是否足以支持自主决策；二元DECIDE/ABSTAIN框架混淆了不同的非决策状态，且未显式评估信息获取。

Method: 提出DECIDE/ASK/DEFER三动作表述和防止模型使用证据完整性元数据的盲法协议；基于DDXPlus构建200个匹配临床证据状态，评估Qwen、Gemini和GPT；对Qwen进行匹配的定向信息与随机信息分析，并与DECIDE/ABSTAIN基线比较。

Result: 相同证据下模型动作选择差异显著，200个状态中137个存在分歧；对Qwen，所选信息对后续自主决策可能性的影响比诊断正确性更清晰；三动作策略相比DECIDE/ABSTAIN在挽救一些错误自主决策的同时，也移除了一些正确自主决策。

Conclusion: 将信息获取与临床医生延迟决策分离，能暴露二元弃权所隐藏的行为，但并未带来统一更优的决策策略。

中文摘要：临床大语言模型不仅必须决定生成什么诊断，还必须判断现有证据是否足以进行自主决策。二元的DECIDE/ABSTAIN表述合并了不同的非决策状态，并且没有显式评估信息获取。我们提出了一种DECIDE/ASK/DEFER表述，以及一种防止模型使用证据完整性元数据的盲法协议。我们在由DDXPlus构建的200个匹配临床证据状态上评估了Qwen、Gemini和GPT。在相同证据下，这些模型在动作选择上表现出显著差异，在200个状态中有137个状态存在分歧。对于Qwen，一项匹配的定向信息与随机信息分析显示，所选信息对后续自主决策可能性的改变比诊断正确性更明显。其匹配的DECIDE/ABSTAIN基线进一步揭示了一种安全—自主性权衡：三动作策略挽救了一些错误的自主决策，但也移除了一些正确的自主决策。这些结果表明，将信息获取与临床医生延迟决策分离，能够揭示二元弃权所隐藏的行为，但并未产生统一更优的决策策略。

Original abstract: Clinical LLMs must decide not only what diagnosis to produce, but also whether the available evidence is sufficient for autonomous decision making. Binary DECIDE/ABSTAIN formulations merge distinct non decision states and do not explicitly evaluate information acquisition. We introduce a DECIDE/ASK/DEFER formulation together with a blinded protocol that prevents models from using evidence completeness metadata. We evaluate Qwen, Gemini, and GPT on 200 matched clinical evidence states constructed from DDXPlus. The models show substantial differences in action selection under identical evidence, with disagreement in 137 of 200 states. For Qwen, a matched targeted versus random analysis shows that selected information changes the likelihood of a subsequent autonomous decision more clearly than diagnostic correctness. Its matched DECIDE/ABSTAIN baseline further reveals a safety autonomy tradeoff: the three action policy rescues some erroneous autonomous decisions but also removes some correct autonomous deci sions. These results show that separating information acquisition from clinician deferral exposes behavior that binary abstention hides, without yielding a uniformly improved decision policy.

</details>


### [72] [Label Agreement Does Not Measure Authorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04544)
*Amir Sabbaghziarani,Bradley Thomas Baker,Theodore J. LaGrow,Sergey Plis*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 审计 agentic LLM 流水线，指出标签一致性无法衡量授权性，并提出可解析性、证据敏感性等分离度量与承诺门控，对 agent 可靠性评测与验证设计有直接迁移价值。

TL;DR: 审计了一个代理式LLM标签协调流水线：汇总分数正常但隐藏失败；标签一致性不衡量授权、完整性和证据敏感性；在COBRE/FBIRN上分开测量并加对照与门控以暴露失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汇总指标掩盖了代理式LLM标签流水线的具体失败模式，需明确标签一致性未覆盖的授权、可行动完整性与证据敏感性，并找出下游隐藏错误。

Method: 构建并审计代理式LLM流水线；在COBRE和FBIRN上分别测量标签一致性、是否授权提出、输出完整性/可行动性、证据变化下标签稳定性；加入对照，并将承诺门控在结果上；检查下游行顺序错误。

Result: 标签一致性0.857→0.870几乎不变，而所选行动一致性0.409→0.830翻倍；JSON可解析仍分别有10%和33%丢必需字段；重放代理原始案例满分、证据改变后零分；下游行顺序错误抹除大部分诊断信号；这些属性与标签一致性及彼此分离。

Conclusion: 应分开测量这些属性、配对照并门控承诺，使失败可见且可路由人工；不能防止失败；规则导出参考标签下一致仅代表一致性而非正确性；代码已公开。

中文摘要：许多团队现在将标签本体与元数据协调委托给代理式LLM流水线。我们构建了一个并对其进行了审计。我们的汇总分数看起来健康，但流水线以这些分数未显示的方式持续失败，因此我们着手找出它们隐藏了什么。标签一致性问的是提出的标签是否与参考标签匹配。它不问代理是否有权提出该标签，输出是否足够完整以便采取行动，或者当证据变化时标签是否随之改变。我们在COBRE和FBIRN这两个来自不同联盟的精神分裂症与对照神经影像队列上分别测量了这三项，它们与标签一致性以及彼此之间都发生了分离。向代理展示上游提案几乎不改变标签一致性，从0.857到0.870，而对所选行动的一致性翻倍，从0.409到0.830。可解析为JSON的输出仍然在一个模型的10%案例和另一个模型的33%案例中丢失了一个必需字段。而一个重放其首个答案的代理在原始案例上得分完美，一旦我们改变决定这些案例的证据，得分就为零。在下游，一个这些指标都未报告的行顺序错误抹除了大部分诊断信号。因此，我们将这些属性分开测量，为每个属性配一个对照，并根据结果对承诺进行门控，这使得失败可见且易于路由给人工。这些都不能防止失败。我们的参考标签是规则导出的，因此与它们一致意味着一致性，而非正确性。代码见 https://github.com/amir-sbg/Label-Agreement-Does-Not-Measure-Authorization。

Original abstract: Many groups now delegate label ontology and metadata harmonization to agentic LLM pipelines. We built one and audited it. Our aggregate scores looked healthy, but the pipeline kept failing in ways they did not show, so we set out to find what they hid. Label agreement asks whether a proposed label matches a reference. It does not ask whether the agent was entitled to propose it, whether the output was complete enough to act on, or whether the label moved when the evidence moved. We measured those three separately on COBRE and FBIRN, two schizophrenia and control neuroimaging cohorts from different consortia, and they come apart, from label agreement and from each other. Showing the agent an upstream proposal barely moves label agreement, 0.857 to 0.870, while agreement on the chosen action doubles, 0.409 to 0.830. Output that parses as JSON still drops a required field on 10% of one model's cases and 33% of the other's. And an agent that replays its first answer scores perfectly on original cases and zero once we change the evidence that decides them. Downstream, a row-order error that none of these metrics reports erases most of the diagnostic signal. So we measure these properties apart, pair each with a control, and gate commitment on the result, which makes failures visible and easy to route to a person. None of this prevents failure. Our reference labels are rule-derived, so agreement with them means consistency, not correctness. Code is available at https://github.com/amir-sbg/Label-Agreement-Does-Not-Measure-Authorization.

</details>


### [73] [$\mathrm{TRIZ}^{a}$: Guiding Agent Evolution from Pattern Recognition to Solution Invention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04555)
*Wenyin Liu,Yiheng Huang,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 13 pages, 3 figures, 7 tables

Selection reason: TRIZ^a 将 TRIZ 理论注入 LLM agent 演化搜索，包含代码变换、硬门控晋升、协议指纹与配对复跑验证，与自动代码优化 agent 的搜索引导和可信评估高度相关。

TL;DR: 提出 TRIZ^a，将 TRIZ 发明理论与 LLM 智能体进化搜索结合用于研发自动化，并在网络安全数据集上验证；部分实验显著提升 F1，但完整 CICIoT2023 战役虽提升 macro-F1 却未通过冻结晋升门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机或无类型变异缺乏结构化、可解释的生成方向；需要将 TRIZ 理论引导、功能信息度量与硬门控结合，以在对抗性快速演变领域中实现可追溯、可验证的研发自动化，并区分指标改进与正式晋升。

Method: 将 TRIZ 的 40 条发明原理和矛盾矩阵与 LLM 驱动的智能体进化搜索结合；在冻结参考契约下操作化功能信息（FI），并与 TRIZ 理想度结合，以可通约信息尺度衡量有用和有害功能；采用硬门控保持晋升与指标改进分离；在 PowerDuck GOOSE、CICIoT2023 和 CIC-DDoS2019 上，使用配对重跑协议、协议指纹和硬门控验证进行验证。

Result: 在配对重跑协议、协议指纹和硬门控验证下，遗留实验分别取得 +2.88、+4.23 和 +0.15 个百分点的绝对 F1 提升；已完成的 45 活动 CICIoT2023 战役将 macro-F1 从 0.8325 提高到 0.8483，但未通过其冻结晋升门控；所有结果均可从矛盾识别和 TRIZ 原理选择追溯到代码变换、评估指标和晋升决策。

Conclusion: TRIZ^a 为研发自动化提供了理论引导、可解释且可追溯的范式，但其结果表明指标提升不等于晋升，必须依赖冻结硬门控；在网络安全领域的验证显示部分提升有效，但完整 campaign 仍需进一步满足晋升标准。

中文摘要：我们提出 TRIZ^a（由智能体指数化的 TRIZ），一种通用的研发自动化范式，将 TRIZ 发明理论与 LLM 驱动的智能体进化搜索相结合。TRIZ 的 40 条发明原理和矛盾矩阵为解决方案生成提供结构化、可解释的方向，用理论引导的构思取代随机或无类型变异。功能信息（FI）在冻结参考契约下操作化，与 TRIZ 理想度结合，以可通约的信息尺度衡量有用功能与有害功能，同时硬门控使晋升与指标改进保持区分。我们在网络安全——一个对抗性且快速演变的领域——在 PowerDuck GOOSE、CICIoT2023 和 CIC-DDoS2019 上验证 TRIZ^a。在带有协议指纹和硬门控验证的配对重跑协议下，遗留实验分别产生 +2.88、+4.23 和 +0.15 个百分点的绝对 F1 提升。一个已完成的 45 活动 CICIoT2023 战役进一步将 macro-F1 从 0.8325 提高到 0.8483，但未通过其冻结的晋升门控。每个结果都可从矛盾识别和 TRIZ 原理选择追溯到代码变换、评估指标和晋升决策。

Original abstract: We propose $\mathrm{TRIZ}^{a}$ (TRIZ exponentiated by an agent), a general R\&D automation paradigm that combines TRIZ inventive theory with LLM-driven agent evolutionary search. TRIZ's 40 inventive principles and contradiction matrix provide structured, explainable directions for solution generation, replacing random or untyped mutation with theory-guided ideation. Functional information (FI), operationalized under a frozen reference contract, is combined with TRIZ Ideality to measure useful and harmful function on a commensurable information scale, while hard gates keep promotion distinct from metric improvement. We validate $\mathrm{TRIZ}^{a}$ in cybersecurity--an adversarial and rapidly evolving domain--on PowerDuck GOOSE, CICIoT2023, and CIC-DDoS2019. Under paired-rerun protocols with protocol fingerprinting and hard-gate validation, the legacy experiments yield absolute F1 improvements of $+2.88$, $+4.23$, and $+0.15$ percentage points, respectively. A completed 45-activity CICIoT2023 campaign further increases macro-F1 from $0.8325$ to $0.8483$, but does not pass its frozen promotion gate. Every result remains traceable from contradiction identification and TRIZ principle selection to code transformation, evaluation metrics, and promotion decision.

</details>


### [74] [Recursive Improvement of a Differentiable Scientific Software Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04561)
*Pengcheng Hou,Xiaojun Tan,Sihan Hu,Ruisi Wang,Shuo Chen,Lei Wang,Youjin Deng,Kun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 以 AI coding agent 递归改进可微分科学软件生态，覆盖需求识别、实现、独立导数校验与性能评估并沉淀为共享资源，是典型的代码修改 agent 闭环与验证机制研究。

TL;DR: 提出智能体驱动的可微科学软件演化环境，统一微分接口与质量评估，在20个科学计算包中构建并验证自动微分扩展，实现跨任务复用与性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微编程能连接科学计算与梯度推断、学习和设计，但跨异构软件生态系统扩展需实现导数、集成接口和评估质量；AI编码智能体虽可加速，但需识别研究需求并评估实现质量。

Method: 提出智能体驱动的可微科学软件演化环境；统一微分接口暴露可复用导数规则；以研究需求指导开发，通过独立导数检查、工作流测试和性能评估实现验证；验证后的软件、程序和测试作为共享资源。

Result: 在20个物理、化学和生物建模包中构建并验证自动微分扩展；研究流程展示跨任务复用；基准测试在梯度评估和完整参数估计上相比有限差分节省计算；研究驱动修订使不支持工作流可微、修正可观测量导数、消除冗余计算；量子控制和热设计研究据物理评估修正目标并改进设计。

Conclusion: 为在既有科学软件中扩展可微编程提供实用方法，并围绕共享计算生态系统的递归改进组织AI智能体。

中文摘要：可微编程将科学计算与基于梯度的推断、学习和设计连接起来。将这些能力扩展到异构软件生态系统中，需要专门的努力来实现导数、集成接口并评估质量。AI编码智能体可以加速这一转变，但将其能力转化为有用的科学软件，需要识别研究需求并评估实现满足这些需求的程度。我们提出了一个用于智能体驱动的可微科学软件演化的环境，该环境连接了需求识别、开发和质量评估。一个统一的微分接口在现有数值例程之外暴露可复用的导数规则，使研究任务能够共享这些能力。研究需求指导开发，并通过独立的导数检查、工作流测试和性能评估来评估实现。经过验证的软件、研究程序和测试成为后续研究的共享资源。我们构建并验证了跨越20个软件包的自动微分扩展，涵盖物理、化学和生物建模，研究工作流展示了跨任务的重用。基准测试表明，在梯度评估和完整参数估计中，相比有限差分具有计算节省。研究驱动的修订使此前不支持的工作流变为可微，纠正科学可观测量的导数并消除冗余计算。量子控制和热设计研究根据物理评估修正目标，在重用现有导数的同时改进设计。这项工作提供了一种实用方法，将可微编程扩展到已有科学软件中，并围绕共享计算生态系统的递归改进来组织AI智能体。

Original abstract: Differentiable programming connects scientific computation with gradient-based inference, learning and design. Extending these capabilities across a heterogeneous software ecosystem requires specialized effort to implement derivatives, integrate interfaces and evaluate quality. AI coding agents can accelerate this transformation, but translating their capabilities into useful scientific software requires identifying research needs and evaluating how well implementations meet them. We present an environment for agent-driven evolution of differentiable scientific software that connects demand identification, development and quality evaluation. A unified differentiation interface exposes reusable derivative rules alongside existing numerical routines, allowing research tasks to share these capabilities. Research requirements guide development, with implementations assessed through independent derivative checks, workflow tests and performance evaluation. Validated software, research programs and tests become shared resources for subsequent studies. We construct and validate automatic differentiation extensions across 20 packages spanning physical, chemical and biological modeling, with research workflows demonstrating reuse across tasks. Benchmarks demonstrate computational savings over finite differences in gradient evaluation and complete parameter estimation. Research-driven revisions make previously unsupported workflows differentiable, correct derivatives of scientific observables and eliminate redundant computation. Quantum-control and thermal-design studies revise objectives in response to physical evaluation, improving designs while reusing existing derivatives. This work provides a practical approach to expanding differentiable programming across established scientific software and organizing AI agents around the recursive improvement of a shared computational ecosystem.

</details>


### [75] [SEIS: Self-Evolving Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04646)
*Zhen Xu,Jingyu Liu,Zongze Li,Tahseen Rabbani,Ce Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 21 pages

Selection reason: SEIS 用 agent 自演化迭代会话优化整个推理引擎源码，继承历史经验并做数值与下游任务正确性验证，是 LLM agent 自动代码性能优化与可靠加速验证的直接相关研究。

TL;DR: SEIS 通过智能体自演化端到端优化 mini-sglang 推理引擎，在 H100 上服务 Qwen3-0.6B 达到 3.27 倍吞吐并超越 vLLM、TensorRT-LLM、SGLang，且验证了正确性，表明该方法可优化复杂系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理系统直接影响语言模型服务速度与成本，但既有工作多只优化内核、内存等局部组件，缺乏端到端整体优化。

Method: 提出 SEIS（Self-Evolving Inference Systems），以智能体自演化方式，通过迭代会话继承经验并修改代码，无需人工干预地自主优化整个 mini-sglang 引擎。

Result: 在 H100 上服务 Qwen3-0.6B，优化后引擎吞吐为原始 mini-sglang 的 3.27 倍，单请求负载下优于 vLLM、TensorRT-LLM、SGLang；通过数值差异及数学、长上下文检索下游准确率验证正确性；会话历史显示加速源于整体重构，继承早期会话优于独立尝试。

Conclusion: 智能体自演化能端到端优化复杂系统；评估也需随引擎演化，让智能体演化评估是自然的下一步。

中文摘要：推理系统决定了语言模型能被服务的速度和成本，因此让它们更快具有直接的实际价值。然而，先前工作大多聚焦于优化大型系统中的某些部分，例如内核或内存。在这项工作中，我们采取整体性方法，应用智能体自演化来端到端地优化整个系统。我们的 SEIS（Self-Evolving Inference Systems，自演化推理系统）在无人干预的情况下，通过带有继承经验和代码更改的迭代会话，自主优化整个 mini-sglang 引擎。在 H100 上服务 Qwen3-0.6B 时，所得引擎达到原始 mini-sglang 实现 3.27 倍的吞吐量，并在单请求工作负载中击败 vLLM、TensorRT-LLM 和 SGLang 等 SOTA 引擎。SEIS 优化后的推理引擎的正确性通过数值差异以及数学和长上下文检索任务上的下游准确率进行测试。代码和会话历史表明，加速来自对整个引擎的重新设计，并且基于早期会话继续构建胜过独立尝试。这些结果表明，智能体自演化可以端到端地优化复杂系统。评估也必须随引擎演化，而让智能体来演化它是自然的下一步。

Original abstract: Inference systems determine how fast and how cheaply language models can be served, so making them faster has direct practical value. However, prior work focuses mostly on optimizing certain parts such as kernels or memory within the large system. In this work, we take a holistic approach and apply agentic self-evolution to optimize the whole system end-to-end. Our SEIS (Self-Evolving Inference Systems) autonomously optimizes the entire mini-sglang engine without human intervention through iterative sessions with inherited experiences and code changes. Serving Qwen3-0.6B on H100, the resulting engine reaches 3.27X the throughput of the original mini-sglang implementation and beats SOTA engines like vLLM, TensorRT-LLM, and SGLang in the single-request workload. The correctness of the optimized inference engine by SEIS is tested in terms of numerical difference and downstream accuracy on math and long-context retrieval tasks. The code and session histories show that the speedup comes from redesigning the whole engine and that building on earlier sessions beats independent attempts. These results suggest that agentic self-evolution can optimize a complex system end-to-end. The evaluation also has to evolve with the engine, and letting agents evolve it is a natural next step.

</details>


### [76] [MASBench: Benchmarking LLM-based Multi-Agent Collaboration under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04672)
*Qizhi Chu,Zekai Yu,Sijie Wen,Yang Liu,Chen Qian,Cheng Yang,Chuan Shi,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: MASBench 在部分可观测下评测协议、记忆、路由三类多 agent 协作机制，并给出性能、通信成本与性价比指标，对多 agent 协作与长程任务评测有可迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出MASBench：一个面向部分可观测约束的多智能体协作基准，涵盖推理、调度、博弈三类渐进任务，系统评估协议、记忆、路由三类协作机制，并提供性能、通信成本与成本效益等确定性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界协作通常是部分可观测的，但许多现有多智能体基准假设全局可观测性，且缺乏对协作机制进行系统评估的支持。

Method: 提出MASBench基准，在部分可观测约束下组织为推理、调度、博弈三个渐进式任务类别，并逐步评估协议、记忆和路由三种代表性协作机制；同时提供性能得分、通信成本和成本效益等确定性评估指标。

Result: 在多种LLM骨干模型和机制配置上开展实验，刻画协作结果与通信开销，并为有效MAS设计提供经验指导。

Conclusion: MASBench为部分可观测条件下的多智能体协作机制评估与设计提供了系统化基准和实证依据，代码已开源。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）已逐步演变为自主智能体的核心。基于这一进展，基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）将多个智能体协调为一个协同团队，以完成超出单个智能体能力的复杂任务。此类系统的有效性不仅取决于智能体本身，还取决于协作机制如何设计与组织。需要注意的是，现实世界中的协作通常是部分可观测的，其中每个智能体由于物理或隐私相关约束只能访问环境的部分信息。然而，许多现有多智能体基准假设全局可观测性，并且对系统评估协作机制的支持有限。为弥补这一差距，我们提出MASBench，一个在部分可观测约束下设计的多智能体协作基准。它组织为三个渐进式任务类别：推理、调度和博弈。通过这种结构，我们渐进式评估三种代表性协作机制：协议、记忆和路由。MASBench进一步提供确定性评估指标，包括性能得分、通信成本和成本效益，以刻画协作结果和通信开销。跨多种LLM骨干模型和机制配置的实验为有效的MAS设计提供了经验指导。代码见：https://github.com/BUPT-GAMMA/MASBench

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have progressively evolved into the core of autonomous agents. Building on this progress, LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) coordinate multiple agents into a synergistic team to accomplish complex tasks that exceed the capabilities of individual agents. The effectiveness of such systems depends not only on the agents themselves, but also on how collaboration mechanisms are designed and organized. Note that real-world collaboration is typically partially observable, where each agent can only access partial information about the environment due to physical or privacy-related constraints. However, many existing multi-agent benchmarks assume global observability, and leave limited support for systematically evaluating collaboration mechanisms. To bridge this gap, we introduce MASBench, a multi-agent collaboration benchmark designed under partially observable constraints. It is organized into three progressive task categories: Reasoning, Scheduling, and Game. Through this structure, we progressively evaluate three representative collaboration mechanisms: Protocol, Memory, and Routing. MASBench further provides deterministic evaluation metrics, including performance score, communication cost, and cost effectiveness, to characterize both collaboration outcomes and communication overhead. Experiments across diverse LLM backbones and mechanism configurations offer empirical guidance for effective MAS design. Code is available at: https://github.com/BUPT-GAMMA/MASBench

</details>


### [77] [When Debate Helps: Proposal Supply and Verification-Aware Readout in Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04686)
*Zihao Zhao,Tunyu Zhang,Haizhou Shi,Yusong Zhao,Xinxi Zhang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 27 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: 分析多 agent 辩论何时优于投票，提出可恢复余量、提案覆盖与验证感知读出的核算模型，对 agent 协作与自我验证机制设计有明确启发。

TL;DR: 多智能体辩论要胜过多数投票，需同时具备足够提案覆盖以提供正确答案，以及能利用真值敏感证据的读出/验证机制；LVD与覆盖度选群显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辩论常不能超过简单多数投票，需厘清其成功条件：提案供给需包含正确答案，读出需在投票漏掉时识别出正确答案。

Method: 提出可恢复余量度量提案供给；提出潜在验证辩论（LVD）核算模型，用受控固定提案干预估计验证证据的潜在效应；用有标签/无标签覆盖目标从神经丛智能体构建群体，并在两骨干、预算匹配推理基准上重复随机评估与轮级控制。

Result: 正确证据改变答案概率与生成决策；覆盖度选群增加互补提案供给并提高总体准确率；交互相对直接使用最终化器有额外增益。

Conclusion: 提案覆盖与真值敏感证据使用是辩论优于投票的互补条件。代码已开源。

中文摘要：多智能体辩论可以提升推理能力，但往往无法击败简单多数投票。我们认为，成功的辩论需要两个不同机制：提案供给必须使正确答案浮现出来，而读出机制必须在投票未能识别该答案时识别出它。我们通过可恢复余量来形式化第一个要求，它衡量的是存在正确提案但多数答案错误的情况。针对第二个要求，我们开发了潜在验证辩论（Latent Verification Debate, LVD），这是一种核算模型，其中候选提案在最终生成之前会接收针对答案的验证证据。受控的固定提案干预以等效同伴支持单位估计这种潜在效应，并表明在提案供给保持不变的情况下，正确证据会改变答案概率和生成决策。为了改进提案供给，我们使用有标签和无标签覆盖目标，由神经丛（neural-thicket）智能体构建群体。在两个骨干模型和预算匹配的推理基准上，覆盖度选择的群体在重复随机评估中增加了互补提案供给并提高了总体准确率。轮级控制进一步表明，与将同一最终化器直接应用于初始提案相比，交互提供了额外增益。这些结果确定提案覆盖度和对真值敏感的证据使用是辩论优于投票的互补条件。代码可在 https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/when-debate-helps 获取。

Original abstract: Multi-agent debate can improve reasoning, yet often fails to beat simple majority voting. We argue that successful debate requires two distinct mechanisms: proposal supply must surface a correct answer, and readout must identify that answer when voting misses it. We formalize the first requirement through recoverable headroom, which measures cases where a correct proposal is available but the majority answer is wrong. For the second, we develop Latent Verification Debate (LVD), an accounting model in which candidate proposals receive answer-specific verification evidence before final generation. Controlled fixed-proposal interventions estimate this latent effect in equivalent peer-support units and show that correct evidence changes answer probabilities and generated decisions while proposal supply remains fixed. To improve proposal supply, we construct societies from neural-thicket agents using labeled and label-free coverage objectives. Across two backbones and matched-budget reasoning benchmarks, coverage-selected societies increase complementary proposal supply and improve aggregate accuracy in repeated stochastic evaluations. Round-level controls further show that interaction provides gains beyond applying the same finalizer directly to the initial proposals. These results identify proposal coverage and truth-sensitive evidence use as complementary conditions for debate to outperform voting. Code is available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/when-debate-helps.

</details>


### [78] [RAGStress: A controlled benchmark for evaluating retrieval-augmented generation under knowledge-base degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04691)
*Shiqi Yang,Jiekai Ma,Gaoyuan Du*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 系统化评测检索增强系统在知识库退化下的鲁棒性，其受控基准设计与'干净检索掩盖鲁棒性差异'结论可迁移到 agent 检索/记忆可靠性验证。

TL;DR: 提出RAGStress受控基准，用四类KB损坏×三级严重度在57个MMLU主题、182,546个文档上评估RAG，发现干净检索会掩盖鲁棒性差异、语义损坏危害更大、无检索准确率不能预测损坏检索鲁棒性、混合KB准确率非最坏情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG评估通常隐含假设知识库干净，缺乏对现实知识库退化下系统行为的刻画，因此需要可控的压力测试基准来系统评估RAG鲁棒性。

Method: 构建RAGStress基准，设置事实损坏、数字拼写错误、相关性投毒、矛盾注入四类自然istic损坏，每类含轻微、中等、明显三个严重度；采用单KB、元数据过滤实验设计，基于57个MMLU主题和182,546个文档，完成52,500次模型-问题-条件评估；同时提供生成脚本、损坏提示、元数据模式和评估代码等工件包。

Result: RAGStress显示：干净检索可能掩盖鲁棒性差异；语义保真度损坏比信号效用扰动危害更大；无检索准确率不能预测损坏检索鲁棒性；混合KB准确率不应被视为最坏情况鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RAGStress旨在作为知识库损坏下RAG鲁棒性的受控压力测试，而非通用模型排行榜；论文明确其预期用途、支持性声明和局限性，并公开可复现工件包。

中文摘要：检索增强生成（RAG）通常在隐含假设底层知识库（KB）是干净的条件下进行评估，这使得RAG系统在现实KB退化下的行为表征不足。我们提出RAGStress，一个受控评估基准，用于在系统性KB损坏下对RAG系统进行压力测试。该基准将四种自然istic损坏类型（事实损坏、数字拼写错误、相关性投毒和矛盾注入）与三个严重程度（轻微、中等和明显）相结合，基于由57个MMLU主题和182,546个文档构建的单KB、元数据过滤实验设计。在52,500次模型-问题-条件评估中，RAGStress揭示：干净的检索可能掩盖鲁棒性差异；语义保真度损坏比信号效用扰动危害更大；无检索准确率不能预测损坏检索鲁棒性；混合KB准确率不应被视为最坏情况鲁棒性。我们记录了该基准的预期用途、支持性声明和局限性，并提供了包含生成脚本、损坏提示、元数据模式和评估代码的工件包。RAGStress旨在作为KB损坏下RAG鲁棒性的受控压力测试，而非通用模型排行榜。

Original abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is typically evaluated under the implicit assumption that the underlying knowledge base (KB) is clean, leaving the behaviour of RAG systems under realistic KB degradation poorly characterised. We introduce RAGStress, a controlled evaluation benchmark for stress-testing RAG systems under systematic KB corruption. The benchmark pairs four naturalistic corruption types (factual corruption, numeric typo, relevance poisoning, and contradiction injection) with three severity levels (subtle, moderate, and obvious) over a single-KB, metadata-filtered experimental design built from 57 MMLU subjects and 182,546 documents. Across 52,500 model-question-condition evaluations, RAGStress reveals that clean retrieval can mask robustness differences, semantic-fidelity corruptions are substantially more harmful than signal-utility perturbations, no-retrieval accuracy does not predict corrupted-retrieval robustness, and mixed-KB accuracy should not be treated as worst-case robustness. We document the benchmark's intended use, supported claims, and limitations, and provide an artifact bundle including generation scripts, corruption prompts, metadata schema, and evaluation code. RAGStress is intended as a controlled stress test for RAG robustness under KB corruption, not as a general model leaderboard.

</details>


### [79] [Penumbra: Sample-Efficient Adversarial Search for Regulatory Obligations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04693)
*Anthony Rhodes*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at the Workshop on Self-Evolving Diversity-Driven Search for Robust AI Systems (EvoRobust) at NeurIPS 2026. Workshop papers are non-archival. 12 pages (4 content pages plus references and appendices)

Selection reason: 提出面向监管合规的对抗式搜索，在有限采样预算下定位合规边界响应，其自适应分配与失败面覆盖思路对 agent 安全红队与验证有启发。

TL;DR: Penumbra 通过自适应对抗搜索，高效寻找仅差少量编辑即可跨越合规/违规边界的回应对，以覆盖监管义务的失败面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融、医疗、法律等高风险领域，违规无字面特征，遗漏是否重大或披露是否充分取决于未说内容；监管红队测试的关键瓶颈是样本效率。

Method: 提出 Penumbra：从已验证锚点出发，在扩展编辑预算下对抗搜索，直到双评估者委员会裁决翻转，输出边界两侧相邻回应；采用自适应分配，以（义务×失败模式）失败面覆盖为目标。

Result: 预算匹配时，自适应分配在 59% 候选上达到均匀分配全预算覆盖率；记录数相同时失败模式覆盖为朴素枚举 1.43 倍，增益限于目标轴。金融顾问章程 60 项义务得 144 对；临床分诊 18 项义务得 49 对，紧密度相同，最难失败模式一致。

Conclusion: 这些边界对子揭示义务条款失效之处，是部署智能体必须测试的内容；搜索成本随边界规模而非文本规模扩展。

中文摘要：智能体正在进入金融、医疗和法律领域，这些领域中违规不会留下词汇特征，却会带来真实处罚。遗漏是否重大、披露是否充分，取决于回应遗漏了什么。探测此类义务意味着要找到距合规性翻转仅差一次最小编辑的回应，而每次探测都需要一次生成和两次裁定，因此监管红队测试的约束瓶颈是样本效率，而非数量。我们提出 Penumbra，一种对抗性搜索：它从已验证的锚点出发，在逐步扩展的编辑预算下行走，直到由两名评估者组成的委员会改变其裁决，并输出跨越该变化的两条相邻回应。分配是自适应的，目标是覆盖失败面：每个候选所解决的不同的（义务 × 失败模式）单元。在预算匹配时，自适应分配在 59% 的候选上达到均匀分配在全预算下的覆盖率；在记录数相同时，它覆盖的失败模式是朴素枚举的 1.43 倍，且增益仅限于其所针对的轴。在金融顾问章程的 60 项经筛选义务上，Penumbra 返回 144 对回应，每对均为一个合规回应和一个违规回应，委员会将其置于边界两侧，二者仅相差几个词，而直接要求模型同时生成两者时，产生的文本几乎毫无共同之处。第二部章程，即用于临床分诊的章程，在 18 项义务上重现了这一点：49 对，紧密度相同，且相同的失败模式最难处理。这样的对子表明义务自身条款在何处不再起决定作用，而这正是部署于该义务下的智能体必须接受测试的内容，并且该搜索以随边界而非文本规模扩展的成本找到它们。

Original abstract: Agents are entering finance, healthcare and law, sectors where a violation leaves no lexical signature and carries real penalties. Whether an omission is material, or a disclosure sufficient, depends on what the response left out. Probing such an obligation means finding responses one minimal edit from flipping compliance, and every probe costs a generation and two adjudications, so the binding constraint on regulatory red-teaming is sample efficiency, not volume. We introduce Penumbra, an adversarial search that walks from a verified anchor under an expanding edit budget until a two-evaluator committee changes its verdict, and emits the two adjacent responses that straddle the change. Allocation is adaptive, and the objective is coverage of the defeat surface: distinct (obligation x defeat mode) cells resolved per candidate. At matched budget, adaptive allocation reaches uniform allocation's full-budget coverage on 59% of the candidates; at equal records it covers 1.43x the defeat modes of naive enumeration, and the gain is confined to the axis it targets. On 60 screened obligations of a financial advisory constitution, Penumbra returns 144 pairs, each a compliant and a violating response that the committee places on opposite sides of the boundary, differing by a handful of words where a model asked for both directly produces texts sharing almost nothing. A second constitution, for clinical triage, reproduces this on 18 obligations: 49 pairs at the same tightness, with the same modes hardest.
