### [134] [From Token-Max to Outcome-Max: How You Use AI Determines Its Productivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05697)
*Chen Xu,Mengqiao Liu,Beibei Li,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出outcome-max目标与停止规则，研究agent过度消耗token的效率问题，对agent目标设计与成本控制有启发。

TL;DR: 论文指出以 token 消耗为目标的 token-max 会导致 AI 使用低效，提出按单位成本奖励独立验证任务完成的 outcome-max 及 OutcomeShare 激励，并通过三层仿真表明其能提升 AI 辅助生产效率、大体维持任务表现并带来多方共享收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更大的 AI 使用量未必带来成比例的生产力提升，因此需要识别低效来源，并重新设计支配 AI 使用的目标与激励机制。

Method: 将 token-max 识别为低效来源；提出 outcome-max，按单位成本奖励经独立验证的任务完成并诱导停止规则；构建覆盖即时交互、长期行为适应和组织协作的三层仿真框架；引入 OutcomeShare 激励机制，并进行理论与仿真分析。

Result: 在三层仿真中，outcome-max 均提升 AI 辅助生产效率，且大体保持经独立验证的任务表现；理论与仿真显示 OutcomeShare 可促成参与，并为员工、企业和 LLM 提供商创造共享收益。

Conclusion: AI 生产力不仅取决于模型能力，也取决于如何构建治理 AI 使用的目标。

中文摘要：生成式人工智能（AI）模型能够执行日益复杂的任务，但更多的 AI 使用并不必然转化为成比例的生产力提升。我们将 token-max 认定为这种低效的一个来源：当 token 消耗被视为有效努力时，智能体被鼓励过度用力，并支出超出必要范围的计算。我们转而提出 outcome-max，它按单位成本奖励经独立验证的任务完成，并诱导出一种有原则的停止规则。随后，为研究这些目标，我们开发了一个三层仿真框架，涵盖即时交互、长期行为适应和组织协作。在所有三个层面上，outcome-max 都提高了 AI 辅助生产的效率，同时大体保持了经独立验证的任务表现。为了进一步使这些激励与 outcome-max 保持一致，我们引入了 OutcomeShare，一种激励机制。理论与仿真表明，OutcomeShare 能够促成参与，同时为员工、企业和 LLM 提供商创造共享收益。总之，我们的结果表明，AI 生产力不仅取决于模型能力，还取决于如何构建治理 AI 使用的目标。

Original abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (AI) models can perform increasingly complex tasks, yet greater AI usage does not necessarily translate into proportional productivity gains. We identify token-max as one source of this inefficiency: when token consumption is treated as productive effort, agents are encouraged to over-exert and expend computation beyond what is necessary. We instead propose outcome-max, which rewards independently verified task completion per unit cost and induces a principled stopping rule. Then, to study these objectives, we develop a three-level simulation framework spanning immediate interaction, long-run behavioral adaptation, and organizational collaboration. Across all three levels, outcome-max improves the efficiency of AI-assisted production while largely preserving verified task performance. To further align these incentives with outcome-max, we introduce OutcomeShare, an incentive mechanism. Theory and simulation show that OutcomeShare can induce participation while generating shared gains for employees, firms, and LLM providers. Together, our results suggest that AI productivity not only depends on model capability, but also on how to construct the objectives governing AI use.

</details>


### [135] [Second-Order Problem Solving for Recursive Self-Improvement in Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05701)
*Yuxuan Jiang,Aditya Vempaty,Ashish Jagmohan*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 二阶诊断式递归自我改进，用于Lean 4证明与Verus代码生成，通过执行迹触发机制探究并编辑工作流，直接相关。

TL;DR: SO-RSI将递归自我改进从一阶参数修补提升为二阶机制诊断，通过监测结构异常、执行诊断探针和持久探究记忆来累积因果证据，在Lean 4与Verus任务中显著提升通过率并抑制失败重现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RSI仅针对即时失败症状修补表面参数，导致反复试错且无法解决底层机制；需要先诊断失败原因，再进行结构性干预。

Method: 提出SO-RSI：被动监测执行轨迹中的重现、对立编辑、期望不匹配三类结构异常，触发机制调查；执行轻量级诊断探针，跨RSI轮次维护持久探究记忆，累积因果证据以指导系统性工作流编辑而非参数修补。

Result: 在Lean 4证明生成和基于Verus的可验证代码生成中，在匹配的24小时搜索预算下，SO-RSI相较Naive RSI将最终留出集通过率分别提高21.8和25.8个百分点；行为分析表明其显著抑制失败重现并消除零进展优化循环。

Conclusion: SO-RSI通过二阶诊断性探究有效克服标准RSI的试错振荡，实现更稳健的工作流优化，并减少失败重现与无效循环。

中文摘要：递归自我改进（Recursive Self-Improvement, RSI）使智能体能够通过执行反馈迭代优化其工作流。然而，标准的RSI通常作为一阶优化器运行：它针对即时失败症状反复修补表面级参数，常常导致试错式振荡，而无法解决底层机制。为解决这一局限，我们提出SO-RSI，一个将工作流优化提升为二阶诊断性探究的框架，在实施结构性干预之前先调查失败为何发生。SO-RSI被动监控执行轨迹中的三种结构性异常（重现、对立编辑和期望不匹配），以触发有针对性的机制调查。通过执行轻量级诊断探针并在RSI轮次间维护持久探究记忆，SO-RSI累积因果证据，以指导系统性的工作流编辑而非参数修补。在Lean 4证明生成和基于Verus的可验证代码生成任务中，在匹配的24小时搜索预算下，SO-RSI相较Naive RSI将最终留出集通过率分别提升21.8和25.8个百分点。行为分析进一步证实，SO-RSI显著抑制失败重现，并消除无产出的零进展优化循环。

Original abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) enables agents to iteratively optimize their workflows via execution feedback. However, standard RSI typically operates as a first-order optimizer: it repeatedly patches surface-level parameters in response to immediate failure symptoms, often leading to trial-and-error thrashing without resolving underlying mechanisms. To address this limitation, we introduce SO-RSI, a framework that elevates workflow optimization to a second-order diagnostic inquiry, investigating why failures occur before committing to structural interventions. SO-RSI passively monitors execution traces for three structural anomalies (recurrence, opposing edits, and expectation mismatch) to trigger targeted mechanism investigations. By executing lightweight diagnostic probes and maintaining persistent inquiry memory across RSI rounds, SO-RSI accumulates causal evidence to guide systematic workflow edits rather than parameter patches. Across Lean 4 proof generation and Verus-based verifiable code generation, SO-RSI improves final held-out pass rates over Naive RSI by 21.8 and 25.8 percentage points under matched 24-hour search budgets. Behavioral analyses further confirm that SO-RSI substantially suppresses failure recurrence and eliminates unproductive zero-progress optimization loops.

</details>


### [136] [Mining Agent Skills from Production Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05777)
*Yue Ran Kang,Colton Mikolajczyk,Chhaya Methani,Hazel Mak,Sahil Bhatnagar,Susheel Suresh,Alejandro Gutierrez Munoz*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 23 pages, 4 figures, 10 tables

Selection reason: 从生产轨迹中挖掘agent技能，研究采样、结果标签与技能形式对下游任务的影响，直接涉及经验复用与技能表示。

TL;DR: 在固定技能挖掘流水线的条件下，比较三种挖掘证据水平与两种技能形式在 ThinkingBox-Bench 和 APEX-Agents 上的效果，发现最优配置因企业领域和任务结构而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中任务成败的可靠信息常不可得，需理解轨迹采样、结果信息可用性及技能形式如何影响下游性能，以指导技能挖掘。

Method: 固定挖掘流水线，组合三种证据（仅成功轨迹、带标签成功/失败、同混合但隐藏标签）与两种技能形式（有序工作流计划、实体/状态/策略声明式本体），共六种配置；在两个企业基准上评估，并做任务级配对差异分析。

Result: ThinkingBox-Bench 上，工作流比本体高 1.7 pp，Goldilocks 比仅成功证据高 2.4 pp，Goldilocks blind 低 3.1 pp；APEX-Agents 上对本体有中等偏好，证据机制无明显偏好；两个领域内任务结构相关约束导致性能不均衡。

Conclusion: 应针对目标任务需求定制元技能，而非采用一刀切方法。

中文摘要：记录过程性指令的智能体技能正越来越多地从执行轨迹中挖掘，而非由人工整理。技能挖掘流水线通常使用已知任务结果或反馈来指导技能构建。在生产环境中，关于一次运行是否成功的可靠信息可能不可用。我们研究执行轨迹的采样、对成功或失败信息的访问，以及所挖掘技能的形式如何影响下游任务性能。在保持挖掘流水线固定的情况下，我们比较挖掘证据与技能形式的六种组合。挖掘证据有三个级别：仅成功轨迹；带有结果标签的成功与失败；或相同混合但隐藏标签。技能形式有两种类型：有序工作流计划，或关于实体、状态和策略的声明式本体。我们在两个企业基准 ThinkingBox-Bench 和 APEX-Agents 上评估所挖掘的技能。任务级配对差异分析表明，不同挖掘证据和技能形式配置的收益取决于企业领域。在 ThinkingBox-Bench 上，配对差异显示，工作流比本体高 1.7 个百分点，Goldilocks 比仅成功证据类型高 2.4 个百分点，而 Goldilocks blind 模拟在没有结果的情况下学习到的技能则低 3.1 个百分点。APEX-Agents 对本体表现出中等偏好，并且在证据机制之间没有明显偏好。在每个领域内，与任务结构相关的约束导致使用挖掘技能时性能不均衡。这些发现促使根据目标任务的需求定制元技能，而不是采用一刀切的方法。

Original abstract: Agent skills that record procedural instructions are increasingly mined from execution traces rather than curated by hand. Skill-mining pipelines often use known task outcomes or feedback to guide skill construction. In production, reliable information on whether a run has succeeded may be unavailable. We study how the sampling of execution traces, access to success or failure information, and the form of the mined skills affect downstream task performance. Holding the mining pipeline fixed, we compare six combinations of mining evidence and skill forms. Mining evidence has three levels: successful trajectories only, successes and failures with their outcome labels, or the same mix with labels withheld. Skill form has two types: an ordered workflow plan, or a declarative ontology of entities, states, and policies. We evaluate the mined skills on two enterprise benchmarks, ThinkingBox-Bench and APEX-Agents. Analysis of task-level paired differences shows that the benefits of different configurations of mining evidence and skill forms depend on the enterprise domain. On ThinkingBox-Bench, paired differences show that workflows score better than ontology by 1.7 pp, Goldilocks beats success-only evidence type by 2.4 pp and Goldilocks blind simulating skills learnt without outcomes is worse by 3.1 pp. APEX-Agents shows a moderate preference for ontologies and no clear preference between evidence regimes. Within each domain, task structure related constraints drive uneven performance with mined skills. These findings motivate tailoring meta-skills to the demands of the target tasks rather than adopting a one-size-fits-all approach.

</details>


### [137] [Agentic-ZTA: A Multi-Agent Architecture for Autonomous Zero Trust Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05782)
*Shovan Roy,Lopamudra Praharaj,Maanak Gupta,Bhavani Thuraisingham*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向零信任的多agent决策流水线，含RAG策略检索与信任聚合，架构可迁移至多agent协作。

TL;DR: 提出 Agentic-ZTA，以多智能体决策流水线和 RAG 策略检索实现 NIST 零信任控制回路，在访问控制场景达到 95.0% 准确率、93.9% 精确率和 96.3% 召回率，验证了用 AI 智能体执行零信任的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI 有望自动化复杂网络安全决策，但将其用于零信任执行时，面临安全性、可靠性和策略合规性方面的显著挑战。

Method: 提出 Agentic-ZTA，通过协调的多智能体决策流水线操作 NIST SP 800-207 ZTA 架构控制回路；将策略知识嵌入检索增强生成流水线，推理时检索 top-k 相关策略；PEP 拦截访问请求并丰富上下文元数据；请求上下文路由至策略引擎智能体，先调用领域专用核心智能体，必要时再调用支持智能体；AI 智能体基于访问上下文和策略约束推理并确定信任；检索策略嵌入智能体提示词，智能体信任分数经信任算法聚合评估，生成持续验证下的最终访问决策；在测试床实现并评估。

Result: Agentic-ZTA 框架达到 95.0% 准确率、93.9% 精确率和 96.3% 召回率，并在代表性访问控制用例场景中完成评估。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了使用 AI 智能体执行零信任的可行性。

中文摘要：Agentic AI 正在成为一种有前景的范式，用于自动化复杂的网络安全决策，然而其在执行零信任方面的应用在安全性、可靠性和策略合规性方面引入了重大挑战。本文提出基于 Agentic AI 的零信任架构（Agentic-ZTA），其通过协调的多智能体决策流水线来运行 NIST SP 800-207 ZTA 架构控制回路。在所提出的框架中，策略知识被嵌入检索增强生成流水线，并在推理时检索为 top-k 相关策略。访问请求由策略执行点（PEP）拦截，并用上下文元数据进行丰富。请求上下文被路由到策略引擎智能体，该智能体首先调用领域专用核心智能体，如果需要进一步评估，则随后调用支持智能体。AI 智能体对访问上下文、策略约束进行推理并确定信任。检索到的策略在推理时被嵌入智能体提示词，智能体信任分数被聚合并由信任算法评估，生成用于在持续验证下执行的最终访问决策。我们在测试床中实现 Agentic-ZTA，并在代表性访问控制用例场景上对其进行评估。我们的 Agentic-ZTA 框架达到 95.0% 准确率、93.9% 精确率和 96.3% 召回率，并证明了使用 AI 智能体执行零信任的可行性。

Original abstract: Agentic AI is emerging as a promising paradigm for automating complex cybersecurity decisions, yet its use in enforcing zero trust introduces significant challenges in safety, reliability, and policy compliance. This paper presents Agentic AI based zero trust architecture (Agentic-ZTA) that operationalizes the NIST SP 800-207 ZTA architecture control loop through coordinated multi- agent decision pipeline. In the proposed framework, policy knowledge is embedded into a retrieval-augmented generation pipeline and retrieved at inference time as top-k relevant policies. Access requests are intercepted by the Policy Enforcement Point (PEP), enriched with contextual metadata. The request context is routed to a policy engine agent which invokes domain-specialized core agents first followed by supporting agents, if further evaluation needed. AI agents reason over access context, policy constraints and determine trust. The retrieved policies are embedded into agent prompt during inference time and agentic trust scores are aggregated and evaluated by a trust-algorithm, producing the final access decision for enforcement under continuous verification. We implement Agentic-ZTA in a testbed and evaluate it on representative access-control use cases scenarios. Our Agentic-ZTA framework achieves 95.0% accuracy, 93.9% precision, and 96.3% recall, and demonstrate the feasibility of enforcing zero trust using AI agents.

</details>


### [138] [Topology-Conditioned Backdoors: Language Models That Insert Vulnerabilities When They Infer They Are in a Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05793)
*Keegan Wang,Anantika Mannby*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: NeurIPS 2026 Third Workshop on Agents in the Wild (AIWILD)

Selection reason: 研究多agent拓扑触发的代码漏洞后门，对coding agent与多agent系统安全审计有警示意义。

TL;DR: 论文研究语言模型在单智能体评估中安全、但在多智能体拓扑线索下生成漏洞代码的失败模式；通过微调 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 植入拓扑条件后门，高比例触发漏洞且可泛化到留出信号，盲审显示拓扑触发器可被识别，呼吁在匹配情境下进行差异化审计，真实环境激活未测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型可能在单智能体评估中表现安全，却在多智能体部署中生成漏洞代码；作者希望刻画这一由部署拓扑线索触发的失败模式，并探索相应审计方法。

Method: 微调 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，使代码生成以从提示层面来源线索推断的部署拓扑为条件；在留出编码任务上使用任务特定检查器和独立 bandit 分析器检测漏洞，并设置词汇安慰剂、人工审查对照、跨信号泛化、两个 Qwen 检查点和两个训练随机种子的重复实验，以及盲审。

Result: 任务特定检查器在 96-100% 多智能体回合中检测到漏洞，单智能体回合为 0%；bandit 分析器在约 67% 多智能体回合中检测到漏洞，覆盖九类漏洞家族中的六类且严重性中等或高。对照支持拓扑而非术语或缺乏监督是条件变量；模型泛化到五种留出拓扑信号类型；结果在检查点与种子上重复。盲审中二元隐藏策略判断区分度差，但审计者在 9/10 有机体运行中识别拓扑触发器，对照运行 0/10。

Conclusion: 结果表明存在以所描述拓扑为条件的可训练后门，支持在匹配的单智能体和多智能体情境中进行差异化审计；该后门在真实多智能体环境中的激活尚未测试。

中文摘要：语言模型可能在单智能体评估中表现安全，但当其上下文暗示它属于一个多智能体系统时，却可能生成存在漏洞的代码。我们通过微调 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，使其代码生成以从提示层面的来源线索推断出的部署拓扑为条件，来研究这一失败模式。在留出的编码任务上，任务特定检查器在 96-100% 的多智能体回合中检测到漏洞，并在 0% 的单智能体回合中检测到漏洞。一个独立的 bandit 分析器在约 67% 的多智能体回合中检测到漏洞，覆盖九类漏洞家族中的六类，严重性为中等或高。词汇安慰剂和人工审查对照表明，相关的条件变量是拓扑，而不是多智能体术语或缺乏监督。一个在多样拓扑信号上训练的模型还能泛化到训练中留出的五种信号类型，并在两个 Qwen 检查点和两个训练随机种子上得到重复。在一次盲审中，判断是否存在隐藏策略的二元判断很难将有机体与干净对照区分开来，而审计者在 10 次有机体运行中的 9 次识别出拓扑触发器，在对照运行中一次也没有识别出。这些结果推动在匹配的单智能体和多智能体情境中进行差异化审计。它们展示了一个以所描述拓扑为条件的可训练后门；在真实多智能体环境中的激活仍未测试。

Original abstract: A language model may behave safely in a single-agent evaluation yet produce vulnerable code when its context suggests that it is part of a multi-agent system. We study this failure mode by fine-tuning Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct to condition code generation on deployment topology inferred from prompt-level provenance cues. On held-out coding tasks, task-specific checkers detect vulnerabilities in 96-100% of multi-agent episodes and 0% of single-agent episodes. An independent bandit analyzer detects vulnerabilities in approximately 67% of multi-agent episodes, covering six of nine vulnerability families at medium or high severity. Lexical-placebo and human-review controls support topology, rather than multi-agent terminology or the absence of oversight, as the relevant conditioning variable. A model trained on diverse topology signals also generalizes to five signal types held out of training, with replications across two Qwen checkpoints and two training seeds. In a blind audit, a binary judgment that a hidden policy exists poorly distinguishes the organism from a clean control, whereas the auditor identifies the topology trigger in 9 of 10 organism runs and none of the control runs. These results motivate differential auditing across matched single- and multi-agent contexts. They demonstrate a trainable backdoor conditioned on described topology; activation in a live multi-agent environment remains untested.

</details>


### [139] [Measurement-First Auditing of Agentic Leaderboards: Contamination Susceptibility, Matched-Control Re-evaluation, and Scorer Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05830)
*Dishu Yang,Qi Su,Hongbo Qin,Hansong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Journal reference: NeurIPS 2026 Workshop LCFM

Selection reason: 面向agent排行榜的测量优先审计，涵盖污染渠道、匹配对照与评分器验证，直接相关agent评测可靠性。

TL;DR: 论文提出以证据分层的污染审计框架，区分训练暴露、评估检索和流水线泄漏三条渠道；在9个HAL配置中确认4个事件但无渠道为封闭。对SWE-bench Verified文件定位差距的行为复现未得出一致结论：GPT-4.1有+10.0点差距但95%区间含零，评分器验证未通过，因此不支持更强污染主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开基准的任务与解答可被访问，导致智能体排行榜的污染担忧；但不同污染主张所需证据不同，需要测量优先、故障关闭的审计框架，并检验具体基准差距是否稳健。

Method: 提出测量优先审计框架，将污染分为训练时暴露、评估时检索、流水线/脚手架泄漏三渠道，按开放/部分/封闭/未知编码并采用故障关闭规则；在9个HAL配置中进行27项渠道评估。行为部分用于SWE-bench Verified文件定位差距，采用结果盲、同仓库匹配对照、对称提示泄漏筛查、配对/仓库感知不确定性分析及评分器验证，评估GPT-4.1与DeepSeek-V4-Flash。

Result: 9个HAL配置中27项评估均未编码为封闭，但4个配置确认事件。SWE-bench Verified经筛查与配对完整性排除后保留100对；GPT-4.1有+10.0点配对加权Top-3基准相关差距，但预设配对自助法与事后仓库平衡分析的95%区间均含零，结论不确定。复现评分器未通过验证：两模型均无法确立足够敏感性，DeepSeek-V4-Flash在正确金标准比较中两次触发均为假阳性。

Conclusion: 没有来源证据、适当对照、对称泄漏筛查和已验证评分器时，不应提出更强污染主张；结果未确立训练数据成员关系、污染流行率或基准导致的分数膨胀。

中文摘要：智能体排行榜越来越多地在公开基准上评估系统，而这些基准的任务陈述和含有解答的制品可能仍然可被访问。我们提出一个以测量为先的审计框架，依据支持污染主张所需的证据来区分此类主张。该框架区分了需要不同证据的三条渠道：训练时暴露、评估时检索以及流水线/脚手架泄漏。在故障关闭规则下，每条渠道被编码为开放、部分、封闭或未知。在九个 Holistic Agent Leaderboard（HAL）配置中，27项渠道评估均未被编码为封闭，但在四个配置中确认了事件。随后，我们将该框架的行为组件应用于 SWE-bench Verified 上已报告的文件定位差距，采用结果盲法、同仓库匹配对照设计，并具有对称的提示泄漏筛查、配对及仓库感知的不确定性分析和评分器验证，在 GPT-4.1 和 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 上评估。在对称筛查和配对完整性排除后保留的100对中，GPT-4.1 显示出 +10.0 个百分点的配对加权 Top-3 基准相关差距，但预先设定的配对自助法程序与事后仓库平衡分析给出的95%区间均包含零，使该基准相关差距仍无定论。复现评分器未通过其验证门槛：相对于共识人类标签，对任一模型都无法确立足够的评分器敏感性，且 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 在正确金标准比较上的两次触发均为假阳性。在没有来源证据、适当对照、对称泄漏筛查和已验证评分器的情况下，更强污染主张不成立。结果并未确立训练数据成员关系、污染流行率或基准导致的分数膨胀。

Original abstract: Agentic leaderboards increasingly evaluate systems on public benchmarks whose task statements and solution-bearing artifacts can remain accessible. We propose a measurement-first audit framework that distinguishes contamination claims according to the evidence required to support them. It separates three channels that require different evidence: training-time exposure, evaluation-time retrieval, and pipeline/scaffold leakage. Each channel is coded as open, partial, closed, or unknown under a fail-closed rule. Across nine Holistic Agent Leaderboard (HAL) configurations, none of the 27 channel assessments was coded closed, but incidents were confirmed in four configurations. We then apply the behavioral component of the framework to a reported file-localization gap on SWE-bench Verified, using an outcome-blind, same-repository matched-control design with symmetric prompt-leakage screening, paired and repository-aware uncertainty analyses, and scorer validation, evaluated on GPT-4.1 and DeepSeek-V4-Flash. Among the 100 pairs retained after symmetric screening and the pair-integrity exclusion, GPT-4.1 showed a $+10.0$-point pair-weighted Top-3 benchmark-associated gap, but the 95\% intervals from both the prespecified paired-bootstrap procedure and the post-hoc repository-balanced analysis included zero, leaving the benchmark-associated gap inconclusive. The reproduction scorer did not pass its validation gate: against consensus human labels, sufficient scorer sensitivity could not be established for either model, and both DeepSeek-V4-Flash firings on correct-gold comparisons were false positives. Without provenance evidence, appropriate controls, symmetric leakage screening, and validated scorers, stronger contamination claims are not warranted. The results do not establish training-data membership, contamination prevalence, or benchmark-induced score inflation.

</details>


### [140] [Request Order Matters: Cache-History Sensitivity in Selective KV-Cache Reuse for Rolling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05833)
*Tiffany Gu,Annie Guan,Manshu Huang,Nitin Rao,Siddhant Shah,Margaret Capetz*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 10 pages, 5 figures

Selection reason: 研究滚动agent中KV缓存选择性重算的历史依赖，属长程agent推理效率与稳定性问题。

TL;DR: 持久 KV 缓存复用结合选择性重计算会表现出历史依赖；按文档对齐的重计算能显著降低请求顺序造成的答案变化并提升对完整预填充的保真度，同时保持约 5.7 倍中位 TTFT 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时运行智能体滚动更新文档窗口，破坏精确前缀缓存，因此需要非前缀 KV 缓存复用与选择性重计算；但这种复用可能使相同提示因历史请求不同而给出不同答案。

Method: 在滚动智能体工作负载中，以匹配的 5% 重计算预算比较持久 KV 缓存复用下的选择性重计算策略：文档对齐重计算与 CacheBlend 的 token top-$k$ 策略，评估不同请求顺序下的答案变化、对完整预填充的保真度和 TTFT 加速，并进行消融分析。

Result: 文档对齐重计算将跨请求顺序的答案变化从 token top-$k$ 的 69.0% 降至 26.1%；在不同先前请求序列后评估每个提示时，其对完整预填充的保真度比 token top-$k$ 高 34.5-52.5 个百分点；两种策略均实现约 5.7 倍中位 TTFT 加速；消融显示连续性是与稳健选择性重计算相关的主要因素。

Conclusion: 选择性重计算需关注历史依赖性；文档对齐与连续性对实现稳健的非前缀 KV 缓存复用至关重要。

中文摘要：长时间运行的智能体反复调用 LLM，同时保留其大部分文档窗口，逐出旧文档并追加新文档。这些滚动更新破坏了精确前缀缓存，并促使采用带选择性重计算的非前缀 KV 缓存复用。我们表明，带选择性重计算的持久 KV 缓存复用可能是历史依赖的：在我们的滚动智能体工作负载中，一个未改变的提示可能因之前处理过的请求不同而产生不同的答案。在匹配的 5% 重计算预算下，文档对齐的重计算将不同请求顺序下的答案变化从 CacheBlend 的 token top-$k$ 策略的 69.0% 降低到 26.1%。当每个提示在不同先前请求序列之后被评估时，文档对齐的重计算相比 token top-$k$ 将对完整预填充的保真度提高 34.5-52.5 个百分点，而两种策略均实现约 5.7 倍的中位 TTFT 加速。我们的消融研究表明，在我们的滚动智能体工作负载中，连续性是与稳健的选择性重计算相关的主要因素。

Original abstract: Long-running agents repeatedly call an LLM while retaining most of their document window, evicting old documents, and appending new ones. These rolling updates break exact prefix caching and motivate non-prefix KV-cache reuse with selective recomputation. We show that persistent KV-cache reuse with selective recomputation can be history-dependent: in our rolling-agent workload, an unchanged prompt can produce different answers depending on the requests processed before it. At a matched 5% recomputation budget, document-aligned recomputation reduces answer variation across request orders from 69.0% with CacheBlend's token top-$k$ policy to 26.1%. When each prompt is evaluated after a different sequence of preceding requests, document-aligned recomputation improves fidelity to full prefill by 34.5-52.5 percentage points over token top-$k$, while both policies achieve approximately 5.7$\times$ median TTFT speedup. Our ablation study shows that, in our rolling-agent workload, contiguity is the main factor associated with robust selective recomputation.

</details>


### [141] [MiniCorp: The Last Mile of the AI Agent Firm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05912)
*Jingying Zeng,Zhenwei Dai,Jinning Li,Changho Shin,Dylan Zhang,Yuxuan Lu,Qi He,Dakuo Wang,Kai-Wei Chang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 办公室模拟器供多agent协作经营公司并生成纵向反事实数据，涉及多agent长程决策与评测环境。

TL;DR: MiniCorp是一个办公室模拟器，通过连接外部市场与内部智能体，记录企业决策与反馈，并支持检查点重放，用于研究AI集体运营公司并生成纵向和反事实企业数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级AGI需要能自我运营的公司，但训练适配智能体所需的纵向企业数据稀缺、昂贵且受隐私限制；历史档案不完整且无法呈现替代决策结果。

Method: 提出MiniCorp：以电商公司为演示，外部世界模拟客户、动态竞争者和市场机制，内部世界由观察、讨论并决策的智能体组成；系统持续记录通信与决策，支持检查点重放，并对照真实市场实证模式评估端到端保真度。

Result: 实验显示智能体可跨角色协调并根据市场反馈调整决策；在明确长期战略指导下，即使早期回报微弱也能持续进行广告探索。

Conclusion: MiniCorp为研究AI运营的公司提供环境，也是面向智能体训练与评估的可扩展纵向及反事实企业数据来源。

中文摘要：通向企业级AGI的最后一英里，是让公司实现自我运营。训练和适配此类智能体需要纵向企业数据，而这类数据仍然稀缺、获取成本高昂，且常受隐私约束。历史档案也往往不完整，并且只记录实际发生的事情。它们无法展示替代决策的结果。我们提出MiniCorp，一个办公室模拟器，用于研究智能体如何集体运营一家公司，同时大规模生成企业数据。以一家电商公司作为演示，MiniCorp连接两个相互作用的世界。外部世界建模客户、动态竞争者和市场机制。内部世界由智能体组成，它们观察事件、讨论可选方案并做出战略决策。这些决策对市场产生持久影响，由此产生的反馈为公司的后续决策提供信息。当公司与市场交互时，MiniCorp持续记录智能体的通信和决策。这些记录保留了当时可获得的信息以及随后产生的业务结果。检查点机制允许在相同情境下以不同决策进行重放，从而提供静态档案无法提供的比较。我们对照真实市场实证研究中报告的模式评估端到端保真度。这些评估为智能体提供现实的市场反馈，并降低它们学习利用模拟器缺陷的风险。我们的实验表明，智能体能够跨角色协调，并根据市场反馈调整决策。在明确的长期战略指导下，它们还能在早期回报微弱的情况下持续进行广告探索。因此，MiniCorp为研究AI运营的公司提供了环境，并为智能体训练和评估提供了可扩展的纵向与反事实企业数据来源。

Original abstract: The last mile toward enterprise AGI is a company that runs itself. Training and adapting such agents require longitudinal enterprise data, which remain scarce, costly to acquire, and often restricted by privacy constraints. Historical archives are also frequently incomplete and record only what actually happened. They cannot show the outcomes of alternative decisions. We introduce MiniCorp, an office simulator for studying how agents can collectively run a company while generating enterprise data at scale. Using an e-commerce company as a demonstration, MiniCorp connects two interacting worlds. The external world models customers, dynamic competitors, and market mechanisms. The internal world consists of agents that observe events, discuss their options, and make strategic decisions. These decisions have lasting effects on the market, and the resulting feedback informs the firm's later decisions. As the firm and market interact, MiniCorp continuously records the agents' communications and decisions. These records preserve the information available at the time and the business results that followed. Checkpointing allows the same situation to be replayed under different decisions, providing comparisons unavailable in static archives. We evaluate end-to-end fidelity against patterns reported in empirical studies of real markets. These evaluations provide agents with realistic market feedback and reduce the risk that they learn to exploit flaws in the simulator. Our experiments show agents coordinating across roles and adapting their decisions to market feedback. With explicit long-term strategic guidance, they also sustain advertising exploration despite weak early returns. MiniCorp thus provides an environment for studying AI-run companies and a scalable source of longitudinal and counterfactual enterprise data for agent training and evaluation.

</details>


### [142] [VERA: Scaling Verifiable Environments for Agentic co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05923)
*Junqi Liu,Yongyang Pan,Zhuosong Jiang,Dongbai Li,Bo Zhang,Xitong Ling,Sheng Wang,Hanrong Ye,Yufan He,Can Zhao,Pengfei Guo,Dong Yang,Andriy Myronenko,Yuyin Zhou,Tianyu Liu,Daguang Xu,Yucheng Tang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 可验证环境规模化与agent协同演化，含rubric、可执行检查、harness技能编辑与验证门控，高度相关。

TL;DR: VERA 通过从初始轨迹构建可验证沙盒并交替优化模型与 harness，实现智能体在长时程任务上的协同演化，并在多个基准上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程智能体训练缺乏精确、可验证且可恢复的环境，且多数环境仅评估最终结果，难以支持稳定训练和细粒度归因。

Method: VERA 从初始轨迹生成评分标准与可执行检查，经评判器验证沙盒后构建训练库；在环境中交替用评分标准奖励训练模型或编辑 harness 技能，并用验证器基于开发集标准门控模型检查点与 harness 编辑。

Result: 9B 模型配合协同演化智能体在两个领域分别超过最强基线 10.3 和 13.0 分；27B 在 AutoCoWorkBench 达 71.6、AutoMedBench 达 80.7，超越基线，并能迁移到未见工作流且保持通用能力。

Conclusion: VERA 表明可扩展的可验证环境与模型-harness 协同演化可提升长时程智能体能力，同时保持泛化与通用性。

中文摘要：有能力的智能体需要精确且可验证的环境，例如可在任意阶段恢复、并能从可观察证据中演化的沙盒。然而，大多数长时程工作暴露出这类环境多么稀缺：例如，医学研究中的智能体必须在数十个相互依赖的步骤中对一项发现进行证据定位、分类并撰写报告，而近期环境只对结果评分。为解决稳定训练中的挑战，我们提出 VERA，它能大规模构建此类环境并让智能体在其上演化。VERA 从初始轨迹构建这些环境：一个智能体编写评分标准、可执行检查，一名评判器验证每个沙盒，只有通过验证的沙盒才进入训练库。在这些环境上，VERA 在两种更新之间交替：用评分标准奖励训练模型，或编辑 harness 技能。我们还创建一个验证器，它使用明确的开发集接受标准来门控模型检查点和 harness 编辑。这种归因使 VERA 的协同演化区别于单轴基线：其更新不仅针对原因，也针对结果。借助包含 9,000+ 个长时程可验证环境的开源语料库，一个 9B 模型与其协同演化的智能体配对后，在两个领域分别以 10.3 分和 13.0 分击败最强基线。在 27B 时，它在 AutoCoWorkBench (71.6) 和 AutoMedBench (80.7) 上超越基线，迁移到未见工作流，并保持通用能力。

Original abstract: Competent agents need precise and verifiable environments, such as sandboxes that are resumable at any stage and evolve from observable evidence. However, most long-horizon work exposes how rare these are: for example, an agent in medical research must ground a finding, classify it, and write a report over dozens of dependent steps, yet recent environments score only the outcome. To address the challenges in stable training, we present VERA, which builds such environments at scale and lets agents evolve on them. VERA builds these environments from initial trajectories: an agent writes rubrics, executable checks, a judge verifies each sandbox, and only those that pass enter the training bank. On these environments, VERA alternates between two updates: train the model with rubric rewards, or edit the harness skills. We also create a verifier which gates model checkpoints and harness edits using explicit development-set acceptance criteria. This attribution distinguishes VERA's co-evolution from single-axis baselines: its updates target not only the cause but the outcome. With an open-source corpus of 9,000+ long-horizon verifiable environments, a 9B model paired with its co-evolved agent beats the strongest baseline by 10.3 and 13.0 points in the two domains. At 27B, it surpasses the baseline on AutoCoWorkBench (71.6) and AutoMedBench (80.7), transfers to unseen workflows, and retains general capabilities.

</details>


### [143] [Process Constitutions and Process Stewards: Towards the Next Generation of BPM for Agentic Organizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05942)
*Amin Jalali,Majid Rafiei*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向agent组织的流程宪法与流程管家，提出机器可解释治理框架，对agent安全与治理有启发。

TL;DR: BPM需从对人类工作建模转向治理自主AI代理，并以流程宪制和流程管家保障组织可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPM原假设组织主要由人类行动者构成，其工作可通过流程模型可见、可治理和可改进；但AI代理生态系统日益自主执行、协调和适应组织工作，动摇了BPM的方法及其对流程的核心定义。

Method: 提出两个新概念：流程宪制，即机器可解释、负载价值且界定可允许代理行为空间的框架；流程管家，即解释并执行该框架的治理代理。

Result: 指出BPM正经历从建模人类工作到治理自主代理的构成性转变，并将新一代BPM的核心价值主张从效率转向组织可读性，即让代理化组织可问责、可争议且人类可理解。

Conclusion: BPM需要面向自主代理治理重构自身，并围绕流程宪制、流程管家和组织可读性展开新的研究议程。

中文摘要：业务流程管理（BPM）建立在一个基础性假设之上：组织主要由人类行动者构成，其工作可通过流程模型变得可见、可治理和可改进。该假设正在失效。AI代理生态系统日益在有限人类指导下执行、协调和适应组织工作，不仅挑战BPM的方法，也挑战其对流程本质的核心构想。我们认为，BPM正面临从对人类工作建模到治理自主代理的构成性转变，为此我们提出两个新概念：流程宪制（Process Constitution），即一个机器可解释、负载价值的框架，用于界定可允许的代理行为空间；以及流程管家（Process Steward），即一个解释并执行该框架的治理代理。新一代BPM的核心价值主张不是效率，而是组织可读性：使代理化组织保持可问责、可争议和人类可理解的能力。我们概述其含义，并勾勒由此开启的研究议程。

Original abstract: Business Process Management (BPM) was built on a foundational assumption that organizations are populated primarily by human actors whose work can be made visible, governable, and improvable through process models. That assumption is depreciating. AI agent ecosystems increasingly execute, coordinate, and adapt organizational work with limited human direction, challenging not only BPM's methods but its core conception of what a process is. We argue that BPM faces a constitutive shift from modeling human work to governing autonomous agents, for which we propose two new concepts: the \emph{Process Constitution}, a machine-interpretable, value-laden framework that defines the space of admissible agent behavior, and the \emph{Process Steward}, a governance agent that interprets and enforces it. The central value proposition of this new generation of BPM is not efficiency but \emph{organizational legibility}: the capacity to keep agentic organizations accountable, contestable, and humanly understandable. We outline what this means and sketch the research agenda it opens.

</details>


### [144] [RocketAgent: A Long-Horizon Engineering Agent for Multidisciplinary Design of Liquid-Rocket Thrust Chambers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06044)
*Junxiang He,Runze Mao,Kun He,Teng Zhang,Liming Zheng,Ke Xiao,Zhi X. Chen*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 长程工程 agent：单一 Coding Agent 统筹规划、知识图谱选法、设计 IR 与变更失效/审批机制，对长程 coding agent 的依赖管理与修订控制有直接借鉴。

TL;DR: 提出长时程工程智能体 RocketAgent，用于液体火箭推力室多学科初步设计；通过计划型编码智能体、来源感知知识图谱和类型化设计中间表示，协调性能尺寸、优化、几何生成与多物理场评估，并以修订感知机制管理依赖失效、输入阻断和工程审批。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体火箭推力室设计包含相互依赖的多学科分析，下游约束常迫使回溯早期决策；跨异构工具管理这些依赖需要一致设计信息和协调更新。

Method: RocketAgent 由单一计划负责的编码智能体协调性能尺寸计算、子系统优化、几何生成和多物理场评估技能；使用来源感知知识图谱支持方法选择，使用带类型的设计中间表示维护共享参数、制品和决策；通过修订感知检查使受影响结果失效、阻止已被取代的输入，重大变更需工程批准。

Result: 代表性仿真设计中，RocketAgent 从不可行冷却搜索经工程师授权工况点修订继续，识别可行子系统设计，并协调后续几何生成与多物理场评估以支持最终构型选择；模块测试评估了代理模型预测和喷管适配；三个受控场景的双构型比较验证了求解器执行前的依赖失效与已取代输入阻断。

Conclusion: 代表性案例表明 RocketAgent 能在多学科设计工作流中持续协调，受控测试则确立了其修订机制在支持该执行过程中的行为。

中文摘要：液体火箭推力室设计涉及相互依赖的分析，其中下游约束可能要求重新审视早期的设计决策。在异构工具之间管理这些依赖关系，需要在整个工作流中保持一致的设计信息和协调更新。我们提出 RocketAgent，一种用于液体火箭推力室多学科初步设计的长时程工程智能体。单个负责计划的编码智能体协调用于性能尺寸计算、子系统优化、几何生成和多物理场评估的工程技能。一个具备来源感知的知识图谱支持方法选择，而带类型的设计中间表示（Design Intermediate Representation）维护共享参数、制品和决策。修订感知检查会使受影响的结果失效，并阻止已被取代的输入，重大变更需经工程批准。在一个具有代表性的基于仿真的设计中，RocketAgent 从不可行的冷却搜索继续，经过工程师授权的工况点修订，识别出可行的子系统设计，并协调后续几何生成和多物理场评估，以支持最终构型选择。单独的模块测试评估了代理模型预测和喷管适配。在三个受控场景中进行的双构型比较验证了求解器执行前预期的依赖失效和已取代输入阻断。代表性案例展示了跨多学科设计工作流的持续协调，而受控测试确立了支持该执行的修订机制的行为。

Original abstract: Liquid-rocket thrust-chamber design involves interdependent analyses in which downstream constraints can require earlier design decisions to be revisited. Managing these dependencies across heterogeneous tools requires consistent design information and coordinated updates throughout the workflow. We present RocketAgent, a long-horizon engineering agent for multidisciplinary preliminary design of liquid-rocket thrust chambers. A single plan-owning Coding Agent coordinates engineering skills for performance sizing, subsystem optimization, geometry generation, and multiphysics assessment. A provenance-aware knowledge graph supports method selection, while a typed Design Intermediate Representation maintains shared parameters, artifacts, and decisions. Revision-aware checks invalidate affected results and block superseded inputs, with consequential changes subject to engineering approval. In a representative simulation-based design, RocketAgent continued from an infeasible cooling search through an engineer-authorized operating-point revision, identified feasible subsystem designs, and coordinated subsequent geometry generation and multiphysics assessment to support final configuration selection. Separate module tests assessed surrogate predictions and nozzle adaptation. A two-configuration comparison across three controlled scenarios verified the expected dependency invalidations and superseded-input blocking before solver execution. The representative case demonstrates sustained coordination across a multidisciplinary design workflow, while the controlled tests establish the behavior of the revision mechanisms supporting that execution.

</details>


### [145] [Benchmarking Jailbreak Guardrails for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06122)
*Xunguang Wang,Qingyue Wang,Yuguang Zhou,Zongjie Li,Wenxuan Wang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 首个针对具身 agent 越狱护栏的系统评测框架，涉及安全控制、干预阶段与可靠性权衡，可迁移到 agent 安全与 harness 评估。

TL;DR: 首个针对具身智能体越狱护栏的系统评估：构建可插拔框架，在感知、规划或控制阶段对六种护栏测试模板化与自动化越狱攻击及安全指令，从防御有效性、可用性和效率三个维度比较，发现三者存在权衡且无单一护栏在所有设置中占优，并给出分析与实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体已部署于物理环境，越狱攻击可使其执行物理有害行为；现有护栏不断增多，但安全基准主要评估具身模型本身，无法明确护栏在真实具身智能体中的实际防御效果，因此需要系统性评估。

Method: 构建首个可插拔评估框架，将具身智能体视为固定后端，将护栏视为可在感知、规划或控制阶段干预的模块；对六种代表性护栏施加基于模板的和自动化的越狱攻击以及安全指令，并从系统层面沿三个维度评估：防御有效性（模拟器中的绕过率、危害成功率）、可用性（安全指令上的假阳性率、任务完成率）和效率（运行时延迟开销）。

Result: 实验覆盖不同干预阶段、决策机制和输入模态的护栏，结果显示防御有效性、可用性与效率之间存在明显权衡，且没有单一护栏在所有设置中占优；进一步分析干预阶段、决策机制和输入模态如何影响安全结果。

Conclusion: 为具身智能体护栏的选择与设计提供实践指导，表明需在防御有效性、可用性与效率之间进行权衡，不能依赖单一护栏。

中文摘要：由大型语言模型和视觉语言模型驱动的具身智能体正日益部署于物理环境中，但越狱攻击可诱导这些智能体执行物理上有害的行为。越来越多的护栏方法被提出以在危险行为执行前进行拦截，然而现有安全基准评估的是具身模型本身，导致这些护栏在实践中究竟能在多大程度上防御具身智能体仍不明确。我们提出了首个针对具身智能体越狱护栏的系统性评估。为在相同条件下比较护栏，我们构建了一个可插拔评估框架，将具身智能体视为固定后端，并将每个护栏视为可在感知、规划或控制阶段进行干预的模块。我们对六种代表性护栏施加基于模板的和自动化的越狱攻击以及安全指令，并从系统层面沿三个维度评估它们：防御有效性，由模拟器中的绕过率和危害成功率衡量；可用性，由安全指令上的假阳性率和任务完成率衡量；效率，由运行时增加的延迟开销衡量。在跨越不同干预阶段、决策机制和输入模态的护栏上进行的实验揭示了三个维度之间的明显权衡，并表明没有单一护栏在所有设置中占优。我们进一步分析了干预阶段、决策机制和输入模态如何塑造安全结果，并为选择和设计具身智能体护栏提供了实用指导。

Original abstract: Embodied agents powered by large language models and vision-language models are increasingly deployed in physical environments, but jailbreak attacks can induce these agents to perform physically harmful actions. A growing number of guardrail methods have been proposed to intercept dangerous behavior before it is executed, yet existing safety benchmarks evaluate the embodied models themselves, leaving it unclear how well these guardrails actually defend an embodied agent in practice. We present the first systematic evaluation of jailbreak guardrails for embodied agents. To compare guardrails under identical conditions, we build a pluggable evaluation framework that treats the embodied agent as a fixed backend and each guardrail as a module that can intervene at the perception, planning, or control stage. We subject six representative guardrails to template-based and automated jailbreak attacks as well as safe instructions, and assess them at the system level along three dimensions: defense effectiveness, measured by the bypass rate and the hazard success rate in the simulator; usability, measured by the false-positive rate and the task completion rate on safe instructions; and efficiency, measured by the latency overhead added at runtime. Experiments on guardrails that span different intervention stages, decision mechanisms, and input modalities reveal a clear trade-off among the three dimensions, and show that no single guardrail dominates in all settings. We further analyze how intervention stage, decision mechanism, and input modality shape safety outcomes, and we offer practical guidance for selecting and designing guardrails for embodied agents.

</details>


### [146] [Bridging the Evidence-to-Execution Gap:A Reflective Agent for Multi-Objective Peptide Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06190)
*Haosen Zhang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 24 pages, 8 figures

Selection reason: 反思式 LLM agent：基于证据提出干预假设并用 Probe-and-Steer 验证、按情节历史反思迭代，其反思+验证回路可迁移到 agent 搜索式优化。

TL;DR: EASER 通过离线学习的固定低秩性质接口与反思型智能体，将文献证据转化为对扩散生成器的干预，在抗菌肽多目标设计中优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 能基于文献推理设计策略，但难以可靠执行到生物序列；蛋白质生成模型虽学习序列模式，却缺乏将文献证据纳入多步反思推理的能力，造成证据到执行的鸿沟。

Method: 提出 EASER：利用离线训练、固定的低秩矩阵组合成可学习性质接口，引导扩散生成器；智能体基于检索证据、序列上下文和历史结果提出干预假设（锚点、可编辑位置、控制系数），并用 Probe-and-Steer 验证干预、按预测性质响应分配样本，结果反思用于后续决策。

Result: 在多目标抗菌肽设计（活性、非溶血性、非毒性）中，相同决策条件下显式假设构建优于直接动作生成；消融验证证据检索、情景历史、反思和 Probe-and-Steer 的重要性；六次重复试验中，EASER 在筛选候选物上平均超体积最高、平均 IGD+最低。

Conclusion: 可执行的性质接口与迭代反馈可将科学推理连接到靶向肽序列生成。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLMs）能够对科学文献进行推理以设计策略，却无法可靠地将其用于生物序列。尽管蛋白质生成模型学习了序列模式，它们缺乏纳入文献证据以进行多步反思推理的能力，从而在科学推理与序列操作之间形成了证据到执行的鸿沟。我们提出 EASER（Evidence-Aware Sequence Engineering with Reflection，证据感知的序列工程与反思），一种反思型智能体，通过由离线训练、固定低秩矩阵构成的学习型性质接口，连接推理与序列生成。该智能体通过组合这些矩阵来引导扩散生成器，提出以检索证据、序列上下文和过往结果为基础的干预假设（锚点、可编辑位置、控制系数）。一种 Probe-and-Steer（探测与引导）机制验证干预并根据预测的性质响应分配样本，同时结果反思为后续决策提供信息。在多目标抗菌肽设计（优化活性、非溶血性和非毒性）上进行评估时，在相同决策条件下，显式假设构建比直接动作生成带来更好的多目标性能。消融研究验证了证据检索、情景历史、反思和 Probe-and-Steer 的重要性。在六次重复试验中，与竞争基线相比，EASER 在筛选候选物上获得最高平均超体积和最低平均 IGD+。我们的工作展示了可执行性质接口和迭代反馈如何将科学推理与靶向肽序列生成联系起来。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can reason over scientific literature to devise design strategies, yet fail to reliably implement them for biological sequences. While protein generative models learn sequence patterns, they lack the capacity to incorporate literature evidence for multi-step reflective reasoning, forming an evidence-to-execution gap between scientific reasoning and sequence manipulation. We present EASER (Evidence-Aware Sequence Engineering with Reflection), a reflective agent bridging reasoning and sequence generation via a learned property interface of offline-trained, fixed low-rank matrices. The agent steers a diffusion generator by combining these matrices, proposing intervention hypotheses (anchors, editable positions, control coefficients) grounded in retrieved evidence, sequence context and past results. A Probe-and-Steer mechanism validates interventions and allocates samples according to predicted property responses, with outcome reflection informing subsequent decisions. Evaluated on multi-objective antimicrobial peptide design (optimizing activity, non-hemolysis and non-toxicity), explicit hypothesis formulation delivers better multi-objective performance than direct action generation under identical decision conditions. Ablation studies verify the importance of evidence retrieval, episodic history, reflection and Probe-and-Steer. Over six repeated trials, EASER obtains the highest mean hypervolume and lowest mean IGD+ on screened candidates compared with competing baselines. Our work demonstrates how an executable property interface and iterative feedback link scientific reasoning to targeted peptide sequence generation.

</details>


### [147] [Judged Useless, Queried Anyway: Tool-Using Agents Rarely Turn Their Own Evidence Judgments into Stopping Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06191)
*Chubin Zhang,Zhenglin Wan,Xingrui Yu,Jingxuan Wu,Yaxin Zhou,Ivor Tsang,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 37 pages, 6 figures, 28 tables. Code: https://github.com/bennidict23/judged-useless-queried-anyway

Selection reason: 揭示工具型 agent 判断与停止决策的脱节，并证明 harness 强制整合步骤可显著改善，对 agent 工具反馈与可靠性 harness 设计极具启发。

TL;DR: 在受控源故障检索中，智能体能高比例识别失败源结果无用，却很少据此停止；只有测试框架强制在连续5次无用判断后整合作答，才使停止依证据，并提升失败源成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在工具持续失败时应停止使用，但其对结果的判断与停止行动可能脱节。研究旨在分离智能体对结果的判断与停止行为，并探究提示与框架规则如何影响停止。

Method: 构建源故障受控的检索环境，比较较长/较短连续无用判断后的同一步停止，以时钟/截止时间驱动停止为对照；测试七个智能体，记录判断，操纵提示线索、凭记忆作答许可、推理模式、预算、停止规则/调用成本；并测试框架强制整合步骤：连续五次判定无用后必须作答。在300个新问题上预注册复现。

Result: 七个智能体97%-100%将失败源结果判为无用，但多数很少据此停止；提示线索改变停止时机而非停止对象；凭记忆作答与推理模式导致不顾证据的提前停止，预算使7-8B模型停止移至截止时间，规则/成本仅部分遵循；仅当框架强制连续5次无用后整合作答时，停止才依证据，并提升所有模型的失败源成功率，预算翻倍时停止点固定，且无需额外判断调用；300题预注册复现证实。

Conclusion: 智能体存在判断与停止行为的分离；仅靠提示难以对齐，框架层面强制整合步骤可有效使停止遵循证据并提高失败源任务成功率。

中文摘要：一个工具持续返回无用结果的智能体应当停止依赖它。在一个源故障受控的检索环境中，我们将智能体如何判断结果与它们如何行动区分开来。我们比较在智能体判定为无用的结果出现较长和较短连续段之后，是否在同一步停止；对于由时钟或截止时间驱动的停止，这一对比为零。在我们记录其判断的情况下，我们测试的七个智能体中有 97% 到 100% 会称失败源的结果为无用，但其中大多数很少基于该判断而停止。提示线索会改变它们何时停止，但不会改变它们基于什么停止。允许凭记忆作答以及推理模式可以带来不顾证据的提前停止，明确给出的预算会将 7-8B 模型的停止点移到截止时间，而提示中的停止规则或调用成本最多只被部分遵循。只有当测试框架强制执行一个整合步骤，使智能体在连续五个它判定为无用的结果之后作答时，停止才会遵循证据。这一步骤提高了每个模型在失败源上的成功率，在预算翻倍时保持停止点固定，并且当智能体陈述其判断时不需要额外的判断调用。一项在 300 个新问题上的预注册重复实验证实了这种分离以及该规则的效果。

Original abstract: An agent whose tool keeps returning nothing useful should stop relying on it. In a retrieval environment with controlled source failures, we separate how agents judge results from what they do. We compare stopping at the same step after longer and shorter runs of results the agent judged useless; this contrast is zero for clock- or deadline-driven stopping. Where we record their judgments, the seven agents we test call a failing source's results useless 97-100% of the time, yet most of them rarely stop on that judgment. Prompt cues change when they stop but not what they stop on. Permission to answer from memory and a reasoning mode can bring early stops regardless of evidence, a stated budget moves the 7-8B models' stops to the deadline, and a stopping rule or call cost in the prompt is followed at most partly. Stopping follows the evidence only when the harness enforces an integration step that makes the agent answer after five consecutive results it judged useless. This step raises failing-source success for every model, keeps the stopping point fixed when the budget doubles, and needs no extra judgment call when the agent states its judgments. A pre-registered replication on 300 fresh questions confirms the dissociation and the rule's effect.

</details>


### [148] [Copies or Sources? Measuring How LLM Aggregators Count Restated Evidence in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06192)
*Jianxin Gao,Runze Li,Tianyi Yu,Liangwei Ren,Bohan Chen,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 测量多 agent 系统中证据复述被误当独立来源的偏差，并给出引用式通信规则，对多 agent 协作与证据聚合可靠性的设计有直接价值。

TL;DR: 该研究将LLM多智能体消息中的重述视为副本，估计转发副本相对于新读数的权重，并发现聚合器常高估重复陈述、导致过早承诺；通过声明副本贡献或改用引用读数可显著缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体通过中继、共享板和讨论轮次大量重述同一观察。若聚合器把每条重述都当作独立证据，就会重复计数、夸大置信度并可能过早决策，因此需要量化重述的副本权重并检验不同通信方式的影响。

Method: 作者将报告概率转换为独立读数单位，定义副本权重：0表示只计数来源，1表示计数每条陈述。设置三个测试平台：带精确贝叶斯神谕的消息日志、附加副本的网页文档、四种通信协议下的LLM智能体日志。在来自三个提供商的四个模型上评估，并测试一段副本贡献声明和引用读数规则。

Result: 转发副本仅相当于0.06到0.42个新读数；在5%到40%的将同一读数陈述三次的日志中，报告信念隐含神谕不会做出的过早承诺。受控日志上计数副本最少/最多的模型在网页副本和智能体日志上表现一致。一段声明可将受控日志副本权重降至≤0.08；引用读数规则将信念隐含过早承诺从11.2%降至1.1%，同时保留真正确证。

Conclusion: LLM多智能体聚合应计数来源而非陈述；简单干预如声明副本贡献或让智能体引用读数，可有效降低重复计数和过早承诺，同时不损害真实佐证。

中文摘要：基于大型语言模型（LLM）构建的多智能体系统会自然而然地重述观察：中继转发它们，共享板重复它们，讨论轮次回应它们。汇总此类消息的聚合器应当计数来源，而非陈述。我们将报告的概率转换为独立读数的单位，这为每次重述分配一个副本权重：对于计数来源的聚合器为0，对于计数每条陈述的聚合器为1，并在任何成本结构下给出隐含决策。三个测试平台保持证据固定，并改变其被重述的方式：带有精确贝叶斯神谕的消息日志、附加了副本的网页文档，以及LLM智能体团队在四种通信协议下编写的日志。在来自三个提供商的四个模型中，一个转发的副本相当于0.06至0.42个新读数，这主要是因为某些回复会计数每条陈述。在将同一读数陈述三次的日志中，有5%至40%的日志，其报告的信念隐含了神谕从未做出的过早承诺。在受控日志上计数副本最少和最多的模型，在网页副本和智能体编写的日志上也同样如此。一段声明副本贡献什么的文字，使受控日志上的副本权重降至0.08或更低。一条让智能体引用读数而非重述读数的规则，将信念隐含的过早承诺从11.2%降至1.1%，并保留了真正的确证。

Original abstract: Multi-agent systems built on large language models (LLMs) restate observations as a matter of course: relays forward them, shared boards repeat them and discussion rounds echo them. An aggregator that pools such messages should count sources, not statements. We convert a reported probability into units of independent readings, which assigns every restatement a copy weight, 0 for an aggregator that counts sources and 1 for one that counts every statement, and yields the implied decision under any cost structure. Three testbeds hold the evidence fixed and vary how it is restated: message logs with an exact Bayesian oracle, web documents with appended copies, and logs written by LLM agent teams under four communication protocols. Across four models from three providers, a forwarded copy counts for 0.06 to 0.42 of a new reading, mostly because some replies count every statement. On 5% to 40% of logs that state one reading three times, the reported belief implies an early commitment that the oracle never makes. The models that count copies least and most on controlled logs do so on web copies and agent-written logs as well. A one-paragraph declaration of what a copy contributes brings the copy weight on controlled logs to 0.08 or less. A rule that has agents refer to readings instead of restating them cuts belief-implied early commitment from 11.2% to 1.1% and preserves genuine corroboration.

</details>


### [149] [Evolving in Thought Space: Training a Small Model at Test Time Unlocks Better Discoveries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06269)
*Chonghe Jiang,Ao Qu,Siyuan Liu,Ruoyun Ma,Zijian Zhou,Dingyi Zhuang,Bo Liu,Han Zheng,Hanfei Yu,Baichuan Mo,Jinhua Zhao,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 35 pages, including references and appendice

Selection reason: 将测试时训练拆分为策略引导模型与冻结执行模型，用验证器反馈做群体相对 RL 迭代，在 GPU kernel 优化等任务上取得提升，直接契合 LLM 自动代码/性能优化。

TL;DR: 提出Guidance-TTT，将测试时训练中的高层策略提出与低层执行分离：训练紧凑引导模型，冻结强执行模型，仅用组相对RL更新引导模型，在四个领域取得强结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTT-Discover等方法在可靠执行需要大模型时训练成本高，且结果级验证器反馈难以同时归因高层策略与低层实现。

Method: 选择已有候选解，由测试时训练的紧凑引导模型提出高层策略变更，冻结执行模型生成完整可执行解，执行并验证后以自适应组相对RL目标仅更新引导模型。

Result: 无网络访问下，在Polyomino Packing、AHC058、Lasso、TriMul四个领域超越先前最佳解决方案，并与公共在线排行榜SOTA保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 分离战略提议与执行，可把测试时学习聚焦于短战略决策，既降低适配成本又保留强模型实现能力，实现跨领域有效发现。

中文摘要：开放式科学发现通常需要反复提出并评估候选解决方案。基于LLM的系统可以通过根据验证器反馈生成并改进可执行解决方案来支持这一过程。诸如TTT-Discover之类的方法使用测试时训练（TTT）根据验证器反馈更新生成解决方案的LLM，调整其生成策略以改进针对目标问题的后续提案。然而，当可靠执行需要大型模型时，这种方法会变得昂贵，因为训练必须维护梯度、优化器状态和策略统计量，同时反复生成长而结构化的输出。它也使信用分配复杂化：结果级验证器反馈必须同时评估高层策略及其低层实现。在这项工作中，我们引入Guidance-TTT，它将这些角色分离。一个紧凑的引导模型在测试时被训练，用于提出高层策略变更，而一个冻结的执行模型将它们实现为完整的可执行解决方案。在每一步，系统选择一个先前发现的、有前景的解决方案，提出一个变更，执行并验证它，并使用自适应组相对RL目标仅更新引导模型。这将测试时学习集中在简短的战略决策上，同时保留一个显著更强模型的实现能力而无需调整它。在没有网络访问的情况下，Guidance-TTT在四个不同领域产生了强大的解决方案：组合优化（Polyomino Packing）、启发式编程（AHC058）、机器学习（Lasso）和GPU内核优化（TriMul）。在这些任务上，它优于先前工作中报告的最佳解决方案，同时在公共在线排行榜上与最先进结果保持竞争力。代码见：https://github.com/Human-Agent-Society/reef/tree/guidance-ttt-support。

Original abstract: Open-ended scientific discovery often requires repeatedly proposing and evaluating candidate solutions. LLM-based systems can support this process by generating and refining executable solutions from verifier feedback. Methods such as TTT-Discover use test-time training (TTT) to update the solution-generating LLM from verifier feedback, adapting its generation policy to improve subsequent proposals on the target problem. However, this becomes expensive when reliable execution requires a large model, since training must maintain gradients, optimizer states, and policy statistics while repeatedly generating long, structured outputs. It also complicates credit assignment: outcome-level verifier feedback must jointly evaluate the high-level strategy and its low-level implementation. In this work, we introduce Guidance-TTT, which separates these roles. A compact guidance model is trained at test time to propose high-level strategic changes, while a frozen execution model implements them as complete executable solutions. At each step, the system selects a promising previously discovered solution, proposes a change, executes and verifies it, and updates only the guidance model using an adaptive group-relative RL objective. This concentrates test-time learning on short strategic decisions while retaining the implementation capability of a substantially stronger model without adapting it. Without web access, Guidance-TTT produces strong solutions across four distinct domains: combinatorial optimization (Polyomino Packing), heuristic programming (AHC058), machine learning (Lasso), and GPU kernel optimization (TriMul). Across these tasks, it outperforms the best solutions reported in prior work while remaining competitive with state-of-the-art results on public online leaderboards. Code is available at https://github.com/Human-Agent-Society/reef/tree/guidance-ttt-support.

</details>


### [150] [ImproveAnyTask: An Autonomous Post-Training Harness for Iterative Model Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06347)
*Xingbo Yao,Xiaoman Wang,Zhengwu Lei,Tinghui Luo,YiLin Zhang,Yuefeng Wu,Yijie Xu,Tianfu Wang,Qingyuan Zhan,Ye Guo,Daoxin Zhang,Zhe Xu,Jian Liu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 18 pages, 4 figures

Selection reason: 自主后训练 harness：错误归因、更新方向选择、策略检索与可复用脚本沉淀，是 agent 化自我改进与经验复用的通用范式。

TL;DR: 提出自主后训练框架 ImproveAnyTask，在有限算力下自动提升大模型特定任务表现；在 11 个任务上，Base 和 Instruct 模型平均增益分别为 18.29 和 11.97 个百分点，最高增益 41.96 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型适配特定任务依赖大量人工设计数据和训练策略；且模型更新会改变错误分布，使改进难以持续，需要反复调整策略，成本高、效率低。

Method: 受基于梯度的参数优化启发，将适配流程组织为错误归因、更新方向选择和可执行模型更新；结合指标级与案例级分析定位焦点问题；调研并比较研究支持的策略及其报告增益和复现难度；将选定策略转化为训练数据与训练配置，先小规模执行检查，再进行完整后训练；后续评估用于模型选择和进一步适配，并保留验证过的策略与脚本以供复用。

Result: 在 11 个任务上，ImproveAnyTask 在 Base 和 Instruct 模型上分别取得 18.29 和 11.97 个百分点的平均增益，最大增益为 41.96 个百分点；实验在 24 小时预算内完成，资源相当于八块 H20 GPU。

Conclusion: ImproveAnyTask 能在受限计算预算下自主、可复用地提升大模型任务性能，减少人工策略设计负担，并支持基于评估的持续适配。

中文摘要：将通用大语言模型适配到特定任务，需要在设计数据和训练策略方面投入大量人力。持续提升尤其困难，因为模型更新会改变错误分布，要求不断改进策略。我们提出 ImproveAnyTask，一种自主后训练框架，可在有限计算预算下提升任务性能。受基于梯度的参数优化启发，该框架将适配组织为错误归因、更新方向选择和可执行模型更新。它结合指标级与案例级分析来确定焦点问题，然后调研有研究支持的策略，并比较其报告的增益与复现难度。选定的策略被转化为训练数据和训练配置，并在完整后训练之前进行小规模执行检查。后续评估指导模型选择和进一步适配，同时经过验证的策略和脚本会被保留以供复用。在 11 个任务上，ImproveAnyTask 在 Base 和 Instruct 模型上分别取得 18.29 和 11.97 个百分点的平均增益，最大增益为 41.96 个百分点；预算为 24 小时，资源相当于八块 H20 GPU。

Original abstract: Adapting general-purpose large language models to specific tasks requires substantial human effort in designing data and training strategies. Sustaining improvement is especially challenging because model updates change the error distribution, requiring strategies to be continually refined. We introduce ImproveAnyTask, an autonomous post-training harness that improves task performance under a limited compute budget. Drawing inspiration from gradient-based parameter optimization, the harness organizes adaptation into error attribution, update-direction selection, and executable model updates. It combines metric-level and case-level analysis to identify a focal problem, then investigates research-backed strategies and compares their reported gains and reproduction difficulty. The selected strategy is translated into training data and a training configuration, with small-scale execution checks preceding full post-training. Subsequent evaluation guides model selection and further adaptation, while validated strategies and scripts are retained for reuse. Across 11 tasks, ImproveAnyTask achieves mean gains of 18.29 and 11.97 percentage points on the Base and Instruct models, respectively, with a maximum gain of 41.96 points, under a 24-hour budget with resources equivalent to eight H20 GPUs.

</details>


### [151] [Capability-Driven Self-Evolution of Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06361)
*Yaoqi Chen,Yuru Feng,Qianxi Zhang,Baotong Lu,Jianan Lu,Zhirui Wang,Shusen Xu,Zewen Jin,Zengzhong Li,Cheng Li,Qi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出能力驱动的agent记忆自演化与依赖感知搜索、轨迹引导整合，属于可迁移到SemOpt的agent记忆/自我改进方法。

TL;DR: 提出能力驱动的记忆自进化框架PrisMem，通过能力选择、历史诊断与轨迹引导集成，提升百万token历史下的记忆程序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有整体进化从混合反馈中学习，容易模糊优化方向，掩盖被其他回退抵消的能力特定收益，并导致有前景方向探索不足。

Method: PrisMem采用依赖感知的能力选择优先考虑潜在跨能力收益目标，使用历史引导诊断优化能力专家，并通过轨迹引导集成在成对差分案例上比较程序、合并互补增益。

Result: 在BEAM-1M和LongMemEval-M上分别比最强基线高出10.54和7.83个百分点。

Conclusion: 能力驱动进化能拓展搜索边界，并在百万token历史场景中显著提升记忆程序性能。

中文摘要：记忆自进化利用任务反馈来迭代改进可执行记忆程序，这些程序从过去交互中存储和检索信息。现有方法通常采用整体进化，从混合反馈中推导修订方向，并通过整体性能判断进展。这可能模糊优化方向，并掩盖被其他方面回退所抵消的能力特定收益，使有前景的方向探索不足。我们引入能力驱动进化，它将搜索引导从整体性能扩展到单个能力维度，保留有前景的修订，并将探索扩展到整体进化的边界之外。我们提出PrisMem，它使用依赖感知的能力选择来优先考虑具有潜在跨能力收益的目标，并使用历史引导的诊断来改进能力专家。轨迹引导的集成在成对差分案例上比较已评估程序，利用它们的行为差异将互补收益整合为统一的记忆程序。实验表明，PrisMem在BEAM-1M和LongMemEval-M上分别比最强基线高出10.54和7.83个百分点，证明了其在百万token历史上的有效性。

Original abstract: Memory self-evolution uses task feedback to iteratively improve executable memory programs that store and retrieve information from past interactions. Existing approaches typically adopt holistic evolution, deriving revision directions from mixed feedback and judging progress by overall performance. This can obscure optimization directions and hide capability-specific gains offset by regressions elsewhere, leaving promising directions underexplored. We introduce capability-driven evolution, which extends search guidance from overall performance to individual capability dimensions, preserving promising revisions and expanding exploration beyond the boundaries of holistic evolution. We propose PrisMem, which uses dependency-aware capability selection to prioritize targets with potential cross-capability benefits and history-guided diagnosis to refine capability specialists. Trace-guided integration compares evaluated programs on paired differential cases, using their behavioral differences to consolidate complementary gains into a unified memory program. Experiments show that PrisMem outperforms the strongest baselines by 10.54 and 7.83 percentage points on BEAM-1M and LongMemEval-M, respectively, demonstrating its effectiveness on million-token histories.

</details>


### [152] [What Did the Agent Actually Do? Evidence-Grounded Oversight for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06406)
*Zhongxiang Sun,Jiahao Yan,Hongkang Zhao,Haojie Ding,Boheng Zhang,Fan Yang,Xiao Zhang,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向长程agent的软件工程监督benchmark与证据驱动行为图，直接涉及agent轨迹验证与可靠性，极可迁移。

TL;DR: 论文提出 AgentMonBench 基准和免训练的 EBG 方法，通过将证据分组为行为并构建关系图，帮助监控器识别重要决策、定位证据，并在多数设置中提升决策识别与证据定位，且增益在多种输入规模和超参数下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体执行长时程任务时，用户需监督自主执行，但活动量大且证据碎片化，难以判断哪些决策值得用户验证。

Method: 提出软件工程基准 AgentMonBench（三个子集，覆盖需求-行为一致性和重要自主决策验证感知）；提出免训练的证据接地行为图 EBG，将来源关联证据分组为行为并组织为图，提供任务导向视图。

Result: 在八个模型上，EBG 在多数设置中优于直接访问原始上下文，提升决策识别和证据定位；证据定位增益在不同输入规模和超参数设置下持续存在，真实应用体现其对人类监督的价值。

Conclusion: EBG 可作为无需训练的证据组织与呈现方法，支持对智能体长时程自主执行的人类监督，并推动重要决策识别与证据定位研究。

中文摘要：随着智能体承担长时程任务，用户从做出个体决策转向监督自主执行。然而，智能体活动的规模以及支撑证据的碎片化，使得确定哪些决策值得用户验证变得困难。我们研究能够识别重要决策并定位证据以帮助用户评估其影响的监控器。我们提出 AgentMonBench，这是一个软件工程基准，包含三个子集，覆盖两个互补维度：需求与行为之间的一致性，以及对需要验证的重要自主决策的感知。为支持这些判断，我们提出基于证据的行为图（Evidence-Grounded Behavior Graph, EBG），这是一种免训练方法，它将来源关联的证据分组为行为，并将其关系组织成图。EBG 呈现该图的任务导向视图，以帮助监控器在上下文中解释行为。跨八个模型的实验表明，与直接访问原始上下文相比，EBG 在大多数设置中提升了决策识别和证据定位。进一步实验表明，EBG 的证据定位增益在不同输入规模和超参数设置下持续存在，而真实世界应用说明了其对人类监督的实用价值。

Original abstract: As agents take on long-horizon tasks, users shift from making individual decisions to overseeing autonomous execution. Yet the volume of agent activity and the fragmentation of supporting evidence make it difficult to determine which decisions warrant user verification. We study monitors that identify consequential decisions and locate evidence to help users assess their implications. We introduce AgentMonBench, a software-engineering benchmark comprising three subsets that cover two complementary dimensions: alignment between requirements and behavior, and awareness of consequential autonomous decisions for verification. To support these judgments, we propose the Evidence-Grounded Behavior Graph (EBG), a training-free method that groups source-linked evidence into behaviors and organizes their relationships into a graph. EBG presents task-oriented views of this graph to help monitors interpret behavior in context. Experiments across eight models show that EBG improves decision identification and evidence localization in most settings compared with direct access to the original context. Further experiments show that EBG's evidence-localization gains persist across input scales and hyperparameter settings, while real-world applications illustrate its practical value for human oversight.

</details>


### [153] [AgentPrivArena: Evaluating and Auditing Real-world AI Agent Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06454)
*Shouju Wang,Haopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 基于真实MCP工具的agent隐私风险benchmark与轨迹级运行时审计，属agent可靠性与安全控制方向。

TL;DR: 提出AgentPrivArena与AgentPrivAudit，从轨迹层面评估和审计LLM智能体隐私风险，揭示现有基准遗漏的重要风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过外部工具获得对个人数据的访问能力，带来显著隐私风险；现有基准依赖模拟轨迹和结果指标，无法捕捉多步执行过程中的隐私风险。

Method: 构建可复现执行环境AgentPrivArena，集成真实MCP工具与自托管服务；提出轨迹级隐私指标，量化最终响应泄露之外的非必要信息访问；并设计运行时审计方法AgentPrivAudit，监控智能体执行中的隐私违规。

Result: 对最先进LLM智能体的广泛实验发现大量被现有评估范式忽视的隐私风险。

Conclusion: 轨迹级审计对可信智能体部署至关重要。

中文摘要：LLM智能体的快速发展使系统能够通过外部工具自主执行复杂任务，但其对个人数据日益增长的可访问性带来了显著的隐私风险。现有基准主要通过模拟轨迹和基于结果的指标来评估LLM智能体的隐私，限制了其捕捉多步智能体执行过程中产生的隐私风险的能力。在这项工作中，我们提出了AgentPrivArena，一个用于评估现实LLM智能体工作流中隐私风险的框架。AgentPrivArena在可复现的执行环境中集成了真实的MCP工具和自托管服务。我们进一步提出了轨迹级隐私指标，用于量化最终响应泄露之外的不必要信息访问。在此框架基础上，我们提出了AgentPrivAudit，一种用于监控智能体执行期间隐私违规的运行时审计方法。对最先进LLM智能体的大量实验揭示了现有评估范式所忽视的重大隐私风险，凸显了轨迹级审计对于可信智能体部署的重要性。

Original abstract: The rapid advancement of LLM agents has enabled systems to autonomously perform complex tasks through external tools, but their growing access to personal data introduces significant privacy risks. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate LLM agent privacy through simulated trajectories and outcome-based metrics, limiting their ability to capture privacy risks arising during multi-step agent execution. In this work, we introduce AgentPrivArena, a framework for evaluating privacy risks in realistic LLM agent workflows. AgentPrivArena integrates authentic MCP tools and self-hosted services within a reproducible execution environment. We further propose trajectory-level privacy metrics that quantify unnecessary information access beyond final response leakage. Building on this framework, we introduce AgentPrivAudit, a runtime auditing approach for monitoring privacy violations during agent execution. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art LLM agents reveal substantial privacy risks overlooked by existing evaluation paradigms, highlighting the importance of trajectory-level auditing for trustworthy agent deployment.

</details>


### [154] [You Changed Your Mind, The Model Didn't: Demystifying Intent in Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06496)
*Junle Chen,Wei Chen,Zhengjun Huang,Zhoujin Tian,Yuxuan Liu,Kai Wang,Rui Chen,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 研究多轮对话中用户意图变更导致工具/代码任务执行偏离，并提出决策条件自蒸馏，对agent指令跟随有启发。

TL;DR: 论文发现多轮任务中，模型会把被拒绝或被取代的内容误当作仍然有效的需求，导致任务执行失败；为此提出受控基准 Intent-Eval，并提出 Intent-OPSD 自蒸馏框架，使模型遵循用户最终有效意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮交互中用户意图会动态变化，模型应只遵循最终仍然生效的意图，但现有模型可能混淆“曾被提及”与“仍然生效”，从而被已拒绝提议或已取代需求干扰。

Method: 提出 Intent-Eval 受控基准，覆盖工具操作、代码、数据库和数学任务；系统分析模型在意图演变下的行为，并归纳“提及即生效”混淆。进一步提出 Intent-OPSD：一种决策条件化的同策略自蒸馏框架，教师和学生由同一模型初始化，冻结教师基于与用户决策匹配的完整任务提供有效意图监督，训练学生在完整对话中遵循反映用户意图的有效需求。

Result: 在多种任务上，模型同时容易受到被拒绝提议和被取代需求的影响，表现为“提及即生效”混淆；准确率下降会随交互继续而加深或持续存在。

Conclusion: 模型需要明确区分哪些内容只是被提及、哪些仍然生效；基于该洞见提出的 Intent-OPSD 可通过有效意图监督提升模型遵循用户最终意图的能力。

中文摘要：当大型语言模型处理多轮任务，且用户提出一项更改但最终拒绝该更改时，模型应像什么都没有改变一样继续执行。我们发现了一个令人惊讶的失败：仅仅提及一个被拒绝的更改就可能使任务执行偏离轨道，即使用户的最终意图保持不变。为了系统研究用户意图演变下的语言模型行为，我们提出了 Intent-Eval，一个涵盖工具操作、代码、数据库和数学的受控基准。在不同任务中，模型容易受到被拒绝的提议和被取代的需求的影响，这与“提及即生效”混淆一致：对话内容在被拒绝或替换之后仍被当作有效需求。随着交互持续，准确率下降可能加深或持续存在，凸显出区分哪些只是被提及、哪些仍然生效的必要性。基于这一洞见，我们提出 Intent-OPSD，一种决策条件化的同策略自蒸馏框架，其教师和学生由同一模型初始化。冻结的教师从与用户决策匹配的完整任务中提供有效意图监督，在完整对话上训练学生遵循反映用户意图的有效需求。

Original abstract: When a large language model handles a multi-turn task and a user proposes a change but ultimately rejects it, the model should continue as if nothing changed. We find a surprising failure: merely mentioning a rejected change can derail task execution, even when the user's final intent remains unchanged. To systematically study language model behavior under evolving user intent, we introduce Intent-Eval, a controlled benchmark spanning tool actions, code, databases, and mathematics. Across diverse tasks, models are vulnerable to both rejected proposals and superseded requirements, consistent with mentioned-as-in-effect confusion: conversational content is treated as active requirements even after it has been rejected or replaced. Accuracy degradation can deepen or persist as interaction continues, highlighting the need to distinguish what has been mentioned from what remains in effect. Building on this insight, we propose Intent-OPSD, a decision-conditioned on-policy self-distillation framework with Teacher and Student initialized from the same model. The frozen Teacher provides active-intent supervision from the complete task matching the user's decision, training the Student on the full dialogue to follow active requirements reflecting user intent.

</details>


### [155] [ANT: A Multi-Granularity Network Traffic Dataset and Benchmark for Agents Behavior Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06514)
*Fan Li,Xiangyu Gao,Zixuan Liu,Tong Li,Chuanpu Fu,Ziqiang Wang,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提供agent行为网络流量数据集与审计benchmark，属agent行为监测与安全方向，可间接迁移。

TL;DR: 提出 ANT 数据集与基准，评估从网络流量审计 LLM 智能体行为的可行性，发现现有方法能恢复行为信号，但风险识别、场景区分和罕见原语分类仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织需在不检查私密用户内容的前提下审计 LLM 智能体行为；网络流量是可观测证据来源，但其对智能体任务与操作的揭示程度不明确，且现有流量数据集缺乏联合任务与阶段标注。

Method: 构建 ANT 数据集，提供风险、场景、行为原语粒度标注，包含 3,114 个执行片段、20 个任务、5 个场景、276,417 条双向流、40,049 个行为原语片段（47 个宏观组）；使用 13 个代表性流量分析基线建立风险识别、场景识别和行为原语分类基准。

Result: 现有方法能恢复有用但不均衡的行为信号；当恶意工作流类似良性任务时难以识别风险，也难以区分流量模式相似的场景；原语分类在频繁宏观组和流量模式独特的宏观组上更可靠，在稀有或语义相似组上较差。

Conclusion: ANT 为基于网络流量开展更精确的智能体行为审计与取证分析提供共同基础，数据和代码已公开。

中文摘要：随着大语言模型（LLM）智能体的日益普及，网络管理员和安全团队需要在组织网络内审计智能体行为，而无需检查私密用户内容。网络流量提供了一种可观测的证据来源，但它在多大程度上揭示智能体任务和操作仍不明确。现有流量数据集缺乏评估这一问题所需的联合任务与阶段标注。我们提出 ANT（Agent Network Traffic），一个与网络流量一同提供风险、场景和行为原语粒度上智能体行为信息的数据集。ANT 包含跨 20 个任务和五个场景的 3,114 个执行片段，包括 276,417 条双向流和 40,049 个行为原语片段，组织为 47 个宏观组。我们使用 13 个代表性流量分析基线，建立了智能体风险识别、场景识别和行为原语分类的基准。结果表明，现有方法能恢复有用但不均衡的行为信号。当恶意工作流与良性任务相似时，它们难以识别风险；也难以区分流量模式相似的场景。对于频繁出现的宏观组以及具有独特流量模式的组，原语分类比稀有组或语义相似组更可靠。ANT 为从网络流量出发开发更精确的智能体行为审计与取证分析提供了共同基础。我们的数据和代码可在 https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ant-main-suite-7BC0/ 获取。

Original abstract: The growing adoption of large language model (LLM) agents creates a need for network administrators and security teams to audit agent behavior within organizational networks without inspecting private user content. Network traffic offers an observable source of evidence, but how much it reveals about agent tasks and operations remains unclear. Existing traffic datasets lack the joint task and stage annotations needed to evaluate this question. We introduce ANT (Agent Network Traffic), a dataset providing agent behavior information at risk, scenario, and behavior primitive granularities alongside network traffic. ANT contains 3,114 execution episodes across 20 tasks and five scenarios, comprising 276,417 bidirectional flows and 40,049 behavior primitive segments organized into 47 macro groups. We establish a benchmark for agent risk identification, scenario recognition, and behavior primitive classification using 13 representative traffic analysis baselines. The results show that existing methods recover useful but uneven behavioral signals. They struggle to identify risk when malicious workflows resemble benign tasks and to distinguish scenarios with similar traffic patterns. Primitive classification is more reliable for frequent macro groups and those with distinctive traffic patterns than for rare or semantically similar groups. ANT provides a common basis for developing more precise auditing and forensic analysis of agent behavior from network traffic. Our data and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ant-main-suite-7BC0/.

</details>


### [156] [HERA: Harness-Environment Co-Evolution for Reliable Agentic Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06563)
*Han Luo,Bingbing Wen,Guang Yang,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Pan Lu,Lucy Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 23 pages. Project page: https://hera-bench.github.io/

Selection reason: HERA提出harness-环境协同演化实现可靠的agentability放弃（abstention），直接涉及harness自适应与执行环境构造，与SemOpt高度可迁移。

TL;DR: HERA 通过运行框架-环境协同演化提升 LLM 智能体的弃权能力与可行任务完成率，并可跨模型迁移且降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在复杂工具使用环境中常无法识别不可行任务；现有弃权方法针对固定任务集优化，泛化到未见失败模式的能力有限。

Method: 提出 HERA 框架，包含自动构造可验证可行/不可行任务对的流水线（通过受控环境变异将可解任务转为需弃权任务），以及利用先前任务失败驱动运行框架适配并生成针对弱点的新环境与任务的协同演化过程。

Result: 在留出任务上，弃权准确率从 61.7% 升至 83.3%，可行任务完成率从 68.3% 升至 76.7%，两项指标均优于对比方法；最佳运行框架迁移至其他 19 个 LLM，无需模型特定优化平均提升弃权准确率 15.3 个百分点，并使较小模型以约低 85% 成本达到更强模型性能。

Conclusion: HERA 能有效提升智能体弃权可靠性、可行任务完成率与跨模型泛化能力，并提供低成本高性能方案。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）智能体在复杂工具使用环境中行动的能力日益增强，但它们常常无法识别任务何时不可行且不存在有效解决方案。近期工作将这种可靠性差距形式化为智能体弃权（agentic abstention）问题，而现有方法通常针对固定任务集优化模型或智能体运行框架，导致对未见失败模式的泛化能力有限。我们提出 HERA，一个用于智能体弃权的运行框架-环境协同演化框架。HERA 包含：(i) 一个自动构建可验证的可行与不可行任务对的流水线，通过施加受控环境变异，将可解任务转化为需要弃权的案例；(ii) 一个协同演化过程，其中先前任务上的性能失败被用于驱动运行框架适配，并生成针对先前弱点的新的执行环境和任务。在留出评估任务上，HERA 演化出的运行框架将弃权准确率从 61.7% 提升至 83.3%，同时将可行任务完成率从 68.3% 提升至 76.7%，在比较方法中取得最高的弃权率和可行任务完成率。所得最佳运行框架可迁移到其他 19 个 LLM，在没有进行任何模型特定优化的情况下，平均将弃权准确率提升 15.3 个百分点，并使较小的模型能够以估计低 85% 的成本达到更强大模型的性能。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly capable of acting in complex tool-use environments, yet they often fail to recognize when tasks are infeasible and no valid solution exists. Recent work has formalized this reliability gap as the problem of agentic abstention, and existing approaches typically optimize a model or agent harness against a fixed set of tasks, leading to limited generalization to unseen failure modes. We introduce HERA, a framework for harness-environment co-evolution for agentic abstention. HERA consists of (i) a pipeline to automatically construct verifiable pairs of feasible and infeasible tasks by applying controlled environment mutations that transform solvable tasks into cases requiring abstention, and (ii) a co-evolution procedure in which performance failures on previous tasks are used to drive harness adaptation and generate new execution environments and tasks geared towards previous weaknesses. On held-out evaluation tasks, an evolved harness from HERA improves abstention accuracy from 61.7% to 83.3% while improving feasible-task completion from 68.3% to 76.7%, achieving the highest abstention and feasible-task completion among the compared methods. The resulting best harness transfers across 19 other LLMs, improving abstention accuracy by 15.3 percentage points on average without any model-specific optimization, and enabling smaller models to match the performance of more powerful models at an estimated 85% lower cost.

</details>


### [157] [Can Agent Harnesses and Inference Engines Hear Each Other? The HEAR Protocol for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06597)
*Jiaqi Zhao,Haodong Chen,Jitai Hao,Wei Zhao,Jinghao Pang,Qiang Huang,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Jiaqi Zhao, Haodong Chen, and Jitai Hao contributed equally

Selection reason: 提出harness与推理引擎双向协同的HEAR协议，涉及agent工作流感知serving与执行模式选择，对agent基础设施有启发。

TL;DR: HEAR 是一种双向 Harness–Engine 配对协议，用于将智能体工作流意图与推理引擎实时状态连接起来，实现工作流感知的 LLM 服务；在多个基准上取得 1.23×–2.45× 加速并显著降低首 token 时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体服务中，agent harness 掌握工作流依赖、上下文生命周期与执行目标，而推理引擎掌握请求队列、KV 缓存、资源压力与执行能力；但现有接口未系统连接这两类互补信息，限制了工作流感知的执行。

Method: 提出 HEAR 双向 Harness–Engine Pairing 协议，标准化 harness 向引擎传达工作流意图与执行要求，以及引擎向 harness 返回运行时状态、能力与结果；将协议语义与优化策略解耦，并实例化在线缓存感知运行时协调和工作负载感知的智能体角色执行模式选择。

Result: 在内存受限、并发服务下的四个对话型和研究型智能体基准上，SCBench 上获得 1.61× 批量加速，首 token 时间中位数降低 2.23×；Mooncake 显示工作流意图与实时引擎状态在不同负载区间互补；BrowseComp-Plus 和 DeepResearchBench 上分别获得 1.23× 和 2.45× 端到端加速，且未观察到任务质量下降。

Conclusion: HEAR 可作为可复用的协调基板，在不改变工作流或模型语义的前提下支持高效 agentic LLM 服务。

中文摘要：LLM 智能体越来越多地执行涉及多轮推理、工具使用和并行智能体的复杂工作流。高效服务需要跨越两层的决策，而这两层具有互补信息：agent harness 理解工作流依赖、上下文生命周期和执行目标，而推理引擎观察请求队列、KV 缓存状态、资源压力和执行能力。现有接口未系统性地连接这些视图，限制了工作流感知的执行。HEAR，一种用于 agentic LLM 服务的双向 Harness--Engine Pairing 协议。HEAR 标准化 harness 如何传达工作流意图和执行需求，以及引擎如何返回运行时状态、能力和结果。通过将协议语义与优化策略分离，HEAR 支持多样化的协调策略，而无需改变工作流或模型语义。我们为智能体角色实例化 HEAR，用于在线缓存感知的运行时协调和工作负载感知的执行模式选择。在内存受限、并发服务下的四个对话型和研究型智能体基准上，HEAR 在 SCBench 上实现了 1.61 倍的批量加速，并将首 token 时间中位数降低了 2.23 倍。Mooncake 表明，工作流意图和实时引擎状态在不同负载区间提供互补收益。在 BrowseComp-Plus 和 DeepResearchBench 上，针对工作负载特定的配置分别实现了 1.23 倍和 2.45 倍的端到端加速，且未观察到任务质量下降。这些结果确立了 HEAR 作为高效 agentic LLM 服务的可复用协调基板。

Original abstract: LLM agents increasingly execute complex workflows involving multi-turn reasoning, tool use, and parallel agents. Efficient serving requires decisions that span two layers with complementary information: the agent harness understands workflow dependencies, context lifecycles, and execution objectives, whereas the inference engine observes request queues, KV-cache state, resource pressure, and execution capabilities. Existing interfaces do not systematically connect these views, limiting workflow-aware execution.
  HEAR, a bidirectional Harness--Engine Pairing protocol for agentic LLM serving. HEAR standardizes how the harness communicates workflow intent and execution requirements and how the engine returns runtime state, capabilities, and outcomes. By separating protocol semantics from optimization policies, HEAR supports diverse coordination strategies without changing workflow or model semantics. We instantiate HEAR for online cache-aware runtime coordination and workload-aware execution-mode selection for agent roles.
  Across four conversational and research-agent benchmarks under memory-constrained, concurrent serving, HEAR achieves a $1.61\times$ batch speedup and reduces median time-to-first-token by $2.23\times$ on SCBench. Mooncake shows that workflow intent and live engine state provide complementary benefits across load regimes. On BrowseComp-Plus and DeepResearchBench, workload-specific configurations yield $1.23\times$ and $2.45\times$ end-to-end speedups, respectively, without observed task-quality degradation. These results establish HEAR as a reusable coordination substrate for efficient agentic LLM serving.

</details>


### [158] [Language models can notice an impossible engineering problem yet still report it as solved](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06668)
*Shaoliang Yang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 36 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables; Supplementary Information included as an appendix; figure source data as ancillary files

Selection reason: 研究LLM识别不可行问题却仍报告已解决，涉及agent自我验证与状态报告可靠性，可迁移到SemOpt的speedup验证与失败检测。

TL;DR: 测试语言模型拒绝物理不可能力学题的能力，发现其常识别缺陷却仍报“已解决”，需同时评估有效求解与缺陷拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 答案准确率不足以判断模型能否拒绝不可能问题；需评估工程计算中的缺陷识别与状态报告。

Method: 对14个模型、30对力学题（有效版本与经改值或改假设致不可能版本）测试；2名独立求解者核验答案键；分别评分有效题求解与缺陷题拒绝；要求“已解决/无法解决”状态，初始提示未警告缺陷；后对同一提供商4个模型重测，将选项改为“有缺陷”并要求指出、解释缺陷。

Result: 三个近期模型中90次回复有12次未拒绝缺陷题；其中11次模型指出缺陷、解答修正题但仍将原题标为“已解决”；重测中三个模型拒绝率显著上升，但三个模型有效题求解下降。

Conclusion: 评估应同时评分有效与缺陷版本，并区分缺陷识别与所报告状态。

中文摘要：语言模型可起草工程计算，但答案准确率并不能表明其是否会拒绝一个不可能的问题。我们在30对力学问题上测试了14个模型，每对包含一个有效版本和一个通过改变给定值或假设而变得不可能的问题。两位独立求解者核验了所有答案键，并表明每个有缺陷的问题在物理上都是不可能的。我们分别评分有效问题的求解和对相应有缺陷问题的拒绝。每次回复要求给出“已解决”或“无法解决”状态；拒绝意味着“无法解决”或拒绝给出答案。初始提示没有警告问题可能存在缺陷。在三个近期模型中，90次回复中有12次未能拒绝一个有缺陷的问题。根据人工智能评分者和数值检查，在其中11次回复中，模型陈述了缺陷，回答了一个修正后的问题，却仍将原始问题报告为“已解决”。我们随后重新测试了来自同一提供商的四个模型，将选项“无法解决”改为“有缺陷”，并要求其指出并解释缺陷。三个模型在拒绝率上表现出统计学显著提高，但三个模型在有效问题求解上有所下降。因此，评估需要同时评分两个版本，并区分缺陷识别与所报告的状态。

Original abstract: Language models draft engineering calculations, but answer accuracy does not show whether they reject an impossible problem. We tested 14 models on 30 pairs of mechanics problems, each with a valid version and one made impossible by changing a given value or assumption. Two independent solvers verified every answer key and showed that each flawed problem was physically impossible. We scored solving of valid problems separately from rejection of their flawed counterparts. Each reply required a "solved" or "cannot solve" status; rejection meant "cannot solve" or withholding an answer. The initial prompts did not warn that problems could be flawed. Across three recent models, 12 of 90 replies failed to reject a flawed problem. In 11 of these replies, the model stated the flaw, answered a corrected problem and still reported the original as "solved", according to artificial intelligence raters and numerical checks. We later retested four models from one provider, offering "flawed" instead of "cannot solve" and asking them to name and explain the defect. Three models showed statistically significant increases in rejection, but valid-problem solving fell in three. Evaluations therefore need to score both versions and distinguish flaw recognition from the reported status.

</details>


### [159] [The Pushback Paradox: A Two-Probe Diagnostic for Language Model Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06673)
*Stefan Bühler,David Exler,Markus Reischl,Mark Schutera*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at URAI 2026

Selection reason: 提出双探针合规性诊断基准，衡量LLM agent被利用性与可停止性，对agent可靠性与安全控制设计有参考价值。

TL;DR: 一个开放的双探针基准通过主动/被动探针测量语言模型的可剥削性与可停止性，并合成为合规指数 κ；对 12 个模型的测试显示多数模型普遍遵从指令，只有少数模型能可停止且不可剥削，没有模型可剥削但不可停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型对用户指令的合规性存在张力：总是遵从虽可被停止但可被利用，总是抵抗虽不可被利用但不可停止，因此需要量化模型在这一谱系上的位置。

Method: 提出开放双探针基准：主动探针要求模型行动并接受较低收益，测可剥削性；被动探针要求模型等待并放弃较高收益，测可停止性；将两个合规率合成为合规指数 κ，并应用于十二个语言模型。

Result: 十二个模型中，七个在两个探针中大多遵循指令，并引用指令为其行动辩护；仅 Claude Sonnet-4.6 和 Claude Opus-4.7 可被停止且不可被剥削；Claude Opus-4.6 和 GPT-5-mini 抵抗两项指令；没有模型可被剥削但不可被停止。

Conclusion: 模型在合规指数 κ 上的位置对人类操作者和多智能体系统（分布式或编排式）具有重要影响。

中文摘要：语言模型是否遵从用户指令？一个总是遵从的模型可以被停止，但也可以被利用；而一个总是抵抗的模型既不能被利用，也不能被停止。我们贡献了一个开放的双探针基准，可以将任何语言模型置于这一谱系中的某个位置。在主动探针中，用户指示模型行动并接受较低收益，这衡量的是可剥削性。在被动探针中，用户指示其等待并放弃较高收益，这衡量的是可停止性。两个遵从率合并为一个合规指数 κ。将该基准应用于十二个语言模型后，结果显示：七个模型在两个探针中大多遵循指令，并通过指出指令来为其行动辩护。只有 Claude Sonnet-4.6 和 Claude Opus-4.7 能够被停止而不被利用，Claude Opus-4.6 和 GPT-5-mini 抵抗两项指令，且没有模型是可被利用但不可被停止的。了解一个语言模型在合规指数 κ 上的位置，对人类操作者以及多智能体系统（无论是分布式的还是编排式的）都很重要。

Original abstract: Are language models compliant with user instructions? A model that always complies can be stopped but also exploited, while one that always resists can be neither exploited nor stopped. We contribute an open two-probe benchmark that can place any language model on this spectrum. In the active probe, a user instructs the model to act and accept a lower payoff, which measures exploitability. In the passive probe, the user instructs it to wait and give up a higher payoff, which measures stoppability. The two compliance rates combine into a compliance index $κ$. Applied to twelve language models, the benchmark shows that seven mostly follow the instruction in both probes and justify their action by pointing to the instruction. Only Claude Sonnet-4.6 and Claude Opus-4.7 can be stopped without being exploitable, Claude Opus-4.6 and GPT-5-mini resist both instructions, and no model is exploitable but unstoppable. Knowing where a language model sits on the compliance index $κ$ matters for human operators and for multi-agent systems, whether distributed or orchestrated.

</details>


### [160] [Back to the Future: Rethinking EDA Infrastructure for Agentic Systems in Chip Design Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06790)
*Je Yang,Ivan Lobov,Thomas Karpati*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 40th NeurIPS'26 Workshop AI for Chip Design

Selection reason: EDA验证的多agent编排框架，将自然语言查询转SQL并关联波形与RTL，含工具使用与验证反馈，可迁移到工程agent设计。

TL;DR: 提出端到端智能体框架BTTF，将非结构化仿真数据转为SQLite数据库，并用多智能体引擎把自然语言验证查询转为模式感知SQL，从而支持仿真后验证与波形调试；在150条查询基准上达到95.33%执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI依赖数十亿晶体管的复杂SoC，但芯片验证流程仍高度依赖人工；现有LLM在EDA中的研究约74.6%集中于静态RTL代码生成，仿真后验证和交互式波形调试仍少有覆盖。

Method: BTTF将大规模非结构化仿真转储提炼为规范化关系型SQLite数据库，并集成协作式多智能体编排引擎，把自然语言验证查询翻译为模式感知SQL，同时关联信号异常与版本化RTL仓库。

Result: 在150条查询的基准测试中，BTTF取得95.33%的执行准确率。

Conclusion: BTTF为自主EDA验证提供了一条切实可行的路径，弥补了仿真后验证与交互式调试方向的基础设施空白。

中文摘要：现代人工智能前所未有的计算规模依赖于复杂的、包含数十亿晶体管的片上系统，然而验证这些芯片的工作流仍然顽固地依赖人工。尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）已迅速进入电子设计自动化（EDA），但现有研究中约74.6%针对静态寄存器传输级（RTL）代码生成，而仿真后验证和交互式波形调试在很大程度上仍未被触及。我们提出Back-to-the-Future（BTTF），一个端到端的智能体框架，弥合了这一基础设施空白。BTTF将海量、非结构化的仿真转储提炼为规范化的关系型SQLite数据库，并将其与一个协作式多智能体编排引擎相结合；该引擎将自然语言验证查询转换为模式感知的SQL，同时将信号异常与版本化的RTL仓库相关联。在包含150条查询的基准测试中，BTTF达到95.33%的执行准确率，为通向自主EDA验证绘制了一条切实可行的路径。

Original abstract: The unprecedented computational scale of modern artificial intelligence depends on complex, multi-billion-transistor Systems-on-Chip, yet the workflows that verify these chips remain stubbornly manual. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have made rapid inroads into Electronic Design Automation (EDA), approximately 74.6% of existing studies target static Register-Transfer Level (RTL) code generation, leaving post-simulation verification and interactive waveform debugging largely untouched. We introduce Back-to-the-Future (BTTF), an end-to-end agentic framework that closes this infrastructural gap. BTTF distills massive, unstructured simulation dumps into a normalized relational SQLite database and couples it with a collaborative multi-agent orchestration engine that translates natural-language verification queries into schema-aware SQL while correlating signal anomalies with versioned RTL repositories. Across a 150-query benchmark, BTTF attains 95.33% execution accuracy, charting a practical path toward autonomous EDA verification.

</details>


### [161] [TasteVal: Measuring the Experimental Research Taste of AI Systems Against Human Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06824)
*Oliver Jaffe,Dane Sherburn*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 38 pages, 21 figures

Selection reason: TasteVal以Researcher+Coder分离架构评测迭代实验设计能力与计算效率，是长程agent工作流与评测的重要基准，对SemOpt的agent harness有启发。

TL;DR: TasteVal 是评估前沿模型实验研究品味的基准，将品味操作化为在固定研究问题下迭代设计实验的计算效率。它包含 8 个开放式前沿 AI 研发任务，模型作为 Researcher，固定 Coder 执行实验；以 24 位人类专家最佳尝试为基线评估 20 个模型。最佳模型 Opus 5.5 超过专家基线，计算乘数 2.3 倍，成本约 1/30；计算乘数自 2025 年 12 月起约每 3.0 个月翻倍，此前为每 14 个月；最终归一化性能无趋势断点，每 14.6 个月翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究品味包括选择有趣问题、设计实验和解释结果，是预测 AI 进展的关键输入；但缺少量化实验研究品味、并将品味与编码能力分离的基准。作者希望以计算效率衡量实验品味，从而估计实验计算乘数及其对 AI 进展预测的影响。

Method: 提出 TasteVal 基准，包含 8 个新颖、具有挑战性、开放式的任务，代表前沿 AI 研发。评估时，被评估模型作为 Researcher 迭代设计实验，固定的 Coder 智能体负责实现并报告结果；Researcher 运行至 40 H100 小时或 120 挂钟小时预算耗尽。招募 24 位人类专家，每个任务至少 2 位，取每任务最佳专家尝试作为专家基线；评估 2023 至 2026 年间发布的 20 个模型。为防污染，不公开发布任务。

Result: 最佳模型 Opus 5.5 超过专家基线，计算乘数 2.3 倍（95% CI 1.15-4.37），成本约为基线者平均每次运行成本的 1/30。在 TasteVal 上，前沿模型的计算乘数自 2025 年 12 月以来约每 3.0 个月翻倍（95% CI 1.7-5.0），而 2023 年至 2025 年 12 月为每 14 个月翻倍。以最终归一化性能衡量，前沿模型没有趋势断点，每 14.6 个月翻倍。

Conclusion: TasteVal 将实验研究品味量化为实验计算效率的乘数，为 AI 进展预测提供关键输入。结果显示前沿模型的实验品味增长在 2025 年 12 月后显著加速，但最终归一化性能的增长趋势未出现断点；任务不公开以避免基准污染。

中文摘要：我们提出 TasteVal，一个用于评估前沿模型实验研究品味的基准。我们将研究品味定义为选择有趣问题加以解决、设计实验以及解释实验结果的能力。TasteVal 衡量研究品味中的实验组成部分；在给定固定研究问题的情况下，我们衡量模型迭代设计实验并根据其结果得出结论的能力如何。我们将实验研究品味操作化为计算效率；一个 Researcher 若以一半的串行实验计算量达到与人类专家相同的分数，则其具有两倍的实验品味。因此，实验品味充当实验计算的乘数，使其成为预测 AI 进展的关键输入。TasteVal 包含 8 个新颖、具有挑战性、开放式的任务，代表前沿 AI 研发。为了将品味与编码能力分离，被评估的模型充当 Researcher，迭代设计实验，而一个固定的 Coder 智能体实现这些实验并报告其结果。Researcher 执行直到 40 H100 小时或 120 挂钟小时预算耗尽。我们招募 24 位人类专家，每个任务至少 2 位，并将每个任务的最佳专家尝试作为专家基线。我们评估了 2023 年至 2026 年间发布的 20 个模型。表现最好的模型 Opus 5.5 超过了我们的专家基线，计算乘数为 2.3 倍（95% CI 1.15-4.37），成本约为我们基线者平均每次运行成本的 1/30。在 TasteVal 上，自 2025 年 12 月以来，前沿模型的计算乘数大约每 3.0 个月翻一番（95% CI 1.7-5.0），而 2023 年至 2025 年 12 月期间为每 14 个月翻一番。以最终归一化性能衡量，前沿模型没有表现出趋势断点，每 14.6 个月翻一番。为保持 TasteVal 不受污染，我们不公开发布这些任务。

Original abstract: We introduce TasteVal, a benchmark to evaluate the experimental research taste of frontier models. We define research taste as the ability to pick interesting problems to solve, design experiments, and interpret experimental results. TasteVal measures the experimental component of research taste; given a fixed research problem, we measure how well a model iteratively designs experiments and draws conclusions from their outcomes. We operationalize experimental research taste as compute efficiency; a Researcher who reaches the same score as an expert human using half the serial experimental compute has twice the experimental taste. Experimental taste thus acts as a multiplier on experimental compute, making it a key input to forecasts of AI progress. TasteVal consists of 8 novel, challenging, open-ended tasks representative of frontier AI R&D. To isolate taste from coding ability, the model under evaluation acts as a Researcher that iteratively designs experiments while a fixed Coder agent implements them and reports their results. The Researcher executes until either the 40 H100 hour or 120 wall-clock hour budgets are exhausted. We recruit 24 human experts, at least 2 per task, and take the best expert attempt per task as the expert baseline. We evaluate 20 models released between 2023 and 2026. The best-performing model, Opus 5.5, exceeds our expert baseline, with a compute multiplier of 2.3x (95% CI 1.15-4.37), at roughly 1/30 of our baseliners' average per-run cost. On TasteVal, the compute multiplier of frontier models has doubled approximately every 3.0 months since December 2025 (95% CI 1.7-5.0), up from every 14 months between 2023 and December 2025. Measured by final normalized performance, frontier models show no trend break, doubling every 14.6 months. To keep TasteVal uncontaminated, we do not release the tasks.

</details>
